## **Phase 3 — Synthetic Data Generation and Data Quality Validation**

Mandatory problem requirement

**Goal:** Generate realistic, deterministic synthetic data at both starter and full scale, then validate it before scheduling.

The problem statement explicitly requires 5,000 students, 300 exams, 100 rooms and 200 invigilators, along with labelled edge cases, reproducibility and exports.&#x20;

plans.txt



### **Subphase 3.1 — Build a configurable data generator**

Create:

```
generator/
    config.py
    master_data.py
    enrollments.py
    anomalies.py
```

Support two scales:

| Entity       | Starter | Full  |
| ------------ | ------- | ----- |
| Students     | 200     | 5,000 |
| Courses      | 30      | 300   |
| Exams        | 20      | 300   |
| Rooms        | 15      | 100   |
| Invigilators | 25      | 200   |

The course count is a configurable example: for a one-exam-per-course examination cycle, one course per exam is a reasonable model. Document the chosen assumption.

Use a fixed seed:

```
20261022
```

Example:

```
python manage.py generate_data \
    --seed 20261022 \
    --scale starter
```

Full dataset:

```
python manage.py generate_data \
    --seed 20261022 \
    --scale full
```

### **Subphase 3.2 — Generate records in dependency order**

Always create parent records before dependent records.

1. Generate Students, Courses, Rooms, Invigilators and TimeSlots.
2. Generate RoomCapacity, Availability, RoomClosure and Accommodations.
3. Generate Student-Course Enrollments.
4. Generate Exams and ExamRequirements.
5. Calculate active enrollment totals from actual enrollment records.
6. Validate the complete baseline dataset.

### **Subphase 3.3 — Implement realistic distributions**

Do not generate all records with identical properties.

Examples:

- Students enroll in approximately 4–6 courses.
- A few courses are highly popular.
- Most students select courses within their department.
- Room capacity varies from small classrooms to large examination halls.
- Rooms have different equipment combinations.
- Some rooms have temporary closures.
- Invigilators have different availability periods.
- A defined percentage of students have accommodation requirements.

For accommodations, include different extra-time percentages and room requirements.

Use a deterministic random generator:

```
import randomrng = random.Random(seed)student_count = rng.randint(4, 6)
```

Use this seeded generator consistently throughout the generation pipeline. If using Faker, initialize its random seed too.

Avoid `datetime.now()` for generated records. Use fixed examination calendar dates or dates derived from a defined seed-based start date.

### **Subphase 3.4 — Use batch database insertion**

For large synthetic datasets, use Django's `bulk_create()`.

```
Student.objects.bulk_create(    student_objects,    batch_size=1000)
```

Remember that bulk insertion bypasses model `save()` methods and save signals. Therefore, generate IDs and perform validation explicitly before insertion.

### **Subphase 3.5 — Build the data validation engine**

Create:

```
apps/validation/services/data_validator.py
```

Check:

| Validation                | Expected |
| ------------------------- | -------- |
| Duplicate primary keys    | 0        |
| Invalid foreign keys      | 0        |
| Invalid enum values       | 0        |
| Invalid date ranges       | 0        |
| Duplicate enrollments     | 0        |
| Invalid room capacities   | 0        |
| Enrollment reconciliation | Pass     |
| Invalid lifecycle states  | 0        |
| Invalid generated IDs     | 0        |

Command:

```
python manage.py validate_data
```

Produce:

```
data/validation_summary.json
```

### **Subphase 3.6 — Export and reproduce the data**

Create:

```
python manage.py export_data
```

Exports:

```
data/exports/
    students.csv
    courses.csv
    enrollments.csv
    exams.csv
    rooms.csv
    invigilators.csv
    accommodations.csv
    availability.csv
```

Also support JSON and SQL seed exports.

Build a reproducibility command:

```
python manage.py verify_reproducibility \
    --seed 20261022
```

Compare row counts and SHA-256 checksums from two isolated runs. Sort records consistently before calculating hashes.

**Expected deliverables:**

- Deterministic synthetic data generator.
- Starter and full datasets.
- Data dictionary alignment.
- Validation command and JSON summary.
- CSV/JSON/SQL exports.
- Reproducibility report.

**Completion criteria:** The full dataset meets the required volume, contains valid relationships and produces identical canonical exports when regenerated with the same seed.

## **Phase 4 — Core Scheduling Engine and Conflict Resolution**

Most important technical phase

**Goal:** Build the actual scheduling engine that allocates exams while respecting all hard constraints.

Keep scheduling algorithms in plain Python services. This makes them independently testable and reusable from both Django views and management commands.

### **Subphase 4.1 — Build the conflict graph**

Represent examinations as graph vertices.

Two examinations are connected when at least one student is enrolled in both.

Example:

```
       EXAM-001
       /     \
  EXAM-002  EXAM-003
       \     /
       EXAM-004
```

The edge weight should represent the number of shared students.

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/conflict_graph.py
```

The graph should be generated using active enrollments only.

### **Subphase 4.2 — Implement exam ordering using DSatur**

Use the DSatur graph-coloring heuristic.

The algorithm prioritizes examinations by:

1. Highest saturation degree.
2. Highest conflict degree.
3. Largest student enrollment.
4. Most restrictive room and equipment requirements.

Each color represents an examination time group.

Exams connected by an edge must receive different, non-overlapping time groups.

Implement this in plain Python first. NetworkX is optional, not mandatory.

### **Subphase 4.3 — Implement candidate time-slot selection**

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/slot_allocator.py
```

For every exam:

- Identify eligible dates and candidate slots.
- Check student conflicts.
- Confirm the slot can accommodate examination duration.
- Include required accommodation time.
- Respect operating hours and time buffers.
- Try another candidate if the current one is infeasible.

Use actual time intervals, not just slot labels, for final conflict validation.

### **Subphase 4.4 — Implement room allocation**

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/room_allocator.py
```

Check:

- Room is active.
- Capacity is sufficient.
- Required equipment is available.
- Accessibility requirements are satisfied.
- Room availability covers the entire allocation.
- No closure overlaps the allocation.
- Room is not already occupied by another exam.

Use best-fit allocation: choose the smallest suitable room or combination of rooms that can accommodate the students.

Support multiple rooms for a single examination.

Use `StudentRoomAllocation` to identify exactly which students are assigned to each room.

### **Subphase 4.5 — Implement accommodation handling**

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/accommodation.py
```

Example:

| Examination | Accommodation | Required duration |
| ----------- | ------------- | ----------------- |
| 120 minutes | None          | 120 minutes       |
| 120 minutes | 25% extra     | 150 minutes       |
| 120 minutes | 50% extra     | 180 minutes       |
| 120 minutes | 100% extra    | 240 minutes       |

Calculate:

\\[ D\_{\text{required}} = D\_{\text{base}} \times \left(1+\frac{\text{extra time percentage}}{100}\right) \\]

Requirements:

- Apply only effective accommodations.
- Allocate separate rooms when required.
- Ensure accessible-room requirements are satisfied.
- Reserve rooms and invigilators for their required durations.
- Do not cut off extended-time students when the base exam ends.

For multiple student groups in different rooms, each room's allocation should have an end time sufficient for the students assigned to it.

A conservative first implementation can reserve the full maximum required duration for all students sharing a room.

### **Subphase 4.6 — Implement invigilator allocation**

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/invigilator_allocator.py
```

Rules:

- Invigilator must be available.
- Invigilator cannot be double-booked.
- Assigned duration must fit availability.
- Daily working-hour limits must be respected.
- Consecutive-hour limits must be respected.
- Every room must have the required staffing level.

Use a documented, configurable staffing policy, such as a minimum of one invigilator per room and one additional invigilator for every 50 students.

### **Subphase 4.7 — Add traceable decision-making**

Create:

```
apps/scheduling/services/explanations.py
```

Every scheduling decision should record:

- Exam selected.
- Candidate times considered.
- Rooms considered.
- Reasons for room rejection.
- Invigilators considered.
- Selected resources.
- Final outcome.
- Algorithm version.
- Relevant constraint evidence.

For example:

```
EXAM-2026-000042

STATUS: UNASSIGNED

PRIMARY REASON:
NO_ROOM_CAPACITY

EXPLANATION:
120 active students require allocation.

Largest eligible room:
80 seats.

No feasible combination of rooms was found
under the selected equipment and availability rules.
```

### **Subphase 4.8 — Preserve scheduling history**

Create a `SchedulerRun` for every execution.

For example:

```
RUN-000001
    EXAM-001 -> ROOM-001 -> 09:00

RUN-000002
    EXAM-001 -> ROOM-003 -> 09:00
```

Never delete or overwrite the first run.

Use `ScheduleEvent` to record assignment creation, rescheduling, cancellation and unassignment.

Use `transaction.atomic()` so a partially allocated examination cannot leave inconsistent room or invigilator records behind.

### **Suggested reason codes**

| Reason code                   | Explanation                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT`       | Every candidate time conflicts with student examinations |
| `NO_ROOM_CAPACITY`            | No eligible room combination can fit                     |
| `NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM`           | Required equipment is unavailable                        |
| `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE`            | Room is closed or unavailable                            |
| `NO_INVIGILATOR`              | No suitable invigilator is available                     |
| `ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE` | Accommodation requirements cannot be met                 |
| `NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT`      | No complete assignment satisfies all constraints         |

The scheduler should track rejected candidate reasons, not just return one unexplained generic failure.

**Expected deliverables:**

- Conflict graph generator.
- DSatur-based time allocation.
- Room allocator.
- Invigilator allocator.
- Accommodation processor.
- Immutable assignment history.
- Explainable decisions and unassigned reasons.

**Completion criteria:** A small test dataset can be scheduled with zero hard-constraint violations, and every unscheduled exam has a documented reason.

## **Phase 5 — Independent Validation, Edge Cases and Evaluation Metrics**

Mandatory evaluation phase

**Goal:** Prove that the generated schedule is correct and that the system can independently detect deliberate violations.

This phase must be independent of the scheduling algorithm.

### **Subphase 5.1 — Build the independent schedule validator**

Create:

```
apps/validation/services/schedule_validator.py
```

The validator must examine the persisted database records.

It should independently check:

- Student overlapping examinations.
- Room capacity violations.
- Missing equipment.
- Invigilator overlapping assignments.
- Accommodation violations.
- Room closures and availability.
- Invalid examination durations.
- Student-room allocation reconciliation.

Do not simply call the scheduler's in-memory feasibility methods again. Use separate checks over persisted data so both systems cannot silently share the same implementation defect.

Persist the results through `ValidationRun` and `DetectedViolation`.

### **Subphase 5.2 — Generate the five mandatory anomaly scenarios**

Maintain two distinct datasets or test scenarios.

**Scenario A — Valid baseline**

Expected result: zero hard violations

Generate valid data → Run scheduler → Run independent validator.

**Scenario B — Deliberate anomalies**

Expected result: five anomaly types detected

Create deliberately invalid assignments → Run detector → Compare results with ground truth.

The five required cases:

| Scenario                       | Deliberate violation                                                            | Expected detection           |
| ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| Student overlapping exams      | Same student assigned to two overlapping exams                                  | `STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING`     |
| Room capacity exceeded         | 120 students assigned to a 50-seat room                                         | `ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED`     |
| Invigilator double-booked      | Same invigilator assigned to two overlapping exams                              | `INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED`  |
| Accommodation ignored          | Extended-time student receives insufficient examination time or unsuitable room | `ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION`    |
| Exam outside room availability | Exam assigned during room closure                                               | `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION` |

Each test fixture must contain a real database condition that violates the rule.

### **Subphase 5.3 — Create separate ground-truth files**

Create:

```
data/
    ground_truth.csv
    ground_truth.json
```

Example:

```
anomaly_id,issue_type,affected_entity_id,expected_violation_code
EDGE-001,STUDENT_OVERLAP,STU-000123,STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING
EDGE-002,CAPACITY_EXCEEDED,ROOM-000012,ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED
EDGE-003,INVIGILATOR_CLASH,INV-000045,INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED
EDGE-004,ACCOMMODATION_BREACH,STU-000305,ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION
EDGE-005,ROOM_CLOSED,ROOM-000088,ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION
```

The ground-truth file must not be part of the detector's input.

It is only used by the evaluation service after detection has completed.

### **Subphase 5.4 — Implement anomaly detection and evaluation**

Create:

```
apps/validation/services/
    anomaly_detector.py
    evaluator.py
```

Implement:

- Detection of actual database violations.
- Comparison of detected cases with expected labels.
- True positive calculation.
- False positive calculation.
- False negative calculation.
- Precision, recall and F1-score.

\\[ Precision=\frac{TP}{TP+FP} \\]

\\[ Recall=\frac{TP}{TP+FN} \\]

\\[ F1=2\times\frac{Precision\times Recall}{Precision+Recall} \\]

Calculate these metrics from actual results. Never hardcode a 100% score.

### **Subphase 5.5 — Add constraint scorecard**

Show each rule and its validation status.

| Constraint               | Checks performed | Failures      | Status    |
| ------------------------ | ---------------- | ------------- | --------- |
| Student conflicts        | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |
| Room capacity            | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |
| Equipment                | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |
| Invigilator availability | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |
| Accommodation            | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |
| Room availability        | Actual result    | Actual result | Pass/Fail |

The scorecard must reflect the selected validation run.

### **Subphase 5.6 — Calculate business metrics**

Include:

| Metric                       | Description                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Scheduling coverage          | Percentage of exams completely scheduled                               |
| Hard constraint violations   | Total violations in the normal baseline                                |
| Student allocation rate      | Percentage of eligible student-exam enrollments successfully allocated |
| Room seat utilization        | Allocated student seats / capacity of rooms used                       |
| Room time utilization        | Booked room-minutes / available room-minutes                           |
| Invigilator utilization      | Assigned invigilator-hours / available hours                           |
| Exception documentation rate | Unassigned exams with documented reasons                               |
| Detection precision          | Correct reported violations / all reported violations                  |
| Detection recall             | Detected actual violations / all actual violations                     |
| Detection F1                 | Combined precision and recall                                          |
| Scheduler execution time     | Actual time taken by the scheduling run                                |

Also enforce these reconciliation checks:

```
Total exams
    =
Completely scheduled exams
    +
Unassigned exams
```

For every completely scheduled exam:

```
Active enrolled students
    =
Allocated students
```

Any mismatch should produce an explicit validation error.

**Expected deliverables:**

- Independent schedule validator.
- Five anomaly test scenarios.
- Separate ground-truth exports.
- Detection and evaluation commands.
- Constraint scorecard.
- Business metrics and reconciliation report.

**Completion criteria:** The normal baseline has zero hard violations, and the deliberately invalid fixtures are detected and correctly evaluated.

## **Phase 6 — Frontend Development and Workflow Integration**

**Goal:** Build a professional, understandable interface that exposes the complete scheduling workflow and makes the application's decisions visible.

Use Django Templates, Bootstrap 5 and JavaScript. Do not waste development time introducing a separate frontend framework.

### **Subphase 6.1 — Create the common layout**

Create:

```
dashboard/templates/dashboard/
    base.html
    navbar.html
    sidebar.html
    footer.html
```

Include:

- Navigation.
- User profile.
- Dashboard layout.
- Responsive sidebar.
- Alerts and status messages.
- Consistent tables and forms.

### **Subphase 6.2 — Build the executive dashboard**

Route:

```
/
```

Display:

- Total students.
- Total courses.
- Total examinations.
- Total rooms.
- Total invigilators.
- Scheduled examinations.
- Unassigned examinations.
- Room utilization.
- Invigilator utilization.
- Hard-constraint violations.
- Latest scheduler run.

Include charts for scheduling outcomes and exception categories.

### **Subphase 6.3 — Build the timetable explorer**

Route:

```
/schedule/
```

Display a timetable organized by examination date and time slot.

Include filters for:

- Date.
- Course.
- Exam.
- Room.
- Invigilator.
- Department.

Show room allocations, examination duration and accommodation indicators.

### **Subphase 6.4 — Build exam details and assignment explanations**

Route:

```
/exams/<exam_id>/
```

Show:

- Exam ID and course.
- Active enrolled student count.
- Base examination duration.
- Accommodated duration.
- Assigned rooms.
- Allocated students per room.
- Invigilators.
- Equipment requirements.
- Assignment history.
- Candidate rejection explanations.

Add a **Why this assignment?** section for explaining how the scheduler selected its resources.

### **Subphase 6.5 — Build the exceptions centre**

Route:

```
/exceptions/
```

Include:

- Unassigned examinations.
- Reason codes.
- Number of enrolled students.
- Failed constraints.
- Supporting evidence.
- Suggested remediation.
- Previous scheduling attempts.

### **Subphase 6.6 — Build validation and conflict graph pages**

Routes:

```
/validation/
/conflict-graph/
```

The validation page should show:

- Constraint scorecard.
- Detected violations.
- Precision, recall and F1.
- Validation run history.
- Data-quality reports.

The conflict graph should visualize exam nodes, shared-student edges and time-slot colouring.

Treat the conflict graph as an enhancement after the essential pages are working.

### **Subphase 6.7 — Add role-based access and actions**

Implement Django authentication and Groups/Permissions.

| Role                   | Access                                            |
| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| Learner                | Own timetable and examination information         |
| Instructor             | Relevant course and examination information       |
| Academic administrator | Data generation, scheduling, exception resolution |
| Support staff          | Accommodation and accessibility records           |
| Platform administrator | Full platform management                          |

Use Django forms for filters and data imports.

Use POST requests with CSRF protection for actions such as:

- Generate dataset.
- Run scheduler.
- Validate data.
- Detect violations.
- Export results.

These actions must call application services, never execute arbitrary shell commands from a web request.

For a hackathon implementation, synchronous service execution is acceptable if the full workload completes within the web request's practical limits. Do not introduce Celery just for appearance.

**Expected deliverables:**

- Responsive dashboard.
- Examination timetable.
- Exam detail view.
- Student lookup.
- Exceptions centre.
- Validation scorecard.
- Conflict graph.
- Role-aware navigation and permissions.

**Completion criteria:** A judge can open the application, understand the scheduling results and inspect why a particular decision was made.

## **Phase 7 — Testing, Documentation, Reproducibility and Final Demonstration**

**Goal:** Verify the complete system, prepare the repository and make the project easy for judges to run and evaluate.

### **Subphase 7.1 — Complete the testing suite**

Organize tests into these categories:

| Test category         | What to verify                                   |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| Model tests           | Database relationships, constraints and IDs      |
| Generator tests       | Fixed seed, data volumes and relationships       |
| Conflict graph tests  | Correct shared-student edges                     |
| Scheduler tests       | Correct time and room allocation                 |
| Accommodation tests   | Extra time, accessibility and separate rooms     |
| Validator tests       | Detection of actual persisted violations         |
| Integration tests     | Complete generate-to-schedule-to-report workflow |
| Reproducibility tests | Identical canonical exports                      |
| UI tests              | Pages, forms, permissions and error handling     |

### **Subphase 7.2 — Add boundary and failure tests**

Do not limit testing to the five mandatory edge cases.

Include:

| Scenario                                         | Expected result                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Students equal room capacity                     | Accepted                                                       |
| Students exceed room capacity by one             | Rejected                                                       |
| Two exams meet at exactly the same boundary time | Accepted if intervals do not overlap and no buffer is required |
| Room closes during an exam                       | Rejected                                                       |
| Invigilator availability exactly covers an exam  | Accepted                                                       |
| Invigilator leaves before the exam finishes      | Rejected                                                       |
| No suitable room exists                          | Unassigned with reason                                         |
| No suitable invigilator exists                   | Unassigned with reason                                         |
| Accommodation is expired                         | Not applied                                                    |
| Accommodation is active                          | Applied                                                        |
| Unknown student ID                               | Friendly error response                                        |
| Repeated scheduler execution                     | Earlier history retained                                       |
| Failed assignment transaction                    | No partial allocations left behind                             |

FILE: README.md

# Exam & Room Scheduling System

Django + SQLite + Bootstrap 5. Status: **Phases 3–6 implemented**.

## Setup
```bash
python -m venv venv
source venv/bin/activate        # Windows: venv\Scripts\activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
python manage.py migrate
python manage.py setup_roles
python manage.py createsuperuser
python manage.py generate_data --seed 20261022 --scale starter
python manage.py validate_data
python manage.py verify_reproducibility --seed 20261022 --scale starter
python manage.py run_scheduler --seed 20261022
python manage.py validate_schedule
python manage.py inject_anomalies
python manage.py detect_violations
python manage.py evaluate_anomalies
python manage.py export_data
python manage.py runserver
# Open http://127.0.0.1:8000/ and sign in at /accounts/login/
python manage.py test tests.test_views
python manage.py test            # all tests
```
See `docs/` for architecture, business rules, ER diagram and data dictionary.

Use `--scale full` to generate the required 5,000-student dataset. To replace
existing generated data before scheduling history exists, add `--force`.
Exports are written under `data/exports/`; generation metadata, validation and
reproducibility summaries are written under `data/`. The scheduler creates a
new run and retains its assignment history; exams that cannot satisfy all hard
constraints are reported with reason codes and evidence. Schedule validation
writes `data/validation_scorecard.json`. The anomaly commands create a separate
fixture run, write run-scoped ground truth to `data/ground_truth.csv` and
`data/ground_truth.json`, and report evaluation metrics to
`data/anomaly_evaluation.json`. Room closures attached to an anomaly fixture
are scoped to that run and do not affect baseline schedules.

## Dashboard (Phase 6)

The read-only dashboard pages are available without signing in:

- `/` — overview and latest run metrics
- `/schedule/` — searchable timetable
- `/exams/<exam-id>/` — exam assignments and explanation history
- `/students/?q=<student-id-or-email>` — student enrollments and personal timetable
- `/exceptions/` — unassigned exams and reason evidence
- `/validation/` — scorecard, detected violations, and validation history
- `/conflict-graph/` — interactive exam conflict graph

Workflow actions are available to Django staff/superusers and members of the
**Academic Administrator** group. `setup_roles` creates the documented role
groups; add users to the appropriate group through Django admin. All dashboard
actions are CSRF-protected POST requests. Use `/admin/` for internal record
management.

from django.contrib import admin

from .models import (
    Accommodation, Course, Enrollment, Equipment, Exam, ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment, Invigilator, Room, RoomCapacity, RoomEquipment, Student,
)


class RoomCapacityInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = RoomCapacity
    extra = 0


class RoomEquipmentInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = RoomEquipment
    extra = 0


class ReqEquipmentInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = ExamRequirementEquipment
    extra = 0


class AccommodationInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = Accommodation
    extra = 0


@admin.register(Student)
class StudentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("student_id", "full_name", "department", "year_level", "status")
    list_filter = ("department", "year_level", "status")
    search_fields = ("student_id", "full_name", "email")
    inlines = [AccommodationInline]
    list_per_page = 50


@admin.register(Course)
class CourseAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("course_id", "course_code", "title", "department", "credits")
    list_filter = ("department",)
    search_fields = ("course_id", "course_code", "title")
    list_per_page = 50


@admin.register(Enrollment)
class EnrollmentAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("enrollment_id", "student", "course", "term", "status", "effective_from", "effective_to")
    list_filter = ("status", "term")
    search_fields = ("enrollment_id", "student__student_id", "course__course_code")
    date_hierarchy = "effective_from"
    raw_id_fields = ("student", "course")
    list_per_page = 50


@admin.register(Exam)
class ExamAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("exam_id", "course", "duration_minutes", "term", "status")

@admin.register(Equipment)

    list_display = ("code", "name")

    list_filter = ("room_type", "is_accessible", "status", "building")
    search_fields = ("room_id", "room_number", "building")

"""Master-data models: Student, Course, Enrollment, Exam, ExamRequirement,
Equipment, Room, RoomCapacity, Invigilator, Accommodation (+ bridge tables)."""
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.validators import MaxValueValidator, MinValueValidator
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F, Q

from apps.core.validators import (
    code_validator,
    course_code_validator,
    id_validator,
    term_validator,
    validate_multiple_of_15,
)


class Department(models.TextChoices):
    CS = "CS", "Computer Science"
    EE = "EE", "Electrical Engineering"
    ME = "ME", "Mechanical Engineering"
    CE = "CE", "Civil Engineering"
    BIO = "BIO", "Biology"
    CHEM = "CHEM", "Chemistry"
    MATH = "MATH", "Mathematics"
    PHY = "PHY", "Physics"
    BUS = "BUS", "Business"
    ARTS = "ARTS", "Arts"


class Student(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "ACTIVE", "Active"
        INACTIVE = "INACTIVE", "Inactive"
        GRADUATED = "GRADUATED", "Graduated"

    student_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("student")])
    full_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    email = models.EmailField(unique=True)
    department = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Department.choices)
    year_level = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(4)])
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["student_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(year_level__gte=1, year_level__lte=4), name="student_year_level_1_4"),
        ]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["department"])]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.student_id


class Course(models.Model):
    course_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("course")])
    course_code = models.CharField(
        max_length=12, unique=True,
        validators=[course_code_validator],
    )
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    department = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Department.choices)
    credits = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(6)])

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["course_id"]
        constraints = [models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(credits__gte=1, credits__lte=6), name="course_credits_1_6")]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["department"])]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.course_id} {self.course_code}"


class Enrollment(models.Model):
    """Bridge table: Student N:M Course."""

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "ACTIVE", "Active"
        DROPPED = "DROPPED", "Dropped"
        WITHDRAWN = "WITHDRAWN", "Withdrawn"
        COMPLETED = "COMPLETED", "Completed"

    enrollment_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("enrollment")])
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="enrollments")
    course = models.ForeignKey(Course, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="enrollments")
    term = models.CharField(max_length=12, validators=[term_validator])
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)
    effective_from = models.DateField()
    effective_to = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["enrollment_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["student", "course", "term"], name="uniq_enrollment_student_course_term"),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=F("effective_from")),
                name="enrollment_dates_ordered",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["course", "status"]), models.Index(fields=["student", "status"])]

    def clean(self):
        if self.effective_to and self.effective_from and self.effective_to < self.effective_from:
            raise ValidationError("effective_to cannot be before effective_from.")
        if self.status == self.Status.ACTIVE and self.student_id:
            student = Student.objects.filter(pk=self.student_id).first()
            if student and student.status != Student.Status.ACTIVE:
                raise ValidationError("Only ACTIVE students can have ACTIVE enrollments.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.enrollment_id


class Exam(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = "DRAFT", "Draft"
        PLANNED = "PLANNED", "Planned"
        SCHEDULED = "SCHEDULED", "Scheduled"
        UNASSIGNED = "UNASSIGNED", "Unassigned"

    exam_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=20, validators=[id_validator("exam")])
    course = models.ForeignKey(Course, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="exams")
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    term = models.CharField(max_length=12, validators=[term_validator])
    duration_minutes = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(
        validators=[MinValueValidator(30), MaxValueValidator(240), validate_multiple_of_15]
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.DRAFT)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["exam_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["course", "term"], name="uniq_exam_course_term"),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(duration_minutes__gte=30, duration_minutes__lte=240), name="exam_duration_30_240"
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["status"])]

    def clean(self):
        if self.exam_id and self.term and self.exam_id[5:9] != self.term[:4]:
            raise ValidationError("Year inside exam_id must match the term year.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.exam_id


class Equipment(models.Model):
    code = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=30, validators=[code_validator])
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["code"]
        verbose_name_plural = "equipment"

    def __str__(self):
        return self.code


class ExamRequirement(models.Model):
    requirement_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("requirement")])
    exam = models.OneToOneField(Exam, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="requirement")
    equipment = models.ManyToManyField(Equipment, through="ExamRequirementEquipment", blank=True)
    allow_multi_room = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    invigilator_ratio = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(
        default=50, validators=[MinValueValidator(20), MaxValueValidator(100)]
    )

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["requirement_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(invigilator_ratio__gte=20, invigilator_ratio__lte=100), name="req_ratio_20_100"
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.requirement_id


class ExamRequirementEquipment(models.Model):
    requirement = models.ForeignKey(ExamRequirement, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    equipment = models.ForeignKey(Equipment, on_delete=models.PROTECT)

    class Meta:
        constraints = [models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["requirement", "equipment"], name="uniq_req_equipment")]


class Room(models.Model):
    class RoomType(models.TextChoices):
        LECTURE_HALL = "LECTURE_HALL", "Lecture hall"
        CLASSROOM = "CLASSROOM", "Classroom"
        COMPUTER_LAB = "COMPUTER_LAB", "Computer lab"
        SCIENCE_LAB = "SCIENCE_LAB", "Science lab"

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "ACTIVE", "Active"
        INACTIVE = "INACTIVE", "Inactive"

    room_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=11, validators=[id_validator("room")])
    building = models.CharField(max_length=60)
    room_number = models.CharField(max_length=10)
    room_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=RoomType.choices)
    is_accessible = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)
    equipment = models.ManyToManyField(Equipment, through="RoomEquipment", blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["room_id"]
        constraints = [models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["building", "room_number"], name="uniq_room_building_number")]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.room_id


class RoomEquipment(models.Model):
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    equipment = models.ForeignKey(Equipment, on_delete=models.PROTECT)

    class Meta:
        constraints = [models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["room", "equipment"], name="uniq_room_equipment")]


class RoomCapacity(models.Model):
    """Exam seating capacity of a room, with effective dates (1 Room : N RoomCapacity)."""

    capacity_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("capacity")])
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="capacities")
    exam_capacity = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(10), MaxValueValidator(500)])
    effective_from = models.DateField()
    effective_to = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["room", "effective_from"]
        verbose_name_plural = "room capacities"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(exam_capacity__gte=10, exam_capacity__lte=500), name="capacity_10_500"),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=F("effective_from")),
                name="capacity_dates_ordered",
            ),
        ]

    def clean(self):
        if self.effective_to and self.effective_from and self.effective_to < self.effective_from:
            raise ValidationError("effective_to cannot be before effective_from.")
        if not self.room_id or not self.effective_from:
            return
        others = RoomCapacity.objects.filter(room_id=self.room_id).exclude(pk=self.pk)
        for other in others:
            a_end_ok = other.effective_to is None or self.effective_from <= other.effective_to
            b_end_ok = self.effective_to is None or other.effective_from <= self.effective_to
            if a_end_ok and b_end_ok:
                raise ValidationError("Capacity date range overlaps another range for this room.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.capacity_id


class Invigilator(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "ACTIVE", "Active"
        INACTIVE = "INACTIVE", "Inactive"

    invigilator_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("invigilator")])
    full_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    email = models.EmailField(unique=True)
    department = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Department.choices, blank=True)
    max_daily_hours = models.DecimalField(
        max_digits=3, decimal_places=1, default=8.0, validators=[MinValueValidator(1), MaxValueValidator(10)]
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["invigilator_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(max_daily_hours__gte=1, max_daily_hours__lte=10), name="inv_hours_1_10"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.invigilator_id


class Accommodation(models.Model):
    class Type(models.TextChoices):
        EXTRA_TIME = "EXTRA_TIME", "Extra time"
        SEPARATE_ROOM = "SEPARATE_ROOM", "Separate room"
        ACCESSIBLE_ROOM = "ACCESSIBLE_ROOM", "Accessible room"

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ACTIVE = "ACTIVE", "Active"
        REVOKED = "REVOKED", "Revoked"

    accommodation_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("accommodation")])
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="accommodations")
    accommodation_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=Type.choices)
    extra_time_percent = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(default=0)
    effective_from = models.DateField()
    effective_to = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["accommodation_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(extra_time_percent__in=[0, 25, 50, 100]), name="acc_percent_allowed"),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(Q(accommodation_type="EXTRA_TIME", extra_time_percent__gt=0)
                           | (~Q(accommodation_type="EXTRA_TIME") & Q(extra_time_percent=0))),
                name="acc_percent_matches_type",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=F("effective_from")),
                name="acc_dates_ordered",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["student", "status"])]

    def clean(self):
        if self.accommodation_type == self.Type.EXTRA_TIME and self.extra_time_percent not in (25, 50, 100):
            raise ValidationError("EXTRA_TIME needs extra_time_percent of 25, 50 or 100.")
        if self.accommodation_type != self.Type.EXTRA_TIME and self.extra_time_percent != 0:
            raise ValidationError("extra_time_percent must be 0 unless the type is EXTRA_TIME.")
        if self.effective_to and self.effective_from and self.effective_to < self.effective_from:
            raise ValidationError("effective_to cannot be before effective_from.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.accommodation_id

from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.validators import RegexValidator

from apps.core.services.id_generator import PATTERNS


def id_validator(kind: str) -> RegexValidator:
    return RegexValidator(PATTERNS[kind], message=f"Invalid {kind} ID format.")


term_validator = RegexValidator(r"^\d{4}-(FALL|SPRING)$", message="Term must look like 2026-FALL.")
code_validator = RegexValidator(r"^[A-Z_]+$", message="Use upper-case letters and underscores.")


def validate_multiple_of_15(value):
    if value % 15 != 0:
        raise ValidationError("Duration must be a multiple of 15 minutes.")

course_code_validator = RegexValidator(r"^[A-Z]{2,4}\d{3}$", message="Course code must look like CS101.")

======================================================================
FILE: apps\core\management\commands\export_data.py

import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand

from apps.validation.services.exporter import (
    export_csv,
    export_json,
    export_sql,
    write_export_manifest,
)
from generator.config import SCALES


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Export generated data to CSV, JSON, and SQL."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--out", default="data/exports")
        parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int)
        parser.add_argument("--scale", choices=sorted(SCALES))

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        base = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / options["out"]
        generation_manifest_path = (
            Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "generation_manifest.json"
        )
        generation_manifest = None
        if generation_manifest_path.exists():
            generation_manifest = json.loads(
                generation_manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )

        seed = options["seed"]
        scale = options["scale"]
        if generation_manifest:
            seed = seed if seed is not None else generation_manifest["seed"]
            scale = scale if scale is not None else generation_manifest["scale"]
        else:
            scale = scale if scale is not None else "unknown"

        export_csv(base / "csv")
        export_json(base / "json")
        export_sql(base / "sql")
        manifest = write_export_manifest(
            base,
            seed=seed,
            scale=scale,
        )
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Exported {len(manifest['files'])} data files to {base}"
            )
        )

======================================================================
FILE: apps\core\management\commands\generate_data.py
======================================================================
import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models.deletion import ProtectedError

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Equipment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.core.services.data_generator import (
    clear_generated_data,
    generate_and_persist,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import Availability, RoomClosure, TimeSlot
from generator.config import SCALES, SEED_DEFAULT


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Generate deterministic synthetic master data."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=SEED_DEFAULT)
        parser.add_argument(
            "--scale", choices=sorted(SCALES), default="starter"
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--force",
            action="store_true",
            help="Clear generated data before generating.",
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        model_classes = (
            Student, Course, Exam, Room, Invigilator, Enrollment,
            Accommodation, Availability, RoomClosure, TimeSlot, Equipment,
            ExamRequirement, ExamRequirementEquipment, RoomCapacity,
            RoomEquipment,
        )
        has_data = any(model.objects.exists() for model in model_classes)
        if has_data and not options["force"]:
            raise CommandError(
                "Generated data already exists. Use --force to replace it."
            )

        try:
            with transaction.atomic():
                if options["force"]:
                    clear_generated_data()
                stats = generate_and_persist(
                    seed=options["seed"],
                    scale=options["scale"],
                )
        except (RuntimeError, ProtectedError) as error:
            raise CommandError(str(error)) from error

        manifest_path = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "generation_manifest.json"
        manifest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        manifest_path.write_text(
            json.dumps(stats, indent=2) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Generated seed={stats['seed']} scale={stats['scale']}: "
                f"{stats['students']} students, {stats['courses']} courses, "
                f"{stats['exams']} exams, {stats['rooms']} rooms, "
                f"{stats['invigilators']} invigilators, "
                f"{stats['enrollments']} enrollments, "
                f"{stats['accommodations']} accommodations, "
                f"{stats['timeslots']} timeslots."
            )
        )

======================================================================

"""Persist deterministic synthetic master data in a single transaction."""

import datetime as dt
import random

from django.db import transaction

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Equipment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    RoomClosure,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    TimeSlot,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun
from generator.config import SCALES, SEED_DEFAULT
from generator.enrollments import generate_enrollments
from generator.master_data import (
    generate_accommodations,
    generate_courses,
    generate_equipment,
    generate_exam_requirements,
    generate_exams,
    generate_invigilator_availability,
    generate_invigilators,
    generate_room_availability,
    generate_room_closures,
    generate_room_equipment_pairs,
    generate_rooms_and_capacities,
    generate_students,
    generate_timeslots,
)


def _as_date(value):
    return dt.date.fromisoformat(value) if isinstance(value, str) else value


@transaction.atomic
def clear_generated_data():
    """Clear generated rows without ever deleting append-only schedule history."""
    if ScheduleEvent.objects.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "ScheduleEvent rows are append-only and exist. "
            "Cannot clear master data; use a fresh database."
        )

    DetectedViolation.objects.all().delete()
    ValidationRun.objects.all().delete()
    UnassignedExam.objects.all().delete()
    InvigilatorAssignment.objects.all().delete()
    StudentRoomAllocation.objects.all().delete()
    ScheduleAssignment.objects.all().delete()
    SchedulerRun.objects.all().delete()
    Availability.objects.all().delete()
    RoomClosure.objects.all().delete()
    TimeSlot.objects.all().delete()
    Accommodation.objects.all().delete()
    Enrollment.objects.all().delete()
    ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.all().delete()
    ExamRequirement.objects.all().delete()
    Exam.objects.all().delete()
    RoomCapacity.objects.all().delete()
    RoomEquipment.objects.all().delete()
    Room.objects.all().delete()
    Invigilator.objects.all().delete()
    Course.objects.all().delete()
    Student.objects.all().delete()
    Equipment.objects.all().delete()


@transaction.atomic
def generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter"):
    if scale not in SCALES:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown scale {scale!r}; choose from {', '.join(SCALES)}.")

    rng = random.Random(seed)
    config = SCALES[scale]

    equipment = generate_equipment()
    timeslots = generate_timeslots()
    students = generate_students(rng, config["students"])
    courses = generate_courses(rng, config["courses"])
    exams = generate_exams(rng, courses, config["exams"])
    rooms, capacities = generate_rooms_and_capacities(rng, config["rooms"])
    invigilators = generate_invigilators(rng, config["invigilators"])
    room_equipment = generate_room_equipment_pairs(rooms)
    room_availability = generate_room_availability(rooms)
    room_closures = generate_room_closures(rng, rooms)
    invigilator_availability = generate_invigilator_availability(
        rng,
        invigilators,
        start_index=len(room_availability) + 1,
    )
    accommodations = generate_accommodations(rng, students)

    exam_course_ids = {exam["course_id"] for exam in exams}
    courses_by_id = {course["course_id"]: course for course in courses}
    requirements, requirement_equipment = generate_exam_requirements(
        rng, exams, courses_by_id
    )
    enrollments = generate_enrollments(rng, students, courses, exam_course_ids)

    Equipment.objects.bulk_create([Equipment(**row) for row in equipment])
    Student.objects.bulk_create(
        [Student(**row) for row in students], batch_size=1000
    )
    Course.objects.bulk_create([Course(**row) for row in courses])
    Invigilator.objects.bulk_create(
        [Invigilator(**row) for row in invigilators], batch_size=1000
    )
    Room.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            Room(
                room_id=row["room_id"],
                building=row["building"],
                room_number=row["room_number"],
                room_type=row["room_type"],
                is_accessible=row["is_accessible"],
                status=row["status"],
            )
            for row in rooms
        ]
    )
    RoomCapacity.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            RoomCapacity(
                capacity_id=row["capacity_id"],
                room_id=row["room_id"],
                exam_capacity=row["exam_capacity"],
                effective_from=_as_date(row["effective_from"]),
                effective_to=_as_date(row["effective_to"]),
            )
            for row in capacities
        ]
    )
    RoomEquipment.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            RoomEquipment(room_id=room_id, equipment_id=code)
            for room_id, code in room_equipment
        ]
    )
    TimeSlot.objects.bulk_create([TimeSlot(**row) for row in timeslots])
    Availability.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            Availability(
                availability_id=row["availability_id"],
                room_id=row["room_id"],
                invigilator_id=row["invigilator_id"],
                start_at=row["start_at"],
                end_at=row["end_at"],
            )
            for row in room_availability + invigilator_availability
        ],
        batch_size=1000,
    )
    RoomClosure.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            RoomClosure(
                closure_id=row["closure_id"],
                room_id=row["room_id"],
                start_at=row["start_at"],
                end_at=row["end_at"],
                reason=row["reason"],
            )
            for row in room_closures
        ]
    )
    Accommodation.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            Accommodation(
                accommodation_id=row["accommodation_id"],
                student_id=row["student_id"],
                accommodation_type=row["accommodation_type"],
                extra_time_percent=row["extra_time_percent"],
                effective_from=_as_date(row["effective_from"]),
                effective_to=_as_date(row["effective_to"]),
                status=row["status"],
            )
            for row in accommodations
        ]
    )
    Enrollment.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            Enrollment(
                enrollment_id=row["enrollment_id"],
                student_id=row["student_id"],
                course_id=row["course_id"],
                term=row["term"],
                status=row["status"],
                effective_from=_as_date(row["effective_from"]),
                effective_to=_as_date(row["effective_to"]),
            )
            for row in enrollments
        ],
        batch_size=1000,
    )
    Exam.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            Exam(
                exam_id=row["exam_id"],
                course_id=row["course_id"],
                title=row["title"],
                term=row["term"],
                duration_minutes=row["duration_minutes"],
                status=row["status"],
            )
            for row in exams
        ]
    )
    ExamRequirement.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            ExamRequirement(
                requirement_id=row["requirement_id"],
                exam_id=row["exam_id"],
                allow_multi_room=row["allow_multi_room"],
                invigilator_ratio=row["invigilator_ratio"],
            )
            for row in requirements
        ]
    )
    ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.bulk_create(
        [
            ExamRequirementEquipment(requirement_id=requirement_id, equipment_id=code)
            for requirement_id, code in requirement_equipment
        ]
    )

    return {
        "seed": seed,
        "scale": scale,
        "students": len(students),
        "courses": len(courses),
        "exams": len(exams),
        "rooms": len(rooms),
        "invigilators": len(invigilators),
        "enrollments": len(enrollments),
        "accommodations": len(accommodations),
        "timeslots": len(timeslots),
    }

======================================================================
FILE: apps\core\services\id_generator.py
======================================================================
"""Deterministic, zero-padded ID helpers.

IDs are built from counters (never random) and are never created in Model.save().
"""
import re

PATTERNS = {
    "student": r"^STU-\d{6}$",
    "course": r"^CRS-\d{6}$",
    "enrollment": r"^ENR-\d{6}$",
    "exam": r"^EXAM-\d{4}-\d{6}$",
    "requirement": r"^REQ-\d{6}$",
    "room": r"^ROOM-\d{6}$",
    "capacity": r"^CAP-\d{6}$",
    "closure": r"^CLS-\d{6}$",
    "invigilator": r"^INV-\d{6}$",
    "availability": r"^AVL-\d{6}$",
    "accommodation": r"^ACC-\d{6}$",
    "slot": r"^SLOT-\d{6}$",
    "run": r"^RUN-\d{6}$",
    "assignment": r"^ASN-EXAM-\d{4}-\d{6}-ROOM-\d{6}-RUN-\d{6}$",
    "invigilator_assignment": r"^IAS-\d{6}$",
    "student_allocation": r"^SRA-\d{6}$",
    "unassigned": r"^UNA-\d{6}$",
    "event": r"^EVT-\d{6}$",
    "validation_run": r"^VAL-\d{6}$",
    "violation": r"^VIO-\d{6}$",
}

PREFIXES = {
    "student": "STU",
    "course": "CRS",
    "enrollment": "ENR",
    "requirement": "REQ",
    "room": "ROOM",
    "capacity": "CAP",
    "closure": "CLS",
    "invigilator": "INV",
    "availability": "AVL",
    "accommodation": "ACC",
    "slot": "SLOT",
    "run": "RUN",
    "invigilator_assignment": "IAS",
    "student_allocation": "SRA",
    "unassigned": "UNA",
    "event": "EVT",
    "validation_run": "VAL",
    "violation": "VIO",
}


def make_id(kind: str, number: int) -> str:
    """make_id('student', 1) -> 'STU-000001'."""
    if number < 1 or number > 999999:
        raise ValueError("number must be between 1 and 999999")
    return f"{PREFIXES[kind]}-{number:06d}"


def make_exam_id(number: int, year: int = 2026) -> str:
    """make_exam_id(1) -> 'EXAM-2026-000001'."""
    if number < 1 or number > 999999:
        raise ValueError("number must be between 1 and 999999")
    return f"EXAM-{year}-{number:06d}"


def make_assignment_id(exam_id: str, room_id: str, run_id: str) -> str:
    """ASN-<exam-id>-<room-id>-<run-id>."""
    return f"ASN-{exam_id}-{room_id}-{run_id}"


def is_valid(kind: str, value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(re.match(PATTERNS[kind], value or ""))

======================================================================
FILE: apps\core\services\lifecycle.py
======================================================================
"""Lifecycle (state machine) rules. Invalid transitions raise InvalidTransition."""


class InvalidTransition(ValueError):
    pass


ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS = {
    "Exam": {
        "DRAFT": {"PLANNED"},
        "PLANNED": {"SCHEDULED", "UNASSIGNED"},
        "SCHEDULED": {"PLANNED"},      # reset when the scheduler is re-run
        "UNASSIGNED": {"PLANNED"},     # reset when the scheduler is re-run
    },
    "Enrollment": {
        "ACTIVE": {"DROPPED", "WITHDRAWN", "COMPLETED"},
        "DROPPED": set(),
        "WITHDRAWN": set(),
        "COMPLETED": set(),
    },
    "Student": {
        "ACTIVE": {"INACTIVE", "GRADUATED"},
        "INACTIVE": {"ACTIVE"},
        "GRADUATED": set(),
    },
    "Accommodation": {"ACTIVE": {"REVOKED"}, "REVOKED": set()},
    "ScheduleAssignment": {
        "ASSIGNED": {"SUPERSEDED", "CANCELLED"},
        "SUPERSEDED": set(),
        "CANCELLED": set(),
    },
    "SchedulerRun": {"RUNNING": {"COMPLETED", "FAILED"}, "COMPLETED": set(), "FAILED": set()},
    "ValidationRun": {
        "RUNNING": {"PASSED", "FAILED_CHECKS", "ERROR"},
        "PASSED": set(),
        "FAILED_CHECKS": set(),
        "ERROR": set(),
    },
}


def can_transition(model_name: str, old: str, new: str) -> bool:
    return new in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(model_name, {}).get(old, set())


def transition(obj, new_status: str, field: str = "status"):
    """Move obj to new_status if the lifecycle allows it, then save only that field."""
    model_name = obj.__class__.__name__
    old = getattr(obj, field)
    if not can_transition(model_name, old, new_status):
        raise InvalidTransition(f"{model_name}: {old} -> {new_status} is not allowed")
    setattr(obj, field, new_status)
    obj.save(update_fields=[field])
    return obj

from django import forms

from apps.core.models import Department, Invigilator, Room


class TimetableFilterForm(forms.Form):
    date = forms.DateField(
        required=False,
        widget=forms.DateInput(attrs={"type": "date", "class": "form-control"}),
    )
    exam = forms.CharField(
        required=False,
        widget=forms.TextInput(
            attrs={"class": "form-control", "placeholder": "EXAM-2026-000042"}
        ),
    )
    course = forms.CharField(
        required=False,
        widget=forms.TextInput(
            attrs={"class": "form-control", "placeholder": "CS101 or CRS-000042"}
        ),
    )
    room = forms.ModelChoiceField(
        queryset=Room.objects.filter(status=Room.Status.ACTIVE).order_by("room_id"),
        required=False,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    invigilator = forms.ModelChoiceField(
        queryset=Invigilator.objects.filter(
            status=Invigilator.Status.ACTIVE
        ).order_by("invigilator_id"),
        required=False,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )
    department = forms.ChoiceField(
        choices=[("", "All departments"), *Department.choices],
        required=False,
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={"class": "form-select"}),
    )

======================================================================
FILE: apps\dashboard\permissions.py
======================================================================
"""Role-based helpers backed by Django Groups."""

from functools import wraps

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import redirect
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST


def user_roles(user) -> set[str]:
    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return set()
    if user.is_superuser or user.is_staff:
        return {settings.ROLE_PLATFORM_ADMIN}
    return set(user.groups.values_list("name", flat=True))


def can_run_actions(user) -> bool:
    if not user.is_authenticated:
        return False
    if user.is_superuser or user.is_staff:
        return True
    return user.groups.filter(name=settings.ROLE_ADMINISTRATOR).exists()


def staff_required(view_func):
    """Require authentication, POST, and an action-capable role."""

    @wraps(view_func)
    @login_required
    @require_POST
    def wrapper(request, *args, **kwargs):
        if not can_run_actions(request.user):
            messages.error(
                request,
                "This action requires the Academic Administrator role or staff status.",
            )
            return redirect("dashboard:home")
        return view_func(request, *args, **kwargs)

    return wrapper

======================================================================
FILE: apps\dashboard\services.py
======================================================================
"""Read-only aggregation helpers used by dashboard views."""

import json
import logging
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models import Count, Q

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    Student,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.conflict_graph import build_conflict_graph
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def latest_run() -> SchedulerRun | None:
    return SchedulerRun.objects.order_by("-run_id").first()


def latest_completed_run() -> SchedulerRun | None:
    return (
        SchedulerRun.objects.filter(status=SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED)
        .order_by("-run_id")
        .first()
    )


def latest_validation(run: SchedulerRun | None = None) -> ValidationRun | None:
    queryset = ValidationRun.objects.order_by("-validation_id")
    if run is not None:
        queryset = queryset.filter(scheduler_run=run)
    return queryset.first()


def load_scorecard() -> dict | None:
    path = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "validation_scorecard.json"
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        logger.warning("Could not read dashboard scorecard at %s.", path, exc_info=True)
        return None


def _reason_breakdown(run: SchedulerRun) -> dict[str, int]:
    rows = (
        UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run)
        .values("primary_reason_code")
        .annotate(count=Count("unassigned_id"))
    )
    return {row["primary_reason_code"]: row["count"] for row in rows}


def _violation_breakdown(validation_run: ValidationRun | None) -> dict[str, int]:
    if validation_run is None:
        return {}
    rows = (
        DetectedViolation.objects.filter(validation_run=validation_run)
        .values("violation_code")
        .annotate(count=Count("violation_id"))
    )
    return {row["violation_code"]: row["count"] for row in rows}


def dashboard_summary() -> dict:
    counts = {
        "students": Student.objects.count(),
        "courses": Course.objects.count(),
        "exams": Exam.objects.count(),
        "rooms": Room.objects.count(),
        "invigilators": Invigilator.objects.count(),
        "enrollments": Enrollment.objects.count(),
        "accommodations": Accommodation.objects.count(),
    }
    run = latest_run()
    validation = latest_validation(run) if run else None
    scorecard = load_scorecard()
    if scorecard and (
        validation is None
        or scorecard.get("validation_id") != validation.validation_id
    ):
        scorecard = None

    scheduled_exams = (
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            run=run, status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED
        )
        .values("exam_id")
        .distinct()
        .count()
        if run
        else 0
    )
    unassigned_exams = (
        UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run).values("exam_id").distinct().count()
        if run
        else 0
    )
    metrics = scorecard.get("business_metrics", {}) if scorecard else {}

    return {
        "counts": counts,
        "latest_run": run,
        "latest_validation": validation,
        "scheduled_exams": scheduled_exams,
        "unassigned_exams": unassigned_exams,
        "unassigned_reasons": _reason_breakdown(run) if run else {},
        "violations_breakdown": _violation_breakdown(validation),
        "scorecard": scorecard,
        "metrics": metrics,
        "hard_violations": validation.failures if validation else None,
    }


def timetable_rows(run: SchedulerRun, filters: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    filters = filters or {}
    queryset = (
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            run=run, status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED
        )
        .select_related("exam__course", "room")
        .prefetch_related(
            "invigilator_assignments__invigilator", "student_allocations"
        )
        .order_by("start_at", "room__room_id")
    )

    if filters.get("date"):
        queryset = queryset.filter(start_at__date=filters["date"])
    if filters.get("exam"):
        queryset = queryset.filter(exam_id=filters["exam"].strip())
    if filters.get("course"):
        code = filters["course"].strip()
        queryset = queryset.filter(
            Q(exam__course_id=code) | Q(exam__course__course_code__iexact=code)
        )
    if filters.get("room"):
        queryset = queryset.filter(room=filters["room"])
    if filters.get("invigilator"):
        queryset = queryset.filter(
            invigilator_assignments__invigilator=filters["invigilator"]
        ).distinct()
    if filters.get("department"):
        queryset = queryset.filter(exam__course__department=filters["department"])

    grouped = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment in queryset:
        grouped[assignment.start_at.date()].append(assignment)
    return dict(sorted(grouped.items()))


def exam_detail(exam: Exam) -> dict:
    run = latest_completed_run()
    context = {
        "exam": exam,
        "run": run,
        "assignments": [],
        "unassigned": None,
        "events": [],
        "required_equipment": list(
            ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
                requirement__exam__exam_id=exam.exam_id
            )
            .values_list("equipment_id", flat=True)
            .order_by("equipment_id")
        ),
        "active_enrollments": Enrollment.objects.filter(
            course=exam.course,
            term=exam.term,
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE,
        ).count(),
        "accommodations": [],
        "assigned_students": [],
    }
    if run is None:
        return context

    assignments = list(
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=run, exam=exam)
        .select_related("room")
        .prefetch_related(
            "invigilator_assignments__invigilator",
            "student_allocations__student",
        )
        .order_by("assignment_id")
    )
    context["assignments"] = assignments
    context["unassigned"] = UnassignedExam.objects.filter(
        run=run, exam=exam
    ).first()
    context["events"] = list(
        ScheduleEvent.objects.filter(run=run, exam=exam).order_by("event_id")
    )

    if assignments:
        exam_date = assignments[0].start_at.date()
        active_accommodations = Accommodation.objects.filter(
            status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE,
            effective_from__lte=exam_date,
        ).filter(Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=exam_date))
        allocated_ids = set(
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(assignment__in=assignments)
            .values_list("student_id", flat=True)
        )
        context["accommodations"] = list(
            active_accommodations.filter(student_id__in=allocated_ids).select_related(
                "student"
            )
        )
        context["assigned_students"] = list(
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(assignment__in=assignments)
            .select_related("student", "assignment")
            .order_by("assignment__room_id", "student_id")
        )
    return context


def exceptions_rows(run: SchedulerRun) -> list[UnassignedExam]:
    return list(
        UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run)
        .select_related("exam__course")
        .order_by("exam_id")
    )


def validation_page_data() -> dict:
    validation = latest_validation()
    violations = []
    if validation:
        violations = list(
            DetectedViolation.objects.filter(validation_run=validation)
            .select_related("assignment__exam__course", "assignment__room")
            .order_by("violation_id")
        )
    scorecard = load_scorecard()
    if scorecard and (
        validation is None
        or scorecard.get("validation_id") != validation.validation_id
    ):
        scorecard = None
    return {
        "latest_validation": validation,
        "scorecard": scorecard,
        "violations": violations,
        "history": list(ValidationRun.objects.order_by("-validation_id")[:15]),
    }


def conflict_graph_data(top_n: int = 30) -> dict:
    term = settings.SCHEDULER_POLICY["TERM"]
    exams = list(Exam.objects.filter(term=term).select_related("course"))
    if not exams:
        return {"nodes": [], "edges": [], "term": term}

    graph, students_by_exam = build_conflict_graph(exams, term=term)
    top = sorted(graph.degree.items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]))[:top_n]
    selected = {exam_id for exam_id, _degree in top}
    exam_by_id = {exam.exam_id: exam for exam in exams}
    slot_by_exam = {}
    run = latest_run()
    if run:
        for assignment in ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            run=run, exam_id__in=selected
        ).order_by("start_at", "assignment_id"):
            slot_by_exam.setdefault(assignment.exam_id, assignment.start_at.isoformat())

    nodes = []
    for exam_id, degree in top:
        exam = exam_by_id[exam_id]
        nodes.append(
            {
                "id": exam_id,
                "label": exam_id,
                "course": exam.course.course_code,
                "degree": degree,
                "enrollment": len(students_by_exam.get(exam_id, ())),
                "slot": slot_by_exam.get(exam_id, ""),
            }
        )

    edges = []
    for first in sorted(selected):
        for second, weight in graph.neighbors(first).items():
            if second in selected and first < second:
                edges.append({"from": first, "to": second, "value": weight})
    return {"nodes": nodes, "edges": edges, "term": term}

======================================================================
FILE: apps\dashboard\views.py
