"""Dashboard presentation and staff-only workflow actions."""

import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib import messages
from django.db import DatabaseError
from django.shortcuts import redirect, render
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils.http import url_has_allowed_host_and_scheme

from apps.core.models import Exam, Enrollment, Student
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    ScheduleAssignment,
    SchedulerRun,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from apps.validation.models import ValidationRun
from apps.validation.services.anomaly_injector import inject
from apps.validation.services.data_validator import validate_master_data
from apps.validation.services.evaluator import evaluate, write_evaluation_report
from apps.validation.services.exporter import (
    export_csv,
    export_json,
    export_sql,
    write_export_manifest,
)
from apps.validation.services.validation_runner import run_all_checks

from .forms import TimetableFilterForm
from .permissions import staff_required
from .services import (
    conflict_graph_data,
    dashboard_summary,
    exam_detail,
    exceptions_rows,
    latest_run,
    timetable_rows,
    validation_page_data,
)


def home(request):
    return render(request, "dashboard/dashboard.html", dashboard_summary())


def timetable(request):
    run = latest_run()
    if run is None:
        return render(
            request, "dashboard/no_latest_run.html", {"what": "timetable"}
        )
    form = TimetableFilterForm(request.GET or None)
    filters = {}
    if form.is_valid():
        filters = {
            key: value for key, value in form.cleaned_data.items() if value
        }
    return render(
        request,
        "dashboard/timetable.html",
        {
            "run": run,
            "form": form,
            "grouped": timetable_rows(run, filters),
            "filters": filters,
            "has_filter": bool(filters),
        },
    )


def exam_detail_view(request, exam_id):
    exam = (
        Exam.objects.select_related("course")
        .filter(pk=exam_id)
        .first()
    )
    if exam is None:
        return render(
            request,
            "dashboard/not_found.html",
            {"what": f"exam {exam_id}"},
        )
    return render(
        request, "dashboard/exam_detail.html", exam_detail(exam)
    )


def student_lookup(request):
    query = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    student = None
    enrollments = []
    assignments = []
    if query:
        student = Student.objects.filter(pk=query).first()
        if student is None:
            student = Student.objects.filter(email__iexact=query).first()
        if student:
            enrollments = list(
                student.enrollments.select_related("course")
                .filter(status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE)
                .order_by("course__course_code")
            )
            run = latest_run()
            if run:
                assignments = list(
                    ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
                        run=run,
                        status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
                        student_allocations__student=student,
                    )
                    .select_related("exam__course", "room")
                    .prefetch_related("invigilator_assignments__invigilator")
                    .order_by("start_at", "room__room_id")
                    .distinct()
                )
    return render(
        request,
        "dashboard/student_lookup.html",
        {
            "query": query,
            "student": student,
            "enrollments": enrollments,
            "assignments": assignments,
            "run": latest_run() if student else None,
        },
    )


def exceptions(request):
    run = latest_run()
    if run is None:
        return render(
            request, "dashboard/no_latest_run.html", {"what": "exceptions"}
        )
    return render(
        request,
        "dashboard/exceptions.html",
        {"run": run, "rows": exceptions_rows(run)},
    )


def validation_page(request):
    return render(
        request, "dashboard/validation.html", validation_page_data()
    )


def conflict_graph(request):
    return render(
        request, "dashboard/conflict_graph.html", conflict_graph_data()
    )


def _back(request):
    fallback = reverse("dashboard:home")
    candidate = request.POST.get("next") or request.META.get("HTTP_REFERER")
    if candidate and url_has_allowed_host_and_scheme(
        candidate,
        allowed_hosts={request.get_host()},
        require_https=request.is_secure(),
    ):
        return candidate
    return fallback


@staff_required
def action_run_scheduler(request):
    seed_value = (request.POST.get("seed") or "").strip()
    try:
        seed = int(seed_value) if seed_value else None
    except ValueError:
        messages.error(request, "Seed must be an integer.")
        return redirect(_back(request))

    run = run_scheduler(seed=seed)
    if run.status == SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED:
        messages.success(
            request, f"Ran scheduler: {run.run_id} — {run.notes}"
        )
    else:
        messages.error(
            request, f"Scheduler run {run.run_id} ended with status {run.status}."
        )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_validate_data(request):
    try:
        summary = validate_master_data()
    except OSError as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Could not write validation report: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    if summary["ok"]:
        messages.success(
            request,
            f"Data validation passed — {len(summary['checks'])} checks, 0 failures.",
        )
    else:
        messages.warning(
            request,
            f"Data validation found {summary['failures']} failures; "
            "see data/validation_summary.json.",
        )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_validate_schedule(request):
    run = latest_run()
    if run is None:
        messages.error(request, "No scheduler run to validate.")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    try:
        validation_run, result = run_all_checks(run)
    except (ValueError, OSError, DatabaseError) as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Schedule validation failed: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    if result["failures"] == 0:
        messages.success(
            request, f"{validation_run.validation_id}: PASS — 0 failures."
        )
    else:
        messages.warning(
            request,
            f"{validation_run.validation_id}: "
            f"{result['failures']} violations recorded.",
        )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_inject_anomalies(request):
    baseline = (
        SchedulerRun.objects.filter(
            run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.BASELINE,
            status=SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED,
        )
        .order_by("-run_id")
        .first()
    )
    if baseline is None:
        messages.error(request, "No completed BASELINE run to copy.")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    try:
        anomaly_run, labels, _selected = inject(baseline.run_id)
    except (ValueError, OSError, DatabaseError) as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Anomaly injection failed: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    messages.success(
        request,
        f"Created {anomaly_run.run_id} with {len(labels)} labelled anomalies.",
    )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_detect_violations(request):
    run = (
        SchedulerRun.objects.filter(
            run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE
        )
        .order_by("-run_id")
        .first()
    )
    if run is None:
        messages.error(
            request, "No ANOMALY_FIXTURE run exists; inject anomalies first."
        )
        return redirect(_back(request))
    try:
        validation_run, result = run_all_checks(run)
    except (ValueError, OSError, DatabaseError) as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Violation detection failed: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    messages.success(
        request,
        f"{validation_run.validation_id}: {result['failures']} violations "
        f"on {run.run_id}.",
    )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_evaluate_anomalies(request):
    validation_run = (
        ValidationRun.objects.filter(
            scheduler_run__run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE
        )
        .select_related("scheduler_run")
        .order_by("-validation_id")
        .first()
    )
    if validation_run is None:
        messages.error(request, "No anomaly validation yet; detect violations first.")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    try:
        result = evaluate(validation_run)
        write_evaluation_report(result)
    except (FileNotFoundError, OSError, ValueError) as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Evaluation failed: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    messages.success(
        request,
        f"Evaluation: TP={result['tp']} FP={result['fp']} FN={result['fn']} "
        f"P={result['precision']} R={result['recall']} F1={result['f1']}",
    )
    return redirect(_back(request))


@staff_required
def action_export_data(request):
    manifest_path = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "generation_manifest.json"
    seed = None
    scale = "unknown"
    if manifest_path.exists():
        try:
            generation_manifest = json.loads(
                manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )
        except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
            messages.error(
                request, f"Could not read generation manifest: {error}"
            )
            return redirect(_back(request))
        seed = generation_manifest.get("seed")
        scale = generation_manifest.get("scale", "unknown")

    export_dir = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "exports"
    try:
        export_csv(export_dir / "csv")
        export_json(export_dir / "json")
        export_sql(export_dir / "sql")
        manifest = write_export_manifest(export_dir, seed=seed, scale=scale)
    except OSError as error:
        messages.error(request, f"Export failed: {error}")
        return redirect(_back(request))
    messages.success(
        request,
        f"Exported {len(manifest['files'])} data files to {export_dir}.",
    )
    return redirect(_back(request))

"""Idempotently create the documented dashboard role groups."""

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth.models import Group
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Create the five documented dashboard role groups."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        for name in (
            settings.ROLE_LEARNER,
            settings.ROLE_INSTRUCTOR,
            settings.ROLE_ADMINISTRATOR,
            settings.ROLE_SUPPORT,
            settings.ROLE_PLATFORM_ADMIN,
        ):
            group, created = Group.objects.get_or_create(name=name)
            status = "CREATED" if created else "exists"
            self.stdout.write(f"{status:7} {group.name}")

{% extends "dashboard/base.html" %}
{% block title %}Conflict graph · Exam Scheduler{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Conflict graph{% endblock %}
{% block extra_head %}<link href="https://unpkg.com/vis-network/styles/vis-network.min.css" rel="stylesheet">{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<div class="card"><div class="card-body"><p class="text-muted">Term {{ term }} · Edges connect exams with shared students; edge weight is the number of shared students. Displaying up to 30 exams with the highest conflict degree.</p>
  {% if nodes %}<div id="conflict-network" class="graph-canvas" role="img" aria-label="Interactive exam conflict network"></div>
  {% else %}<div class="alert alert-info mb-0">No exams are available to build the conflict graph.</div>{% endif %}
</div></div>
{{ nodes|json_script:"conflict-nodes" }}
{{ edges|json_script:"conflict-edges" }}
{% endblock %}
{% block extra_js %}
{% if nodes %}
<script src="https://unpkg.com/vis-network/standalone/umd/vis-network.min.js"></script>
<script>
  (() => {
    const nodes = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("conflict-nodes").textContent);
    const edges = JSON.parse(document.getElementById("conflict-edges").textContent);
    const graphNodes = nodes.map((node) => ({
      id: node.id,
      label: `${node.label}\n${node.course} · degree ${node.degree}`,
      title: `${node.course} · ${node.enrollment} enrolled${node.slot ? ` · ${node.slot}` : ""}`,
      value: Math.max(1, node.degree)
    }));
    const graphEdges = edges.map((edge) => ({
      from: edge.from,
      to: edge.to,
      value: edge.value,
      title: `${edge.value} shared student(s)`
    }));
    const network = new vis.Network(
      document.getElementById("conflict-network"),
      {nodes: new vis.DataSet(graphNodes), edges: new vis.DataSet(graphEdges)},
      {nodes: {shape: "dot", scaling: {min: 10, max: 30}}, edges: {smooth: true}, physics: {stabilization: true}, interaction: {hover: true}}
    );
    network.on("doubleClick", ({nodes: selected}) => {
      if (selected.length) window.location.href = `/exams/${encodeURIComponent(selected[0])}/`;
    });
  })();
</script>
{% endif %}
{% endblock %}

    </div>
    <p class="small text-muted mt-3 mb-0">Actions run synchronously and record results through the same services used by management commands.</p>
  </div>

FILE: apps\dashboard\templatetags\dashboard_tags.py
======================================================================
"""Small template filters used by the dashboard."""

import json

from django import template

from apps.dashboard.permissions import can_run_actions as user_can_run_actions

register = template.Library()


@register.filter
def prettyjson(value):
    try:
        return json.dumps(value, indent=2, default=str)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return str(value)


@register.filter
def tags_for_bootstrap(tags: str) -> str:
    return {"error": "danger", "debug": "secondary"}.get(tags, tags)


@register.filter
def percent(value):
    try:
        return f"{float(value) * 100:.1f}%"
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return "—"


@register.filter
def can_run_actions(user):
    return user_can_run_actions(user)

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\admin.py
======================================================================
from django.contrib import admin

from .models import (
    Availability, InvigilatorAssignment, RoomClosure, ScheduleAssignment, ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun, StudentRoomAllocation, TimeSlot, UnassignedExam,
)


class ReadOnlyAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    """History tables: browse only, never edit or delete from the admin."""

    def has_add_permission(self, request):
        return False

    def has_change_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False

    def has_delete_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False


@admin.register(TimeSlot)
class TimeSlotAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("slot_id", "slot_date", "start_time", "label")
    list_filter = ("slot_date",)


@admin.register(RoomClosure)
class RoomClosureAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("closure_id", "room", "start_at", "end_at", "reason")
    list_filter = ("reason",)
    date_hierarchy = "start_at"
    search_fields = ("closure_id", "room__room_id")


@admin.register(Availability)
class AvailabilityAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("availability_id", "room", "invigilator", "start_at", "end_at")
    date_hierarchy = "start_at"
    search_fields = ("availability_id", "room__room_id", "invigilator__invigilator_id")


@admin.register(SchedulerRun)
class SchedulerRunAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("run_id", "run_type", "status", "algorithm_version", "seed", "started_at", "finished_at")
    list_filter = ("run_type", "status")
    readonly_fields = ("started_at", "finished_at")


@admin.register(ScheduleAssignment)
class ScheduleAssignmentAdmin(ReadOnlyAdmin):
    list_display = ("assignment_id", "run", "exam", "room", "start_at", "end_at", "allocated_count", "status")
    list_filter = ("run", "status")
    search_fields = ("assignment_id", "exam__exam_id", "room__room_id")
    date_hierarchy = "start_at"
    list_per_page = 50


@admin.register(StudentRoomAllocation)
class StudentRoomAllocationAdmin(ReadOnlyAdmin):
    list_display = ("allocation_id", "assignment", "student", "required_minutes")
    search_fields = ("allocation_id", "student__student_id", "exam__exam_id")
    list_per_page = 50


@admin.register(InvigilatorAssignment)
class InvigilatorAssignmentAdmin(ReadOnlyAdmin):
    list_display = ("ias_id", "assignment", "invigilator", "role")
    search_fields = ("ias_id", "invigilator__invigilator_id")


@admin.register(UnassignedExam)
class UnassignedExamAdmin(ReadOnlyAdmin):
    list_display = ("unassigned_id", "run", "exam", "primary_reason_code")
    list_filter = ("primary_reason_code", "run")


@admin.register(ScheduleEvent)
class ScheduleEventAdmin(ReadOnlyAdmin):
    list_display = ("event_id", "run", "exam", "event_type", "event_at")
    list_filter = ("event_type", "run")
    date_hierarchy = "event_at"
    search_fields = ("event_id", "exam__exam_id")

"""Scheduling models: runs, assignments, availability, closures, allocations, events."""
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.core.validators import MinValueValidator
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F, Q
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.models import Exam, Invigilator, Room, Student
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_assignment_id
from apps.core.validators import id_validator


class TimeSlot(models.Model):
    slot_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=11, validators=[id_validator("slot")])
    slot_date = models.DateField()
    start_time = models.TimeField()
    label = models.CharField(max_length=40, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["slot_date", "start_time"]
        constraints = [models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["slot_date", "start_time"], name="uniq_slot_date_time")]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.slot_id


class RoomClosure(models.Model):
    class Reason(models.TextChoices):
        MAINTENANCE = "MAINTENANCE", "Maintenance"
        EVENT = "EVENT", "Event"
        REPAIR = "REPAIR", "Repair"

    closure_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("closure")])
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="closures")
    run = models.ForeignKey(
        "SchedulerRun",
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="room_closures",
    )
    start_at = models.DateTimeField()
    end_at = models.DateTimeField()
    reason = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Reason.choices)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["start_at"]
        constraints = [models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(end_at__gt=F("start_at")), name="closure_end_after_start")]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["room", "start_at", "end_at"])]

    def clean(self):
        if self.start_at and self.end_at and self.end_at <= self.start_at:
            raise ValidationError("end_at must be after start_at.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.closure_id


class Availability(models.Model):
    """Availability window for EITHER a room OR an invigilator (exactly one)."""

    availability_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("availability")])
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="availabilities")
    invigilator = models.ForeignKey(
        Invigilator, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="availabilities"
    )
    start_at = models.DateTimeField()
    end_at = models.DateTimeField()

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["start_at"]
        verbose_name_plural = "availabilities"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(end_at__gt=F("start_at")), name="availability_end_after_start"),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(Q(room__isnull=False, invigilator__isnull=True)
                           | Q(room__isnull=True, invigilator__isnull=False)),
                name="availability_exactly_one_target",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["room", "start_at", "end_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["invigilator", "start_at", "end_at"]),
        ]

    def clean(self):
        if (self.room_id is None) == (self.invigilator_id is None):
            raise ValidationError("Set exactly one of room or invigilator.")
        if self.start_at and self.end_at and self.end_at <= self.start_at:
            raise ValidationError("end_at must be after start_at.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.availability_id


class SchedulerRun(models.Model):
    class RunType(models.TextChoices):
        BASELINE = "BASELINE", "Baseline"
        ANOMALY_FIXTURE = "ANOMALY_FIXTURE", "Anomaly fixture"

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        RUNNING = "RUNNING", "Running"
        COMPLETED = "COMPLETED", "Completed"
        FAILED = "FAILED", "Failed"

    run_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("run")])
    run_type = models.CharField(max_length=16, choices=RunType.choices, default=RunType.BASELINE)
    status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.RUNNING)
    algorithm_version = models.CharField(max_length=20, default="1.0")
    seed = models.BigIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    finished_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["run_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(finished_at__isnull=True) | Q(finished_at__gte=F("started_at")),
                name="run_finish_after_start",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.run_id


class ScheduleAssignment(models.Model):
    """Bridge Exam N:M Room, one row per exam-room-run."""

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        ASSIGNED = "ASSIGNED", "Assigned"
        SUPERSEDED = "SUPERSEDED", "Superseded"
        CANCELLED = "CANCELLED", "Cancelled"

    assignment_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=64, validators=[id_validator("assignment")])
    run = models.ForeignKey(SchedulerRun, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="assignments")
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="assignments")
    room = models.ForeignKey(Room, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="assignments")
    start_at = models.DateTimeField()
    end_at = models.DateTimeField()
    allocated_count = models.PositiveIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(1)])
    status = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ASSIGNED)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["assignment_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["run", "exam", "room"], name="uniq_assignment_run_exam_room"),
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(end_at__gt=F("start_at")), name="assignment_end_after_start"),
            models.CheckConstraint(condition=Q(allocated_count__gte=1), name="assignment_count_positive"),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["room", "start_at", "end_at"]),
            models.Index(fields=["run", "exam"]),
            models.Index(fields=["status"]),
        ]

    def clean(self):
        if self.start_at and self.end_at and self.end_at <= self.start_at:
            raise ValidationError("end_at must be after start_at.")
        if self.exam_id and self.room_id and self.run_id:
            expected = make_assignment_id(self.exam_id, self.room_id, self.run_id)
            if self.assignment_id != expected:
                raise ValidationError(f"assignment_id must be {expected}.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.assignment_id


class StudentRoomAllocation(models.Model):
    """Which student sits in which room. run and exam are stored too, so the database
    itself guarantees one student is never placed in two rooms for the same exam."""

    allocation_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("student_allocation")])
    assignment = models.ForeignKey(ScheduleAssignment, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="student_allocations")
    run = models.ForeignKey(SchedulerRun, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="student_allocations")
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="student_allocations")
    student = models.ForeignKey(Student, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="room_allocations")
    required_minutes = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(validators=[MinValueValidator(30)])

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["allocation_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["assignment", "student"], name="uniq_alloc_assignment_student"),
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["run", "exam", "student"], name="uniq_alloc_run_exam_student"),
        ]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["student", "run"])]

    def clean(self):
        if self.assignment_id:
            a = ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(pk=self.assignment_id).first()
            if a and (a.exam_id != self.exam_id or a.run_id != self.run_id):
                raise ValidationError("run and exam must match the assignment.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.allocation_id


class InvigilatorAssignment(models.Model):
    class Role(models.TextChoices):
        CHIEF = "CHIEF", "Chief"
        ASSISTANT = "ASSISTANT", "Assistant"

    ias_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("invigilator_assignment")])
    assignment = models.ForeignKey(ScheduleAssignment, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="invigilator_assignments")
    invigilator = models.ForeignKey(Invigilator, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="assignments")
    role = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=Role.choices, default=Role.ASSISTANT)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["ias_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["assignment", "invigilator"], name="uniq_ias_assignment_invigilator"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.ias_id


class UnassignedExam(models.Model):
    class Reason(models.TextChoices):
        NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT = "NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT", "No conflict-free slot"
        NO_ROOM_CAPACITY = "NO_ROOM_CAPACITY", "No room capacity"
        NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM = "NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM", "No room with equipment"
        ROOM_UNAVAILABLE = "ROOM_UNAVAILABLE", "Room unavailable"
        NO_INVIGILATOR = "NO_INVIGILATOR", "No invigilator"
        ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE = "ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE", "Accommodation unsatisfiable"
        NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT = "NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT", "No feasible assignment"

    unassigned_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("unassigned")])
    run = models.ForeignKey(SchedulerRun, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="unassigned_exams")
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="unassigned_records")
    primary_reason_code = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=Reason.choices)
    evidence = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["unassigned_id"]
        constraints = [models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["run", "exam"], name="uniq_unassigned_run_exam")]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.unassigned_id


class AppendOnlyQuerySet(models.QuerySet):
    def update(self, **kwargs):
        raise PermissionError("ScheduleEvent rows are append-only.")

    def delete(self):
        raise PermissionError("ScheduleEvent rows are append-only.")


class ScheduleEvent(models.Model):
    """Append-only history. Rows can be created, never changed or deleted."""

    class EventType(models.TextChoices):
        ASSIGNED = "ASSIGNED", "Assigned"
        MOVED = "MOVED", "Moved"
        CANCELLED = "CANCELLED", "Cancelled"
        UNASSIGNED = "UNASSIGNED", "Unassigned"
        SUPERSEDED = "SUPERSEDED", "Superseded"

    event_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("event")])
    run = models.ForeignKey(SchedulerRun, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="events")
    assignment = models.ForeignKey(
        ScheduleAssignment, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="events"
    )
    exam = models.ForeignKey(Exam, null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="events")
    event_type = models.CharField(max_length=12, choices=EventType.choices)
    detail = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    event_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)

    objects = AppendOnlyQuerySet.as_manager()

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["event_id"]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["run", "exam"])]

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self._state.adding:
            raise PermissionError("ScheduleEvent rows are append-only and cannot be changed.")
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def delete(self, *args, **kwargs):
        raise PermissionError("ScheduleEvent rows are append-only and cannot be deleted.")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.event_id

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\management\commands\run_scheduler.py
======================================================================
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.scheduling.models import (
    ScheduleAssignment,
    SchedulerRun,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Schedule exams and persist a new, traceable SchedulerRun."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int)
        parser.add_argument(
            "--run-type",
            choices=[choice.value for choice in SchedulerRun.RunType],
            default=SchedulerRun.RunType.BASELINE,
        )
        parser.add_argument(
            "--no-reset",
            action="store_true",
            help="Schedule only exams currently in PLANNED status.",
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        run = run_scheduler(
            seed=options["seed"],
            run_type=options["run_type"],
            reset_exams=not options["no_reset"],
        )
        assignment_count = ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=run).count()
        unassigned_count = UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run).count()
        output = (
            f"{run.run_id}: {assignment_count} room assignments, "
            f"{unassigned_count} unassigned "
            f"({run.notes})"
        )
        if run.status != SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED:
            raise CommandError(f"Scheduling run failed: {output}")
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(output))

======================================================================

"""Resolve effective student accommodations for an exam date."""

from django.db.models import Q

from apps.core.models import Accommodation


def accommodations_active_on(student_id, on_date):
    return list(
        Accommodation.objects.filter(
            student_id=student_id,
            status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE,
            effective_from__lte=on_date,
        )
        .filter(Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=on_date))
        .order_by("accommodation_id")
    )


def active_accommodations(accommodations, on_date):
    """Filter preloaded accommodations without issuing per-student queries."""
    return [
        accommodation
        for accommodation in accommodations
        if accommodation.status == Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE
        and accommodation.effective_from <= on_date
        and (
            accommodation.effective_to is None
            or accommodation.effective_to >= on_date
        )
    ]


def required_minutes(base_minutes, accommodations):
    extra_time_percent = max(
        (
            accommodation.extra_time_percent
            for accommodation in accommodations
            if accommodation.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME
        ),
        default=0,
    )
    return (base_minutes * (100 + extra_time_percent) + 99) // 100


def needs_separate_room(accommodations):
    return any(
        accommodation.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM
        for accommodation in accommodations
    )


def needs_accessible_room(accommodations):
    return any(
        accommodation.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM
        for accommodation in accommodations
    )

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\services\conflict_graph.py
======================================================================
"""Build a weighted exam-conflict graph from active term enrollments."""

from collections import defaultdict

from django.conf import settings

from apps.core.models import Enrollment


class ConflictGraph:
    """Exam vertices; edge weights count students enrolled in both exams."""

    def __init__(self, exams=()):
        self.exams = list(exams)
        self.adj = {exam.exam_id: {} for exam in self.exams}
        self.degree = {exam.exam_id: 0 for exam in self.exams}

    def add_edge(self, first, second, weight=1):
        if first == second:
            return
        self.adj.setdefault(first, {})
        self.adj.setdefault(second, {})
        self.adj[first][second] = self.adj[first].get(second, 0) + weight
        self.adj[second][first] = self.adj[second].get(first, 0) + weight
        self.degree[first] = len(self.adj[first])
        self.degree[second] = len(self.adj[second])

    def neighbors(self, exam_id):
        return self.adj.get(exam_id, {})

    def edge_weight(self, first, second):
        return self.adj.get(first, {}).get(second, 0)


def build_conflict_graph(exams, term=None):
    """Return the graph and active student IDs grouped by exam ID."""
    term = term or settings.SCHEDULER_POLICY["TERM"]
    graph = ConflictGraph(exams)
    exam_by_course = {exam.course_id: exam.exam_id for exam in graph.exams}
    students_by_exam = {exam.exam_id: set() for exam in graph.exams}
    exams_by_student = defaultdict(set)

    enrollments = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE,
            term=term,
            course_id__in=exam_by_course,
        )
        .order_by("student_id", "course_id")
        .values_list("student_id", "course_id")
        .iterator()
    )
    for student_id, course_id in enrollments:
        exam_id = exam_by_course[course_id]
        students_by_exam[exam_id].add(student_id)
        exams_by_student[student_id].add(exam_id)

    for exam_ids in exams_by_student.values():
        ordered_ids = sorted(exam_ids)
        for index, first in enumerate(ordered_ids):
            for second in ordered_ids[index + 1:]:
                graph.add_edge(first, second)

    return graph, students_by_exam

FILE: apps\scheduling\services\context.py

"""Preloaded, per-run in-memory scheduling state."""

from collections import defaultdict

from django.db.models import Q

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_id
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    RoomClosure,
    ScheduleEvent,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    TimeSlot,
    UnassignedExam,
)


def _next_counter(model, field_name):
    last_value = (
        model.objects.order_by(f"-{field_name}")
        .values_list(field_name, flat=True)
        .first()
    )
    if not last_value:
        return 0
    try:
        return int(last_value.rsplit("-", 1)[-1])
    except ValueError as error:
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid {field_name} value in {model._meta.label}.") from error


class SchedulingContext:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        run,
        term,
        exams,
        graph,
        students_by_exam,
        invigilators=(),
        capacities=None,
        invigilator_avails=None,
        exam_requirements=None,
        policy=None,
        rooms=(),
        room_equipment=None,
        exam_equipment=None,
        room_avails=None,
        room_closures=None,
        slots=(),
        accommodations=None,
    ):
        self.run = run
        self.term = term
        self.exams = list(exams)
        self.graph = graph
        self.students_by_exam = students_by_exam
        self.invigilators = list(invigilators)
        self.capacities = capacities or {}
        self.invigilator_avails = invigilator_avails or {}
        self.exam_requirements = exam_requirements or {}
        self.policy = policy or {}
        self.rooms = list(rooms)
        self.room_equipment = room_equipment or {}
        self.exam_equipment = exam_equipment or {}
        self.room_avails = room_avails or {}
        self.room_closures = room_closures or {}
        self.slots = list(slots)
        self.accommodations = accommodations or {}
        self.students = {}

        self.room_bookings = defaultdict(list)
        self.invigilator_bookings = defaultdict(list)
        self.student_intervals = defaultdict(list)
        self.exam_slots = {}
        self.time_groups = {}
        self.last_room_evidence = {}

        self._sra_counter = _next_counter(
            StudentRoomAllocation, "allocation_id"
        )
        self._ias_counter = _next_counter(InvigilatorAssignment, "ias_id")
        self._evt_counter = _next_counter(ScheduleEvent, "event_id")
        self._una_counter = _next_counter(UnassignedExam, "unassigned_id")

    def next_sra(self):
        self._sra_counter += 1
        return make_id("student_allocation", self._sra_counter)

    def next_ias(self):
        self._ias_counter += 1
        return make_id("invigilator_assignment", self._ias_counter)

    def next_evt(self):
        self._evt_counter += 1
        return make_id("event", self._evt_counter)

    def next_una(self):
        self._una_counter += 1
        return make_id("unassigned", self._una_counter)

    def capacity(self, room, on_date):
        effective = [
            (effective_from, capacity)
            for effective_from, effective_to, capacity
            in self.capacities.get(room.room_id, ())
            if effective_from <= on_date
            and (effective_to is None or effective_to >= on_date)
        ]
        return max(effective, default=(None, 0))[1]

def build_context(run, term, policy):
    exams = list(
        Exam.objects.filter(term=term)
        .select_related("course")
        .order_by("exam_id")
    )
    from apps.scheduling.services.conflict_graph import build_conflict_graph

    graph, students_by_exam = build_conflict_graph(exams, term=term)

    capacities = defaultdict(list)
    for capacity in RoomCapacity.objects.order_by("room_id", "effective_from"):
        capacities[capacity.room_id].append(
            (
                capacity.effective_from,
                capacity.effective_to,
                capacity.exam_capacity,
            )
        )

    invigilator_avails = defaultdict(list)
    room_avails = defaultdict(list)
    for availability in Availability.objects.order_by("availability_id"):
        interval = (availability.start_at, availability.end_at)
        if availability.invigilator_id:
            invigilator_avails[availability.invigilator_id].append(interval)
        elif availability.room_id:
            room_avails[availability.room_id].append(interval)

    room_closures = defaultdict(list)
    for closure in RoomClosure.objects.filter(
        Q(run__isnull=True) | Q(run=run)
    ).order_by("closure_id"):
        room_closures[closure.room_id].append(
            (closure.start_at, closure.end_at)
        )

    room_equipment = defaultdict(set)
    for room_id, equipment_id in RoomEquipment.objects.order_by(
        "room_id", "equipment_id"
    ).values_list("room_id", "equipment_id"):
        room_equipment[room_id].add(equipment_id)

    requirements = {
        requirement.exam_id: requirement
        for requirement in ExamRequirement.objects.order_by("requirement_id")
    }
    requirement_equipment = defaultdict(set)
    for requirement_id, equipment_id in ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.values_list(
        "requirement_id", "equipment_id"
    ):
        requirement_equipment[requirement_id].add(equipment_id)
    exam_equipment = {
        exam_id: requirement_equipment[requirement.requirement_id]
        for exam_id, requirement in requirements.items()
    }
    active_student_ids = {
        student_id
        for student_ids in students_by_exam.values()
        for student_id in student_ids
    }

    accommodations = defaultdict(list)
    for accommodation in Accommodation.objects.filter(
        status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE
    ).order_by("accommodation_id"):
        accommodations[accommodation.student_id].append(accommodation)

    context = SchedulingContext(
        run=run,
        term=term,
        exams=exams,
        graph=graph,
        students_by_exam=students_by_exam,
        invigilators=Invigilator.objects.filter(
            status=Invigilator.Status.ACTIVE
        ).order_by("invigilator_id"),
        capacities=capacities,
        invigilator_avails=invigilator_avails,
        exam_requirements=requirements,
        policy=policy,
        rooms=Room.objects.filter(status=Room.Status.ACTIVE).order_by("room_id"),
        room_equipment=room_equipment,
        exam_equipment=exam_equipment,
        room_avails=room_avails,
        room_closures=room_closures,
        slots=TimeSlot.objects.order_by("slot_date", "start_time", "slot_id"),
        accommodations=accommodations,
    )
    context.students = Student.objects.in_bulk(active_student_ids)
    return context

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\services\invigilator_allocator.py

"""Allocate available invigilators without overlaps or daily-hour violations."""

import math

from apps.scheduling.models import UnassignedExam
from apps.scheduling.services.slot_allocator import buffered_overlap


def _available(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context):
    return any(
        window_start <= start_at and window_end >= end_at
        for window_start, window_end in context.invigilator_avails.get(
            invigilator.invigilator_id, ()
        )
    )


def _free(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context):
    return not any(
        buffered_overlap(
            start_at,
            end_at,
            booked_start,
            booked_end,
            context.policy.get("HARD_BUFFER_MINUTES", 0),
        )
        for booked_start, booked_end in context.invigilator_bookings.get(
            invigilator.invigilator_id, ()
        )
    )


def _within_daily_limit(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context):
    assigned_minutes = sum(
        (booked_end - booked_start).total_seconds() / 60
        for booked_start, booked_end in context.invigilator_bookings.get(
            invigilator.invigilator_id, ()
        )
        if booked_start.date() == start_at.date()
    )
    required_minutes = (end_at - start_at).total_seconds() / 60
    return (
        assigned_minutes + required_minutes
        <= float(invigilator.max_daily_hours) * 60
    )


def _within_consecutive_limit(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context):
    limit_hours = context.policy.get("MAX_CONSECUTIVE_INVIGILATOR_HOURS")
    if limit_hours is None:
        return True

    intervals = sorted(
        [
            *context.invigilator_bookings.get(
                invigilator.invigilator_id, ()
            ),
            (start_at, end_at),
        ]
    )
    chain_start, chain_end = intervals[0]
    limit_minutes = float(limit_hours) * 60
    for next_start, next_end in intervals[1:]:
        if next_start > chain_end:
            chain_start, chain_end = next_start, next_end
        else:
            chain_end = max(chain_end, next_end)
        if (chain_end - chain_start).total_seconds() / 60 > limit_minutes:
            return False
    return (chain_end - chain_start).total_seconds() / 60 <= limit_minutes


def allocate_invigilators(context, exam, room_allocations, start_at, end_at):
    """Return per-room staff assignments or a ``NO_INVIGILATOR`` reason."""
    requirement = context.exam_requirements.get(exam.exam_id)
    ratio = requirement.invigilator_ratio if requirement else 50
    used_ids = set()
    result = []

    workloads = getattr(context, "invigilator_workload_minutes", {})
    for room, students in room_allocations:
        needed = max(1, math.ceil(len(students) / ratio))
        eligible = [
            invigilator
            for invigilator in context.invigilators
            if invigilator.invigilator_id not in used_ids
            and _available(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context)
            and _free(invigilator, start_at, end_at, context)
            and _within_daily_limit(
                invigilator, start_at, end_at, context
            )
            and _within_consecutive_limit(
                invigilator, start_at, end_at, context
            )
        ]
        eligible.sort(
            key=lambda invigilator: (
                workloads.get(invigilator.invigilator_id, 0),
                invigilator.invigilator_id,
            )
        )
        selected = eligible[:needed]
        if len(selected) != needed:
            return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_INVIGILATOR
        used_ids.update(inv.invigilator_id for inv in selected)
        result.append((room, selected))

    return result, None

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\services\room_allocator.py

"""Best-fit room allocation with capacity, equipment, and availability checks."""

from apps.scheduling.services.accommodation import (
    needs_accessible_room,
    needs_separate_room,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.slot_allocator import buffered_overlap, overlap
from apps.scheduling.models import UnassignedExam


def _rooms_for_interval(ctx, exam, start_at, end_at):
    on_date = start_at.date()
    equipment_needed = getattr(ctx, "exam_equipment", {}).get(exam.exam_id, set())
    available = []
    rejected = {
        "inactive_or_missing_capacity": 0,
        "missing_equipment": 0,
        "unavailable_or_closed": 0,
        "already_booked": 0,
    }

    for room in ctx.rooms:
        capacity = ctx.capacity(room, on_date)
        if capacity <= 0:
            rejected["inactive_or_missing_capacity"] += 1
            continue
        if not equipment_needed.issubset(
            ctx.room_equipment.get(room.room_id, set())
        ):
            rejected["missing_equipment"] += 1
            continue
        if not any(
            window_start <= start_at and window_end >= end_at
            for window_start, window_end in ctx.room_avails.get(
                room.room_id, ()
            )
        ):
            rejected["unavailable_or_closed"] += 1
            continue
        if any(
            overlap(start_at, end_at, closure_start, closure_end)
            for closure_start, closure_end in ctx.room_closures.get(
                room.room_id, ()
            )
        ):
            rejected["unavailable_or_closed"] += 1
            continue
        if any(
            buffered_overlap(
                start_at,
                end_at,
                booked_start,
                booked_end,
                ctx.policy.get("HARD_BUFFER_MINUTES", 0),
            )
            for booked_start, booked_end in ctx.room_bookings.get(
                room.room_id, ()
            )
        ):
            rejected["already_booked"] += 1
            continue
        available.append((room, capacity))

    return available, rejected


def _place_group(
    ctx,
    students,
    candidate_rooms,
    allocations,
    *,
    separate,
    allow_multi_room,
    require_accessible,
):
    """Place a group in best-fit rooms; return False without partial side effects."""
    remaining = list(students)
    if not remaining:
        return True

    candidates = sorted(
        candidate_rooms,
        key=lambda item: (item[1], item[0].room_id),
    )
    existing = [
        allocation
        for allocation in allocations
        if allocation["separate"] == separate
        and (not require_accessible or allocation["accessible"])
        and allocation["capacity"] > len(allocation["students"])
    ]
    for allocation in existing:
        take = min(
            len(remaining),
            allocation["capacity"] - len(allocation["students"]),
        )
        allocation["students"].extend(remaining[:take])
        remaining = remaining[take:]
        if not remaining:
            return True

    eligible = [
        item for item in candidates
        if item[0].room_id not in {allocation["room"].room_id for allocation in allocations}
    ]
    if not allow_multi_room:
        eligible = [
            item for item in eligible if item[1] >= len(remaining)
        ]
        if not eligible:
            return False
        selected = min(
            eligible,
            key=lambda item: (item[1] - len(remaining), item[0].room_id),
        )
        allocations.append(
            {
                "room": selected[0],
                "capacity": selected[1],
                "students": remaining,
                "separate": separate,
                "accessible": selected[0].is_accessible,
            }
        )
        return True

    while remaining:
        fitting = [item for item in eligible if item[1] >= len(remaining)]
        if fitting:
            selected = min(
                fitting,
                key=lambda item: (item[1] - len(remaining), item[0].room_id),
            )
            take = len(remaining)
        elif eligible:
            selected = max(
                eligible,
                key=lambda item: (item[1], item[0].room_id),
            )
            take = selected[1]
        else:
            return False

        allocations.append(
            {
                "room": selected[0],
                "capacity": selected[1],
                "students": remaining[:take],
                "separate": separate,
                "accessible": selected[0].is_accessible,
            }
        )
        remaining = remaining[take:]
        eligible.remove(selected)

    return True


def allocate_rooms(ctx, exam, students_with_acc, start_at, end_at):
    """Return ``(room/student allocations, reason)`` for one exam and slot."""
    compatible_rooms, rejected = _rooms_for_interval(
        ctx, exam, start_at, end_at
    )
    ctx.last_room_evidence = {
        "compatible_room_count": len(compatible_rooms),
        "rejected_room_counts": rejected,
    }
    if not compatible_rooms:
        if rejected["missing_equipment"]:
            return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM
        if rejected["unavailable_or_closed"] or rejected["already_booked"]:
            return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.ROOM_UNAVAILABLE
        return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_ROOM_CAPACITY

    allocations = []
    separate_students = []
    separate_accessible = []
    accessible_students = []
    regular_students = []
    for student, accommodations in students_with_acc:
        if needs_separate_room(accommodations):
            separate_students.append(student)
            if needs_accessible_room(accommodations):
                separate_accessible.append(student)
        elif needs_accessible_room(accommodations):
            accessible_students.append(student)
        else:
            regular_students.append(student)

    requirement = ctx.exam_requirements.get(exam.exam_id)
    allow_multi_room = requirement.allow_multi_room if requirement else True

    if not allow_multi_room:
        if separate_students and len(separate_students) != len(students_with_acc):
            ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = "separate_room"
            return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE
        needs_accessible = any(
            needs_accessible_room(accommodations)
            for _, accommodations in students_with_acc
        )
        candidates = [
            item for item in compatible_rooms
            if not needs_accessible or item[0].is_accessible
        ]
        candidates = [
            item for item in candidates
            if item[1] >= len(students_with_acc)
        ]
        if not candidates:
            ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = (
                "accessible_room" if needs_accessible else "room_capacity"
            )
            reason = (
                UnassignedExam.Reason.ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE
                if needs_accessible
                else UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_ROOM_CAPACITY
            )
            return None, reason
        room, _capacity = min(
            candidates,
            key=lambda item: (
                item[1] - len(students_with_acc),
                item[0].room_id,
            ),
        )
        return [(room, [student for student, _ in students_with_acc])], None

    accessible_rooms = [
        item for item in compatible_rooms if item[0].is_accessible
    ]
    if separate_accessible:
        if not _place_group(
            ctx,
            separate_accessible,
            accessible_rooms,
            allocations,
            separate=True,
            allow_multi_room=allow_multi_room,
            require_accessible=True,
        ):
            ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = "separate_accessible"
            return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE

    separate_regular = [
        student for student in separate_students
        if student not in set(separate_accessible)
    ]
    if separate_regular and not _place_group(
        ctx,
        separate_regular,
        compatible_rooms,
        allocations,
        separate=True,
        allow_multi_room=allow_multi_room,
        require_accessible=False,
    ):
        ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = "separate_room"
        return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE

    if accessible_students and not _place_group(
        ctx,
        accessible_students,
        accessible_rooms,
        allocations,
        separate=False,
        allow_multi_room=allow_multi_room,
        require_accessible=True,
    ):
        ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = "accessible_room"
        return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE

    # Students without a room restriction may use spare seats in rooms that
    # already contain non-separated accessible students.
    for allocation in allocations:
        if allocation["separate"]:
            continue
        if allocation["capacity"] <= len(allocation["students"]):
            continue
        take = min(
            len(regular_students),
            allocation["capacity"] - len(allocation["students"]),
        )
        allocation["students"].extend(regular_students[:take])
        regular_students = regular_students[take:]
        if not regular_students:
            break

    if regular_students and not _place_group(
        ctx,
        regular_students,
        compatible_rooms,
        allocations,
        separate=False,
        allow_multi_room=allow_multi_room,
        require_accessible=False,
    ):
        ctx.last_room_evidence["failure_group"] = "room_capacity"
        return None, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_ROOM_CAPACITY

    if len(allocations) == 0:
        return [], None
    return [
        (allocation["room"], allocation["students"])
        for allocation in allocations
    ], None

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\services\scheduler.py
======================================================================
"""Schedule exams and persist each run without overwriting prior history."""

import datetime as dt
import logging
from collections import Counter

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import DatabaseError, transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.models import Exam
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_assignment_id, make_id
from apps.core.services.lifecycle import transition
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.accommodation import (
    active_accommodations,
    required_minutes,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.context import build_context
from apps.scheduling.services.explanations import (
    explain_unassigned,
    record_event,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.invigilator_allocator import allocate_invigilators
from apps.scheduling.services.room_allocator import allocate_rooms
from apps.scheduling.services.slot_allocator import (
    buffered_overlap,
    color_exams,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _next_run_id():
    last_id = SchedulerRun.objects.order_by("-run_id").values_list(
        "run_id", flat=True
    ).first()
    number = int(last_id.rsplit("-", 1)[-1]) + 1 if last_id else 1
    return make_id("run", number)


def _reset_exam_statuses(term):
    for exam in Exam.objects.filter(
        term=term,
        status__in=[Exam.Status.SCHEDULED, Exam.Status.UNASSIGNED],
    ).order_by("exam_id"):
        transition(exam, Exam.Status.PLANNED)


def _students_for_exam(context, exam):
    students = context.students_by_exam.get(exam.exam_id, set())
    return [
        context.students[student_id]
        for student_id in sorted(students)
        if student_id in context.students
    ]


def _student_interval_conflict(context, students, start_at, end_at):
    for student, _accommodations in students:
        if any(
            buffered_overlap(
                start_at,
                end_at,
                booked_start,
                booked_end,
                context.policy.get("HARD_BUFFER_MINUTES", 0),
            )
            for booked_start, booked_end in context.student_intervals.get(
                student.student_id, ()
            )
        ):
            return True
    return False


def _candidate_intervals(context, exam, students):
    """Yield fixed-calendar slots with accommodation-aware durations."""
    operating_start = context.policy.get("OPERATING_START", "08:00")
    operating_end = context.policy.get("OPERATING_END", "20:00")
    earliest = dt.time.fromisoformat(operating_start)
    latest = dt.time.fromisoformat(operating_end)
    any_slot = False
    color = context.time_groups.get(exam.exam_id)
    preferred_slot = (
        context.slots[color]
        if color is not None and color < len(context.slots)
        else None
    )
    slots = (
        ([preferred_slot] if preferred_slot else [])
        + [
            slot
            for slot in context.slots
            if slot is not preferred_slot
        ]
    )
    for slot in slots:
        if slot.start_time < earliest or slot.start_time >= latest:
            continue
        start_at = dt.datetime.combine(
            slot.slot_date, slot.start_time, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc
        )
        student_accommodations = [
            (
                student,
                active_accommodations(
                    context.accommodations.get(student.student_id, ()),
                    slot.slot_date,
                ),
            )
            for student in students
        ]
        duration = max(
            (
                required_minutes(exam.duration_minutes, accommodations)
                for _, accommodations in student_accommodations
            ),
            default=exam.duration_minutes,
        )
        end_at = start_at + dt.timedelta(minutes=duration)
        if end_at.date() != slot.slot_date or end_at.time() > latest:
            continue
        if start_at.time() < earliest:
            continue
        any_slot = True
        yield slot, start_at, end_at, student_accommodations

    if not any_slot:
        return


def _commit_assignment(
    context,
    exam,
    room_allocations,
    invigilator_allocations,
    students_with_accommodations,
    start_at,
    end_at,
):
    created_assignments = []
    students_by_id = {
        student.student_id: accommodations
        for student, accommodations in students_with_accommodations
    }

    for room, students in room_allocations:
        assignment = ScheduleAssignment.objects.create(
            assignment_id=make_assignment_id(
                exam.exam_id, room.room_id, context.run.run_id
            ),
            run=context.run,
            exam=exam,
            room=room,
            start_at=start_at,
            end_at=end_at,
            allocated_count=len(students),
        )
        created_assignments.append(assignment)

        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                StudentRoomAllocation(
                    allocation_id=context.next_sra(),
                    assignment=assignment,
                    run=context.run,
                    exam=exam,
                    student=student,
                    required_minutes=required_minutes(
                        exam.duration_minutes,
                        students_by_id[student.student_id],
                    ),
                )
                for student in students
            ],
            batch_size=1000,
        )

        invigilators = next(
            (
                assigned
                for assigned_room, assigned in invigilator_allocations
                if assigned_room.room_id == room.room_id
            ),
            [],
        )
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.bulk_create(
            [
                InvigilatorAssignment(
                    ias_id=context.next_ias(),
                    assignment=assignment,
                    invigilator=invigilator,
                    role=(
                        InvigilatorAssignment.Role.CHIEF
                        if index == 0
                        else InvigilatorAssignment.Role.ASSISTANT
                    ),
                )
                for index, invigilator in enumerate(invigilators)
            ],
            batch_size=1000,
        )

    transition(exam, Exam.Status.SCHEDULED)
    record_event(
        context,
        exam,
        ScheduleEvent.EventType.ASSIGNED,
        {
            "assignment_ids": [
                assignment.assignment_id for assignment in created_assignments
            ],
            "start_at": start_at.isoformat(),
            "end_at": end_at.isoformat(),
            "allocated_count": sum(
                len(students) for _, students in room_allocations
            ),
            "room_count": len(room_allocations),
        },
        assignment=created_assignments[0] if len(created_assignments) == 1 else None,
    )

    # Update feasibility state only after the database writes have succeeded.
    for room, students in room_allocations:
        context.room_bookings[room.room_id].append((start_at, end_at))
        invigilators = next(
            assigned
            for assigned_room, assigned in invigilator_allocations
            if assigned_room.room_id == room.room_id
        )
        for invigilator in invigilators:
            invigilator_id = invigilator.invigilator_id
            context.invigilator_bookings[invigilator_id].append(
                (start_at, end_at)
            )
            workload = getattr(context, "invigilator_workload_minutes", None)
            if workload is None:
                context.invigilator_workload_minutes = workload = Counter()
            workload[invigilator_id] += int(
                (end_at - start_at).total_seconds() // 60
            )
        for student in students:
            context.student_intervals[student.student_id].append(
                (start_at, end_at)
            )

    context.exam_slots[exam.exam_id] = (start_at, end_at)
    return created_assignments


def _record_unassigned(context, exam, reasons, evidence=None):
    if reasons:
        primary_reason, _count = min(
            reasons.items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0])
        )
    else:
        primary_reason = UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT

    valid_reasons = {choice.value for choice in UnassignedExam.Reason}
    if primary_reason not in valid_reasons:
        primary_reason = UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT

    detail = {
        "reasons": dict(sorted(reasons.items())),
        "rejection_evidence": evidence or {},
    }
    UnassignedExam.objects.create(
        unassigned_id=context.next_una(),
        run=context.run,
        exam=exam,
        primary_reason_code=primary_reason,
        evidence=explain_unassigned(exam.exam_id, primary_reason, detail),
    )
    transition(exam, Exam.Status.UNASSIGNED)
    record_event(
        context,
        exam,
        ScheduleEvent.EventType.UNASSIGNED,
        {
            "primary_reason": primary_reason,
            **detail,
        },
    )
    return primary_reason


def try_schedule_exam(context, exam):
    """Try every configured slot, returning ``(scheduled, rejection_counts)``."""
    students = _students_for_exam(context, exam)
    if not students:
        transition(exam, Exam.Status.SCHEDULED)
        record_event(
            context,
            exam,
            ScheduleEvent.EventType.ASSIGNED,
            {"reason": "no_active_enrollments", "allocated_count": 0},
        )
        return True, Counter()

    reasons = Counter()
    evidence = {
        "student_count": len(students),
        "slots_considered": 0,
        "rooms": {},
    }
    for _slot, start_at, end_at, students_with_accommodations in (
        _candidate_intervals(context, exam, students)
    ):
        evidence["slots_considered"] += 1
        if _student_interval_conflict(
            context, students_with_accommodations, start_at, end_at
        ):
            reasons[UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT.value] += 1
            continue

        room_allocations, room_reason = allocate_rooms(
            context,
            exam,
            students_with_accommodations,
            start_at,
            end_at,
        )
        if room_allocations is None:
            reasons[room_reason.value] += 1
            evidence["rooms"][room_reason.value] = (
                context.last_room_evidence
            )
            continue

        invigilator_allocations, staffing_reason = allocate_invigilators(
            context, exam, room_allocations, start_at, end_at
        )
        if invigilator_allocations is None:
            reasons[staffing_reason.value] += 1
            continue

        _commit_assignment(
            context,
            exam,
            room_allocations,
            invigilator_allocations,
            students_with_accommodations,
            start_at,
            end_at,
        )
        return True, reasons

    if evidence["slots_considered"] == 0:
        reasons[UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT.value] += 1
        evidence["detail"] = (
            "No exam-calendar slot can contain the required duration "
            "within the configured operating hours."
        )
    _record_unassigned(context, exam, reasons, evidence)
    return False, reasons


@transaction.atomic
def run_scheduler(seed=None, run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.BASELINE, reset_exams=True):
    """Run the deterministic scheduler and append a new run to history."""
    valid_run_types = {choice.value for choice in SchedulerRun.RunType}
    if run_type not in valid_run_types:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown run type {run_type!r}.")

    policy = settings.SCHEDULER_POLICY
    term = policy["TERM"]
    if reset_exams:
        _reset_exam_statuses(term)

    run = SchedulerRun.objects.create(
        run_id=_next_run_id(),
        run_type=run_type,
        seed=seed,
        algorithm_version="phase-4-dsatur-1",
        notes=f"term={term}",
    )
    context = build_context(run, term, policy)
    ordered_exams, context.time_groups = color_exams(
        context.graph, context.students_by_exam, context
    )

    scheduled_count = 0
    exams_with_assignments = 0
    unassigned_count = 0
    failures = []

    for exam in ordered_exams:
        if exam.status != Exam.Status.PLANNED:
            continue
        savepoint_id = transaction.savepoint()
        try:
            scheduled, _reasons = try_schedule_exam(context, exam)
            transaction.savepoint_commit(savepoint_id)
            if scheduled:
                scheduled_count += 1
                if context.exam_slots.get(exam.exam_id):
                    exams_with_assignments += 1
            else:
                unassigned_count += 1
        except (DatabaseError, ValidationError, ValueError):
            transaction.savepoint_rollback(savepoint_id)
            logger.exception(
                "Could not persist a valid schedule for exam %s.", exam.exam_id
            )
            exam.refresh_from_db()
            failure_evidence = {
                "error": "Schedule persistence failed for this exam.",
            }
            failure_savepoint = transaction.savepoint()
            _record_unassigned(
                context,
                exam,
                Counter(
                    {
                        UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT.value: 1
                    }
                ),
                failure_evidence,
            )
            transaction.savepoint_commit(failure_savepoint)
            unassigned_count += 1
            failures.append(exam.exam_id)

    final_status = (
        SchedulerRun.Status.FAILED
        if failures
        else SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED
    )
    transition(run, final_status)
    run.finished_at = timezone.now()
    run.notes = (
        f"term={term}; scheduled={scheduled_count}; "
        f"scheduled_with_assignments={exams_with_assignments}; "
        f"unassigned={unassigned_count}; failed_exam_persistence={len(failures)}"
    )
    run.save(update_fields=["finished_at", "notes"])
    return run

======================================================================
FILE: apps\scheduling\services\slot_allocator.py
======================================================================
"""DSatur exam ordering and half-open interval helpers."""

import datetime as dt


def overlap(first_start, first_end, second_start, second_end):
    return first_start < second_end and second_start < first_end


def buffered_overlap(
    first_start, first_end, second_start, second_end, buffer_minutes=0
):
    if buffer_minutes < 0:
        raise ValueError("buffer_minutes must be non-negative.")
    buffer = dt.timedelta(minutes=buffer_minutes)
    return overlap(
        first_start - buffer,
        first_end + buffer,
        second_start,
        second_end,
    )


def _restriction_score(exam, context):
    requirement = context.exam_requirements.get(exam.exam_id)
    equipment_count = len(
        getattr(context, "exam_equipment", {}).get(exam.exam_id, ())
    )
    return (
        equipment_count,
        requirement.invigilator_ratio if requirement else 50,
    )


def color_exams(graph, students_by_exam, context=None):
    """Return a deterministic DSatur ordering and a time-group color per exam."""
    remaining = {exam.exam_id: exam for exam in graph.exams}
    colors = {}
    ordered = []

    while remaining:
        def priority(exam):
            neighbor_colors = {
                colors[neighbor]
                for neighbor in graph.neighbors(exam.exam_id)
                if neighbor in colors
            }
            restriction = _restriction_score(exam, context) if context else (0, 50)
            return (
                -len(neighbor_colors),
                -graph.degree.get(exam.exam_id, 0),
                -len(students_by_exam.get(exam.exam_id, ())),
                -restriction[0],
                restriction[1],
                -exam.duration_minutes,
                exam.exam_id,
            )

        exam = min(remaining.values(), key=priority)
        used_colors = {
            colors[neighbor]
            for neighbor in graph.neighbors(exam.exam_id)
            if neighbor in colors
        }
        color = 0
        while color in used_colors:
            color += 1
        colors[exam.exam_id] = color
        ordered.append(exam)
        del remaining[exam.exam_id]

    return ordered, colors


def order_exams(graph, students_by_exam, context=None):
    """Return exams in deterministic DSatur priority order."""
    return color_exams(graph, students_by_exam, context)[0]

from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F, Q
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.validators import id_validator


class ValidationRun(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        RUNNING = "RUNNING", "Running"
        PASSED = "PASSED", "Passed"
        FAILED_CHECKS = "FAILED_CHECKS", "Failed checks"
        ERROR = "ERROR", "Error"

    validation_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("validation_run")])
    scheduler_run = models.ForeignKey(
        "scheduling.SchedulerRun", on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="validation_runs"
    )
    status = models.CharField(max_length=14, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.RUNNING)
    checks_performed = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    failures = models.PositiveIntegerField(default=0)
    started_at = models.DateTimeField(default=timezone.now)
    finished_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["validation_id"]
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(finished_at__isnull=True) | Q(finished_at__gte=F("started_at")),
                name="valrun_finish_after_start",
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.validation_id


class DetectedViolation(models.Model):
    class Code(models.TextChoices):
        STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING = "STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING", "Student double booking"
        ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED = "ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED", "Room capacity exceeded"
        MISSING_EQUIPMENT = "MISSING_EQUIPMENT", "Missing equipment"
        ROOM_DOUBLE_BOOKED = "ROOM_DOUBLE_BOOKED", "Room double booked"
        INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED = "INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED", "Invigilator double booked"
        INVIGILATOR_UNAVAILABLE = "INVIGILATOR_UNAVAILABLE", "Invigilator unavailable"
        ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION = "ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION", "Accommodation violation"
        ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION = "ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION", "Room unavailable"
        STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH = "STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH", "Student allocation mismatch"
        INVALID_DURATION = "INVALID_DURATION", "Invalid duration"

    violation_id = models.CharField(primary_key=True, max_length=10, validators=[id_validator("violation")])
    validation_run = models.ForeignKey(ValidationRun, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="violations")
    violation_code = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=Code.choices)
    assignment = models.ForeignKey(
        "scheduling.ScheduleAssignment", null=True, blank=True, on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="violations"
    )
    entity_id = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    detail = models.TextField(blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["violation_id"]
        indexes = [models.Index(fields=["validation_run", "violation_code"])]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.violation_id

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.scheduling.models import SchedulerRun
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation
from apps.validation.services.validation_runner import run_all_checks


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Validate an ANOMALY_FIXTURE run and summarize detected violations."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--run", default=None)

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        run_id = options["run"]
        if run_id:
            try:
                run = SchedulerRun.objects.get(pk=run_id)
            except SchedulerRun.DoesNotExist as error:
                raise CommandError(f"Scheduler run {run_id} was not found.") from error
        else:
            run = (
                SchedulerRun.objects.filter(
                    run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE
                )
                .order_by("-run_id")
                .first()
            )
            if run is None:
                raise CommandError(
                    "No ANOMALY_FIXTURE run exists; run inject_anomalies first."
                )
        if run.run_type != SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE:
            raise CommandError(
                f"{run.run_id} is not an ANOMALY_FIXTURE run."
            )

        try:
            validation_run, result = run_all_checks(run)
        except ValueError as error:
            raise CommandError(str(error)) from error

        counts = {}
        for code in (
            DetectedViolation.objects.filter(
                validation_run=validation_run
            ).values_list("violation_code", flat=True)
        ):
            counts[code] = counts.get(code, 0) + 1
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"{validation_run.validation_id}: validated {run.run_id}"
            )
        )
        for code, count in sorted(counts.items()):
            self.stdout.write(f"  {code}: {count}")
        self.stdout.write(f"  total: {result['failures']} violations")
