from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.scheduling.models import SchedulerRun
from apps.validation.models import ValidationRun
from apps.validation.services.evaluator import (
    evaluate,
    write_evaluation_report,
)


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Evaluate anomaly detection precision, recall, and F1."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--validation", default=None)
        parser.add_argument("--run", default=None)

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        if options["validation"]:
            try:
                validation_run = ValidationRun.objects.select_related(
                    "scheduler_run"
                ).get(pk=options["validation"])
            except ValidationRun.DoesNotExist as error:
                raise CommandError(
                    f"Validation run {options['validation']} was not found."
                ) from error
        elif options["run"]:
            validation_run = (
                ValidationRun.objects.filter(
                    scheduler_run_id=options["run"]
                )
                .select_related("scheduler_run")
                .order_by("-validation_id")
                .first()
            )
            if validation_run is None:
                raise CommandError(
                    f"No validation run exists for {options['run']}."
                )
        else:
            validation_run = (
                ValidationRun.objects.filter(
                    scheduler_run__run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE
                )
                .select_related("scheduler_run")
                .order_by("-validation_id")
                .first()
            )
            if validation_run is None:
                raise CommandError(
                    "No anomaly validation exists; run detect_violations first."
                )

        if (
            validation_run.scheduler_run.run_type
            != SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE
        ):
            raise CommandError("Evaluation requires an ANOMALY_FIXTURE run.")
        try:
            result = evaluate(validation_run)
        except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as error:
            raise CommandError(str(error)) from error
        report_path = write_evaluation_report(result)
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"{result['validation_id']} on {result['scheduler_run']}: "
                f"TP={result['tp']} FP={result['fp']} FN={result['fn']} "
                f"precision={result['precision']} recall={result['recall']} "
                f"F1={result['f1']}"
            )
        )
        self.stdout.write(f"  report: {report_path}")

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError
from django.db import DatabaseError

from apps.scheduling.models import SchedulerRun
from apps.validation.services.anomaly_injector import inject


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Create a separate anomaly fixture from a completed baseline run."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument(
            "--baseline",
            default=None,
            help="Completed baseline run ID; defaults to the latest baseline.",
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        if options["baseline"]:
            baseline_id = options["baseline"]
        else:
            baseline = (
                SchedulerRun.objects.filter(
                    run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.BASELINE,
                    status=SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED,
                )
                .order_by("-run_id")
                .first()
            )
            if baseline is None:
                raise CommandError(
                    "No completed baseline exists; run run_scheduler first."
                )
            baseline_id = baseline.run_id

        try:
            anomaly_run, labels, selected = inject(baseline_id)
        except (ValueError, OSError, DatabaseError) as error:
            raise CommandError(f"Anomaly injection failed: {error}") from error

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Created {anomaly_run.run_id} (ANOMALY_FIXTURE) "
                f"from {baseline_id}."
            )
        )
        self.stdout.write(
            "  exams: "
            + ", ".join(
                f"{key}={exam_id}" for key, exam_id in selected.items()
            )
        )
        self.stdout.write(f"  ground-truth rows: {len(labels)}")
        for label in labels:
            self.stdout.write(
                f"  {label['anomaly_id']} | {label['issue_type']} | "
                f"{label['expected_violation_code']} | "
                f"{label['assignment_id']}"
            )

import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.validation.services.data_validator import validate_master_data


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Validate generated master data and write a JSON summary."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        summary = validate_master_data()
        output = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "validation_summary.json"
        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output.write_text(
            json.dumps(summary, indent=2, default=str) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        if not summary["ok"]:
            self.stderr.write(
                self.style.ERROR(
                    f"FAIL - {summary['failures']} validation failures:"
                )
            )
            for check in summary["checks"]:
                if check["failures"]:
                    self.stderr.write(
                        f"  {check['check']}: {check['failures']}"
                    )
            raise CommandError(
                f"Data validation failed; see {output} for details."
            )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"OK - 0 failures across {len(summary['checks'])} checks."
            )
        )

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.scheduling.models import SchedulerRun
from apps.validation.services.validation_runner import run_all_checks


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Independently validate a completed SchedulerRun."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument(
            "--run",
            default=None,
            help="Scheduler run ID; defaults to the latest run.",
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        run_id = options["run"]
        if run_id:
            try:
                run = SchedulerRun.objects.get(pk=run_id)
            except SchedulerRun.DoesNotExist as error:
                raise CommandError(f"Scheduler run {run_id} was not found.") from error
        else:
            run = SchedulerRun.objects.order_by("-run_id").first()
            if run is None:
                raise CommandError(
                    "No scheduler run exists; run run_scheduler first."
                )

        try:
            validation_run, result = run_all_checks(run)
        except ValueError as error:
            raise CommandError(str(error)) from error

        if result["failures"]:
            for check in result["checks"]:
                if check["failures"]:
                    self.stderr.write(
                        f"  {check['check']}: {check['failures']} failure(s)"
                    )
            raise CommandError(
                f"{validation_run.validation_id}: FAIL — "
                f"{result['checks_performed']} checks, "
                f"{result['failures']} violations."
            )
        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"{validation_run.validation_id}: PASS — "
                f"{result['checks_performed']} checks, 0 violations."
            )
        )
        self.stdout.write(
            f"  Scorecard: data/validation_scorecard.json"
        )
        self.stdout.write(
            f"  Business metrics: {result['business_metrics']}"
        )

FILE: apps\validation\management\commands\verify_reproducibility.py

import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.scheduling.models import ScheduleEvent
from apps.validation.services.reproducibility import verify_reproducibility
from generator.config import SCALES, SEED_DEFAULT


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = (
        "Regenerate twice with a fixed seed and compare checksums. "
        "Database changes are rolled back."
    )

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=SEED_DEFAULT)
        parser.add_argument(
            "--scale", choices=sorted(SCALES), default="starter"
        )

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        if ScheduleEvent.objects.exists():
            raise CommandError(
                "ScheduleEvent rows exist; reproducibility verification "
                "requires a fresh database."
            )

        result = verify_reproducibility(
            seed=options["seed"],
            scale=options["scale"],
        )
        output = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "reproducibility_report.json"
        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output.write_text(
            json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

        if not result["match"]:
            raise CommandError(
                f"Generator is not reproducible; see {output} for details."
            )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Reproducible: {result['checksum_run_1']}"
            )
        )

======================================================================

"""Create a separate run containing five controlled scheduling anomalies."""

import csv
import datetime as dt
import itertools
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from itertools import islice
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
)
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_assignment_id, make_id
from apps.core.services.lifecycle import transition
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    RoomClosure,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
)
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation

CODE = DetectedViolation.Code


def _next_id(model, field_name, kind):
    last_id = model.objects.order_by(f"-{field_name}").values_list(
        field_name, flat=True
    ).first()
    number = int(last_id.split("-")[-1]) + 1 if last_id else 1
    return make_id(kind, number)


class _CopyContext:
    def __init__(self, run):
        self.run = run
        self.sra_number = _last_number(
            StudentRoomAllocation, "allocation_id"
        )
        self.ias_number = _last_number(
            InvigilatorAssignment, "ias_id"
        )
        self.event_number = _last_number(ScheduleEvent, "event_id")
        self.closure_number = _last_number(RoomClosure, "closure_id")
        self.assignments = {}

    def next_sra(self):
        self.sra_number += 1
        return make_id("student_allocation", self.sra_number)

    def next_ias(self):
        self.ias_number += 1
        return make_id("invigilator_assignment", self.ias_number)

    def next_event(self):
        self.event_number += 1
        return make_id("event", self.event_number)

    def next_closure(self):
        self.closure_number += 1
        return make_id("closure", self.closure_number)


def _last_number(model, field_name):
    last_id = model.objects.order_by(f"-{field_name}").values_list(
        field_name, flat=True
    ).first()
    return int(last_id.split("-")[-1]) if last_id else 0


def _effective_capacity(room_id, on_date, capacities):
    return max(
        (
            capacity
            for start, end, capacity in capacities.get(room_id, ())
            if start <= on_date and (end is None or end >= on_date)
        ),
        default=0,
    )


def _covered(intervals, start_at, end_at):
    return any(
        available_start <= start_at and available_end >= end_at
        for available_start, available_end in intervals
    )


def _overlap(first_start, first_end, second_start, second_end):
    return first_start < second_end and second_start < first_end


def _copy_assignment(ctx, baseline_assignment):
    assignment = ScheduleAssignment.objects.create(
        assignment_id=make_assignment_id(
            baseline_assignment.exam_id,
            baseline_assignment.room_id,
            ctx.run.run_id,
        ),
        run=ctx.run,
        exam_id=baseline_assignment.exam_id,
        room_id=baseline_assignment.room_id,
        start_at=baseline_assignment.start_at,
        end_at=baseline_assignment.end_at,
        allocated_count=baseline_assignment.allocated_count,
        status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
    )
    for student_id, required_minutes in (
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(
            assignment=baseline_assignment
        ).values_list("student_id", "required_minutes")
    ):
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.create(
            allocation_id=ctx.next_sra(),
            assignment=assignment,
            run=ctx.run,
            exam_id=baseline_assignment.exam_id,
            student_id=student_id,
            required_minutes=required_minutes,
        )
    for invigilator_id, role in (
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment=baseline_assignment
        ).values_list("invigilator_id", "role")
    ):
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.create(
            ias_id=ctx.next_ias(),
            assignment=assignment,
            invigilator_id=invigilator_id,
            role=role,
        )
    ctx.assignments[baseline_assignment.assignment_id] = assignment
    return assignment


def _replace_assignment_room(ctx, assignment, target_room_id):
    students = list(
        assignment.student_allocations.values_list(
            "student_id", "required_minutes"
        )
    )
    invigilators = list(
        assignment.invigilator_assignments.values_list(
            "invigilator_id", "role"
        )
    )
    old_id = assignment.assignment_id
    assignment.delete()
    replacement = ScheduleAssignment.objects.create(
        assignment_id=make_assignment_id(
            assignment.exam_id, target_room_id, ctx.run.run_id
        ),
        run=ctx.run,
        exam_id=assignment.exam_id,
        room_id=target_room_id,
        start_at=assignment.start_at,
        end_at=assignment.end_at,
        allocated_count=assignment.allocated_count,
        status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
    )
    for student_id, required_minutes in students:
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.create(
            allocation_id=ctx.next_sra(),
            assignment=replacement,
            run=ctx.run,
            exam_id=assignment.exam_id,
            student_id=student_id,
            required_minutes=required_minutes,
        )
    for invigilator_id, role in invigilators:
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.create(
            ias_id=ctx.next_ias(),
            assignment=replacement,
            invigilator_id=invigilator_id,
            role=role,
        )
    ctx.assignments[old_id] = replacement
    return replacement


def _replace_invigilators(ctx, assignment, invigilator_ids):
    InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(assignment=assignment).delete()
    for index, invigilator_id in enumerate(invigilator_ids):
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.create(
            ias_id=ctx.next_ias(),
            assignment=assignment,
            invigilator_id=invigilator_id,
            role=(
                InvigilatorAssignment.Role.CHIEF
                if index == 0
                else InvigilatorAssignment.Role.ASSISTANT
            ),
        )


def _write_ground_truth(rows, run):
    data_dir = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data"
    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fields = [
        "anomaly_id",
        "issue_type",
        "assignment_id",
        "affected_entity_id",
        "expected_violation_code",
        "run_id",
    ]
    enriched = [{**row, "run_id": run.run_id} for row in rows]
    csv_path = data_dir / "ground_truth.csv"
    json_path = data_dir / "ground_truth.json"
    csv_temp = csv_path.with_suffix(".csv.tmp")
    json_temp = json_path.with_suffix(".json.tmp")
    with csv_temp.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(file, fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(enriched)
    json_temp.write_text(
        json.dumps(enriched, indent=2) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    csv_temp.replace(csv_path)
    json_temp.replace(json_path)


def _anomaly_row(anomaly_id, issue_type, assignment_id, entity_id, code):
    return {
        "anomaly_id": anomaly_id,
        "issue_type": issue_type,
        "assignment_id": assignment_id,
        "affected_entity_id": entity_id,
        "expected_violation_code": code,
    }


def inject(baseline_run_id):
    """Clone selected baseline assignments and add five labeled anomalies."""
    with transaction.atomic():
        try:
            baseline = SchedulerRun.objects.get(pk=baseline_run_id)
        except SchedulerRun.DoesNotExist as error:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Baseline scheduler run {baseline_run_id} was not found."
            ) from error
        if baseline.run_type != SchedulerRun.RunType.BASELINE:
            raise ValueError(f"{baseline_run_id} is not a BASELINE run.")
        if baseline.status != SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED:
            raise ValueError(f"{baseline_run_id} is not COMPLETED.")

        selection = _select_fixture(baseline)
        run = SchedulerRun.objects.create(
            run_id=_next_id(SchedulerRun, "run_id", "run"),
            run_type=SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE,
            status=SchedulerRun.Status.RUNNING,
            seed=baseline.seed,
            algorithm_version="phase-5-anomaly-fixture-1",
            notes=f"Anomaly fixture copied from {baseline.run_id}.",
        )
        ctx = _CopyContext(run)
        exam_ids = {
            selection[key].exam_id
            for key in ("A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G")
        }
        source_assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
                run=baseline,
                status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
                exam_id__in=exam_ids,
            ).order_by("exam_id", "assignment_id")
        )
        for source in source_assignments:
            _copy_assignment(ctx, source)

        a = ctx.assignments[selection["A"].assignment_id]
        b = ctx.assignments[selection["B"].assignment_id]
        b.start_at = a.start_at
        b.end_at = a.end_at
        b.save(update_fields=["start_at", "end_at"])
        _replace_invigilators(
            ctx, b, selection["B_invigilators"]
        )

        c = _replace_assignment_room(
            ctx,
            ctx.assignments[selection["C"].assignment_id],
            selection["C_room"].room_id,
        )

        d = ctx.assignments[selection["D"].assignment_id]
        e = ctx.assignments[selection["E"].assignment_id]
        e.start_at = d.start_at
        e.end_at = d.end_at
        e.save(update_fields=["start_at", "end_at"])
        _replace_invigilators(
            ctx, e, selection["E_invigilators"]
        )

        f = ctx.assignments[selection["F"].assignment_id]
        f.end_at = f.start_at + dt.timedelta(
            minutes=f.exam.duration_minutes
        )
        f.save(update_fields=["end_at"])

        g = ctx.assignments[selection["G"].assignment_id]
        RoomClosure.objects.create(
            closure_id=ctx.next_closure(),
            room_id=g.room_id,
            run=run,
            start_at=g.start_at - dt.timedelta(minutes=10),
            end_at=g.start_at + dt.timedelta(minutes=10),
            reason=RoomClosure.Reason.MAINTENANCE,
        )

        selected_assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=run).order_by(
                "exam_id", "assignment_id"
            )
        )
        for exam_id in sorted(exam_ids):
            exam_assignments = [
                assignment
                for assignment in selected_assignments
                if assignment.exam_id == exam_id
            ]
            ScheduleEvent.objects.create(
                event_id=ctx.next_event(),
                run=run,
                exam_id=exam_id,
                assignment=(
                    exam_assignments[0]
                    if len(exam_assignments) == 1
                    else None
                ),
                event_type=ScheduleEvent.EventType.ASSIGNED,
                detail={
                    "fixture": True,
                    "assignment_ids": [
                        assignment.assignment_id
                        for assignment in exam_assignments
                    ],
                },
            )

        shared_student = selection["shared_student"]
        shared_invigilator = selection["D_invigilator"]
        rows = [
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-001",
                "STUDENT_OVERLAP",
                a.assignment_id,
                shared_student,
                CODE.STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-001",
                "STUDENT_OVERLAP",
                b.assignment_id,
                shared_student,
                CODE.STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-002",
                "CAPACITY_EXCEEDED",
                c.assignment_id,
                c.room_id,
                CODE.ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-003",
                "INVIGILATOR_CLASH",
                d.assignment_id,
                shared_invigilator,
                CODE.INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-003",
                "INVIGILATOR_CLASH",
                e.assignment_id,
                shared_invigilator,
                CODE.INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-004",
                "ACCOMMODATION_BREACH",
                f.assignment_id,
                selection["F_student"],
                CODE.ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION.value,
            ),
            _anomaly_row(
                "EDGE-005",
                "ROOM_CLOSED",
                g.assignment_id,
                g.room_id,
                CODE.ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION.value,
            ),
        ]
        transition(run, SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED)
        run.finished_at = timezone.now()
        run.save(update_fields=["finished_at"])

    _write_ground_truth(rows, run)
    return run, rows, {
        key: selection[key].exam_id
        for key in ("A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G")
    }


def _select_fixture(baseline):
    assignments = list(
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            run=baseline,
            status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
        )
        .select_related("exam", "room", "exam__requirement")
        .order_by("exam_id", "assignment_id")
    )
    if len({assignment.exam_id for assignment in assignments}) < 7:
        raise ValueError(
            "At least seven assigned exams are required to build the fixture."
        )

    assignment_students = defaultdict(set)
    assignment_required = defaultdict(dict)
    for assignment_id, student_id, required_minutes in (
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=baseline).values_list(
            "assignment_id", "student_id", "required_minutes"
        )
    ):
        assignment_students[assignment_id].add(student_id)
        assignment_required[assignment_id][student_id] = required_minutes
    assignment_invigilators = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment_id, invigilator_id in (
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment__run=baseline
        ).values_list("assignment_id", "invigilator_id")
    ):
        assignment_invigilators[assignment_id].append(invigilator_id)
    assignments_by_exam = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment in assignments:
        assignments_by_exam[assignment.exam_id].append(assignment)

    equipment_by_exam = defaultdict(set)
    for exam_id, equipment_id in (
        ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
            requirement__exam_id__in=assignments_by_exam
        ).values_list("requirement__exam_id", "equipment_id")
    ):
        equipment_by_exam[exam_id].add(equipment_id)
    capacities = defaultdict(list)
    for room_id, start, end, capacity in RoomCapacity.objects.values_list(
        "room_id", "effective_from", "effective_to", "exam_capacity"
    ):
        capacities[room_id].append((start, end, capacity))
    rooms = {
        room.room_id: room
        for room in Room.objects.filter(status=Room.Status.ACTIVE).prefetch_related(
            "equipment"
        )
    }
    room_equipment = {
        room_id: {equipment.code for equipment in room.equipment.all()}
        for room_id, room in rooms.items()
    }
    room_availability = defaultdict(list)
    invigilator_availability = defaultdict(list)
    for room_id, invigilator_id, start_at, end_at in Availability.objects.values_list(
        "room_id", "invigilator_id", "start_at", "end_at"
    ):
        if room_id:
            room_availability[room_id].append((start_at, end_at))
        if invigilator_id:
            invigilator_availability[invigilator_id].append(
                (start_at, end_at)
            )
    closures = defaultdict(list)
    for room_id, start_at, end_at in RoomClosure.objects.filter(
        run__isnull=True
    ).values_list(
        "room_id", "start_at", "end_at"
    ):
        closures[room_id].append((start_at, end_at))
    active_invigilators = list(
        Invigilator.objects.filter(status=Invigilator.Status.ACTIVE)
        .order_by("invigilator_id")
        .values_list("invigilator_id", flat=True)
    )
    accommodations_by_student = defaultdict(list)
    for accommodation in Accommodation.objects.filter(
        status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE
    ):
        accommodations_by_student[accommodation.student_id].append(
            accommodation
        )

    def accommodations_for(student_id, on_date):
        return [
            accommodation
            for accommodation in accommodations_by_student[student_id]
            if accommodation.effective_from <= on_date
            and (
                accommodation.effective_to is None
                or accommodation.effective_to >= on_date
            )
        ]

    def required_for(assignment_id):
        return max(
            assignment_required[assignment_id].values(),
            default=0,
        )

    def staff_count(assignment):
        requirement = getattr(assignment.exam, "requirement", None)
        ratio = requirement.invigilator_ratio if requirement else 50
        return max(1, (assignment.allocated_count + ratio - 1) // ratio)

    overlap_candidates = []
    for first in assignments:
        first_exam_students = set().union(
            *(
                assignment_students[row.assignment_id]
                for row in assignments_by_exam[first.exam_id]
            )
        )
        for second in assignments:
            if (
                first.exam_id == second.exam_id
                or first.room_id == second.room_id
            ):
                continue
            shared = assignment_students[first.assignment_id] & assignment_students[
                second.assignment_id
            ]
            if not shared:
                continue
            other_first_rooms = first_exam_students - assignment_students[
                first.assignment_id
            ]
            if assignment_students[second.assignment_id] & other_first_rooms:
                continue
            if required_for(second.assignment_id) > (
                first.end_at - first.start_at
            ).total_seconds() / 60:
                continue
            if not _covered(
                room_availability[second.room_id],
                first.start_at,
                first.end_at,
            ):
                continue
            if any(
                _overlap(
                    first.start_at,
                    first.end_at,
                    closure_start,
                    closure_end,
                )
                for closure_start, closure_end in closures[second.room_id]
            ):
                continue
            overlap_candidates.append((first, second, min(shared)))
    overlap_candidates.sort(
        key=lambda item: (
            len(
                assignment_students[item[0].assignment_id]
                & assignment_students[item[1].assignment_id]
            ),
            item[0].assignment_id,
            item[1].assignment_id,
        )
    )

    capacity_candidates = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        needed = equipment_by_exam[assignment.exam_id]
        accessible_students = {
            student_id
            for student_id in assignment_students[assignment.assignment_id]
            if any(
                accommodation.accommodation_type
                == Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM
                for accommodation in accommodations_for(
                    student_id, assignment.start_at.date()
                )
            )
        }
        for room_id, room in rooms.items():
            if room_id in {
                row.room_id
                for row in assignments_by_exam[assignment.exam_id]
            }:
                continue
            capacity = _effective_capacity(
                room_id, assignment.start_at.date(), capacities
            )
            if (
                capacity <= 0
                or capacity >= assignment.allocated_count
                or not needed.issubset(room_equipment[room_id])
                or (accessible_students and not room.is_accessible)
                or not _covered(
                    room_availability[room_id],
                    assignment.start_at,
                    assignment.end_at,
                )
                or any(
                    _overlap(
                        assignment.start_at,
                        assignment.end_at,
                        closure_start,
                        closure_end,
                    )
                    for closure_start, closure_end in closures[room_id]
                )
            ):
                continue
            capacity_candidates.append((assignment, room))

    extra_time_candidates = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        if assignment.exam.duration_minutes >= (
            assignment.end_at - assignment.start_at
        ).total_seconds() / 60:
            continue
        for student_id in assignment_students[assignment.assignment_id]:
            if assignment_required[assignment.assignment_id].get(
                student_id, 0
            ) <= assignment.exam.duration_minutes:
                continue
            if any(
                accommodation.accommodation_type
                == Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME
                for accommodation in accommodations_for(
                    student_id, assignment.start_at.date()
                )
            ):
                extra_time_candidates.append((assignment, student_id))
    if not overlap_candidates or not capacity_candidates or not extra_time_candidates:
        raise ValueError(
            "Baseline does not contain suitable student-overlap, capacity, "
            "and extra-time candidates for the anomaly fixture."
        )

    invigilator_sets = {
        assignment.assignment_id: set(
            assignment_invigilators[assignment.assignment_id]
        )
        for assignment in assignments
    }
    chosen = None
    for a, b, shared_student in overlap_candidates:
        b_staff_needed = max(
            staff_count(b), len(assignment_invigilators[b.assignment_id])
        )
        b_staff_pool = [
            invigilator_id
            for invigilator_id in active_invigilators
            if invigilator_id not in invigilator_sets[a.assignment_id]
            and _covered(
                invigilator_availability[invigilator_id],
                a.start_at,
                a.end_at,
            )
        ]
        b_staff_candidates = list(
            islice(
                itertools.combinations(b_staff_pool, b_staff_needed),
                16,
            )
        )
        if not b_staff_candidates:
            continue
        for c, c_room in capacity_candidates:
            used = {a.exam_id, b.exam_id, c.exam_id}
            if len(used) != 3:
                continue
            for d in assignments:
                if d.exam_id in used:
                    continue
                d_invigilators = assignment_invigilators[d.assignment_id]
                for e in assignments:
                    if (
                        e.exam_id in used
                        or e.exam_id == d.exam_id
                        or e.room_id == d.room_id
                        or not d_invigilators
                    ):
                        continue
                    if assignment_students[d.assignment_id] & assignment_students[
                        e.assignment_id
                    ]:
                        continue
                    if required_for(e.assignment_id) > (
                        d.end_at - d.start_at
                    ).total_seconds() / 60:
                        continue
                    if not _covered(
                        room_availability[e.room_id], d.start_at, d.end_at
                    ) or any(
                        _overlap(
                            d.start_at,
                            d.end_at,
                            closure_start,
                            closure_end,
                        )
                        for closure_start, closure_end in closures[e.room_id]
                    ):
                        continue
                    e_staff_needed = max(
                        staff_count(e),
                        len(assignment_invigilators[e.assignment_id]),
                        1,
                    )
                    d_staff_set = set().union(
                        *(
                            invigilator_sets[row.assignment_id]
                            for row in assignments_by_exam[d.exam_id]
                        )
                    )
                    e_staff_pool = [
                        invigilator_id
                        for invigilator_id in active_invigilators
                        if invigilator_id not in d_staff_set
                        and invigilator_id not in {d_invigilators[0]}
                        and _covered(
                            invigilator_availability[invigilator_id],
                            d.start_at,
                            d.end_at,
                        )
                    ]
                    e_staff_candidates = [
                        (d_invigilators[0], *other_staff)
                        for other_staff in islice(
                            itertools.combinations(
                                e_staff_pool, e_staff_needed - 1
                            ),
                            16,
                        )
                    ]
                    if not e_staff_candidates:
                        continue
                    for f, f_student in extra_time_candidates:
                        used_with_f = used | {d.exam_id, e.exam_id, f.exam_id}
                        if len(used_with_f) != 6:
                            continue
                        for g in assignments:
                            if g.exam_id in used_with_f:
                                continue
                            selection = {
                                "A": a,
                                "B": b,
                                "C": c,
                                "C_room": c_room,
                                "D": d,
                                "E": e,
                                "F": f,
                                "F_student": f_student,
                                "G": g,
                                "shared_student": shared_student,
                                "D_invigilator": d_invigilators[0],
                            }
                            for b_staff in b_staff_candidates:
                                for e_staff in e_staff_candidates:
                                    selection["B_invigilators"] = b_staff
                                    selection["E_invigilators"] = e_staff
                                    if _fixture_is_valid(
                                        selection,
                                        assignments_by_exam,
                                        assignment_students,
                                        assignment_invigilators,
                                        assignment_required,
                                        invigilator_availability,
                                        room_availability,
                                        closures,
                                        capacities,
                                        rooms,
                                        room_equipment,
                                        equipment_by_exam,
                                        accommodations_for,
                                    ):
                                        chosen = selection.copy()
                                        break
                                if chosen:
                                    break
                            if chosen:
                                break
                        if chosen:
                            break
                    if chosen:
                        break
                if chosen:
                    break
            if chosen:
                break
        if chosen:
            break
    if chosen is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "Could not select seven compatible exams for the anomaly fixture."
        )
    return chosen


def _fixture_is_valid(
    selection,
    assignments_by_exam,
    assignment_students,
    assignment_invigilators,
    assignment_required,
    invigilator_availability,
    room_availability,
    closures,
    capacities,
    rooms,
    room_equipment,
    equipment_by_exam,
    accommodations_for,
):
    selected_exam_ids = {
        selection[key].exam_id for key in ("A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G")
    }
    records = []
    for exam_id in selected_exam_ids:
        for assignment in assignments_by_exam[exam_id]:
            assignment_id = assignment.assignment_id
            record = {
                "source": assignment,
                "key": assignment_id,
                "exam_id": exam_id,
                "room_id": assignment.room_id,
                "start_at": assignment.start_at,
                "end_at": assignment.end_at,
                "students": assignment_students[assignment_id],
                "invigilators": set(assignment_invigilators[assignment_id]),
            }
            if assignment_id == selection["B"].assignment_id:
                record["start_at"] = selection["A"].start_at
                record["end_at"] = selection["A"].end_at
                record["invigilators"] = set(
                    selection["B_invigilators"]
                )
            if assignment_id == selection["C"].assignment_id:
                record["room_id"] = selection["C_room"].room_id
            if assignment_id == selection["E"].assignment_id:
                record["start_at"] = selection["D"].start_at
                record["end_at"] = selection["D"].end_at
                record["invigilators"] = set(
                    selection["E_invigilators"]
                )
            if assignment_id == selection["F"].assignment_id:
                record["end_at"] = record["start_at"] + dt.timedelta(
                    minutes=assignment.exam.duration_minutes
                )
            records.append(record)

    expected_student_pair = {
        selection["A"].assignment_id,
        selection["B"].assignment_id,
    }
    expected_invigilator_pair = {
        selection["D"].assignment_id,
        selection["E"].assignment_id,
    }
    for record in records:
        assignment = record["source"]
        interval_minutes = (
            record["end_at"] - record["start_at"]
        ).total_seconds() / 60
        room = rooms.get(record["room_id"])
        if room is None:
            return False
        if not _covered(
            room_availability[record["room_id"]],
            record["start_at"],
            record["end_at"],
        ):
            return False
        if any(
            _overlap(
                record["start_at"],
                record["end_at"],
                closure_start,
                closure_end,
            )
            for closure_start, closure_end in closures[record["room_id"]]
        ):
            return False
        capacity = _effective_capacity(
            record["room_id"], record["start_at"].date(), capacities
        )
        if (
            record["key"] != selection["C"].assignment_id
            and assignment.allocated_count > capacity
        ):
            return False
        if not equipment_by_exam[record["exam_id"]].issubset(
            room_equipment[record["room_id"]]
        ):
            return False
        accommodations = {
            student_id: accommodations_for(
                student_id, record["start_at"].date()
            )
            for student_id in record["students"]
        }
        separate_students = {
            student_id
            for student_id, student_accommodations in accommodations.items()
            if any(
                item.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM
                for item in student_accommodations
            )
        }
        if separate_students and len(separate_students) != len(
            record["students"]
        ):
            return False
        if any(
            item.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM
            for student_accommodations in accommodations.values()
            for item in student_accommodations
        ) and not room.is_accessible:
            return False
        if (
            record["key"] != selection["F"].assignment_id
            and any(
                assignment_required[record["key"]].get(student_id, 0)
                > interval_minutes
                for student_id in record["students"]
            )
        ):
            return False
        if interval_minutes < assignment.exam.duration_minutes:
            return False
        for invigilator_id in record["invigilators"]:
            if not _covered(
                invigilator_availability[invigilator_id],
                record["start_at"],
                record["end_at"],
            ):
                return False

    for index, first in enumerate(records):
        for second in records[index + 1:]:
            if first["exam_id"] == second["exam_id"]:
                continue
            if not _overlap(
                first["start_at"],
                first["end_at"],
                second["start_at"],
                second["end_at"],
            ):
                continue
            pair = {first["key"], second["key"]}
            if first["room_id"] == second["room_id"]:
                return False
            if (
                first["students"] & second["students"]
                and pair != expected_student_pair
            ):
                return False
            if (
                first["invigilators"] & second["invigilators"]
                and pair != expected_invigilator_pair
            ):
                return False
    return True

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\data_validator.py
======================================================================
"""Independent data-quality checks over persisted generated records."""

import re

from django.db.models import Count, F, Q, Sum

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Equipment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.core.services.id_generator import is_valid
from apps.scheduling.models import Availability, RoomClosure, TimeSlot
from generator.config import TERM


_ID_FIELDS = (
    (Student, "student"),
    (Course, "course"),
    (Enrollment, "enrollment"),
    (Exam, "exam"),
    (ExamRequirement, "requirement"),
    (Room, "room"),
    (RoomCapacity, "capacity"),
    (Invigilator, "invigilator"),
    (Accommodation, "accommodation"),
    (Availability, "availability"),
    (RoomClosure, "closure"),
    (TimeSlot, "slot"),
)


def _check(checks, name, failures):
    checks.append({"check": name, "failures": int(failures)})


def _invalid_choice_count(model, field_name, allowed_values):
    return model.objects.exclude(
        **{f"{field_name}__in": allowed_values}
    ).count()


def _invalid_ids(model, kind):
    field_name = model._meta.pk.name
    return sum(
        not is_valid(kind, value)
        for value in model.objects.values_list(field_name, flat=True)
    )


def _duplicate_group_count(model, fields):
    primary_key = model._meta.pk.name
    return (
        model.objects.values(*fields)
        .annotate(row_count=Count(primary_key))
        .filter(row_count__gt=1)
        .count()
    )


def _orphan_foreign_key_count(model, field_name, parent_model, parent_field=None):
    parent_field = parent_field or parent_model._meta.pk.name
    valid_parent_ids = parent_model.objects.values_list(parent_field, flat=True)
    return model.objects.exclude(
        **{f"{field_name}__in": valid_parent_ids}
    ).count()


def _overlapping_capacity_ranges():
    ranges = {}
    for room_id, start, end in RoomCapacity.objects.order_by(
        "room_id", "effective_from"
    ).values_list("room_id", "effective_from", "effective_to"):
        ranges.setdefault(room_id, []).append((start, end))

    overlaps = 0
    for room_ranges in ranges.values():
        latest_end = None
        has_open_end = False
        for start, end in room_ranges:
            if has_open_end or (latest_end is not None and start <= latest_end):
                overlaps += 1
            if end is None:
                has_open_end = True
            elif latest_end is None or end > latest_end:
                latest_end = end
    return overlaps


def validate_master_data():
    checks = []
    counts = {
        "students": Student.objects.count(),
        "courses": Course.objects.count(),
        "exams": Exam.objects.count(),
        "rooms": Room.objects.count(),
        "invigilators": Invigilator.objects.count(),
        "enrollments": Enrollment.objects.count(),
        "active_enrollments": Enrollment.objects.filter(
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE, term=TERM
        ).count(),
        "accommodations": Accommodation.objects.count(),
        "room_capacities": RoomCapacity.objects.count(),
        "availability": Availability.objects.count(),
        "room_closures": RoomClosure.objects.count(),
        "timeslots": TimeSlot.objects.count(),
        "equipment": Equipment.objects.count(),
        "room_equipment": RoomEquipment.objects.count(),
        "exam_requirement_equipment": ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.count(),
    }

    orphan_foreign_keys = (
        _orphan_foreign_key_count(Enrollment, "student_id", Student)
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(Enrollment, "course_id", Course)
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(Exam, "course_id", Course)
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            ExamRequirement, "exam_id", Exam
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            RoomCapacity, "room_id", Room
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            RoomEquipment, "room_id", Room
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            RoomEquipment, "equipment_id", Equipment, "code"
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            ExamRequirementEquipment,
            "requirement_id",
            ExamRequirement,
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            ExamRequirementEquipment, "equipment_id", Equipment, "code"
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            Accommodation, "student_id", Student
        )
        + _orphan_foreign_key_count(
            RoomClosure, "room_id", Room
        )
        + Availability.objects.filter(room_id__isnull=False)
        .exclude(room_id__in=Room.objects.values_list("room_id", flat=True))
        .count()
        + Availability.objects.filter(invigilator_id__isnull=False)
        .exclude(
            invigilator_id__in=Invigilator.objects.values_list(
                "invigilator_id", flat=True
            )
        )
        .count()
    )
    _check(checks, "invalid_foreign_keys", orphan_foreign_keys)

    _check(
        checks,
        "exams_have_course",
        Exam.objects.filter(course__isnull=True).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "exams_have_requirement",
        Exam.objects.filter(requirement__isnull=True).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "rooms_have_capacity",
        Room.objects.filter(capacities__isnull=True).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "accommodations_have_student",
        Accommodation.objects.filter(student__isnull=True).count(),
    )

    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_enrollments",
        _duplicate_group_count(
            Enrollment, ["student_id", "course_id", "term"]
        ),
    )
    primary_key_models = (
        Student,
        Course,
        Enrollment,
        Exam,
        ExamRequirement,
        Room,
        RoomCapacity,
        Invigilator,
        Accommodation,
        Availability,
        RoomClosure,
        TimeSlot,
        Equipment,
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_primary_keys",
        sum(
            _duplicate_group_count(model, [model._meta.pk.name])
            for model in primary_key_models
        ),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_exam_per_course",
        _duplicate_group_count(Exam, ["course_id", "term"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_student_email",
        _duplicate_group_count(Student, ["email"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_invigilator_email",
        _duplicate_group_count(Invigilator, ["email"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_course_code",
        _duplicate_group_count(Course, ["course_code"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_room_building_number",
        _duplicate_group_count(Room, ["building", "room_number"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_timeslot",
        _duplicate_group_count(TimeSlot, ["slot_date", "start_time"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_room_equipment",
        _duplicate_group_count(RoomEquipment, ["room_id", "equipment_id"]),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "duplicate_requirement_equipment",
        _duplicate_group_count(
            ExamRequirementEquipment, ["requirement_id", "equipment_id"]
        ),
    )

    exam_course_ids = Exam.objects.values_list("course_id", flat=True)
    _check(
        checks,
        "enrollments_point_to_courses_with_exam",
        Enrollment.objects.exclude(course_id__in=exam_course_ids).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "active_enrollment_student_is_active",
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE
        ).exclude(student__status=Student.Status.ACTIVE).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "student_course_load_4_to_6",
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE, term=TERM
        )
        .values("student_id")
        .annotate(course_count=Count("enrollment_id"))
        .exclude(course_count__gte=4, course_count__lte=6)
        .count()
        + Student.objects.exclude(
            student_id__in=Enrollment.objects.filter(
                status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE, term=TERM
            ).values("student_id")
        ).count(),
    )

    _check(
        checks,
        "capacity_out_of_range",
        RoomCapacity.objects.exclude(
            exam_capacity__gte=10, exam_capacity__lte=500
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "room_capacity_dates_ordered",
        RoomCapacity.objects.filter(
            effective_to__lt=F("effective_from")
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "room_capacity_ranges_overlap",
        _overlapping_capacity_ranges(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "enrollment_dates_ordered",
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            effective_to__lt=F("effective_from")
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "accommodation_dates_ordered",
        Accommodation.objects.filter(
            effective_to__lt=F("effective_from")
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "availability_dates_ordered",
        Availability.objects.filter(end_at__lte=F("start_at")).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "room_closure_dates_ordered",
        RoomClosure.objects.filter(end_at__lte=F("start_at")).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "accommodation_percent_matches_type",
        Accommodation.objects.filter(
            Q(
                accommodation_type=Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME,
            )
            & ~Q(extra_time_percent__in=[25, 50, 100])
            | (
                ~Q(accommodation_type=Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME)
                & ~Q(extra_time_percent=0)
            )
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "availability_has_exactly_one_target",
        Availability.objects.filter(
            Q(room__isnull=False, invigilator__isnull=False)
            | Q(room__isnull=True, invigilator__isnull=True)
        ).count(),
    )

    _check(
        checks,
        "exam_duration_invalid",
        Exam.objects.exclude(duration_minutes__in=[60, 90, 120, 180]).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "exam_id_year_mismatch",
        sum(
            exam_id[5:9] != term[:4]
            for exam_id, term in Exam.objects.values_list("exam_id", "term")
        ),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_course_code",
        sum(
            re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z]{2,4}\d{3}", code) is None
            for code in Course.objects.values_list("course_code", flat=True)
        ),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_equipment_code",
        sum(
            re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z_]+", code) is None
            for code in Equipment.objects.values_list("code", flat=True)
        ),
    )

    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_generated_ids",
        sum(_invalid_ids(model, kind) for model, kind in _ID_FIELDS),
    )

    enum_specs = (
        (Student, "department", [value for value, _ in Student._meta.get_field("department").choices]),
        (Student, "status", [value for value, _ in Student.Status.choices]),
        (Course, "department", [value for value, _ in Course._meta.get_field("department").choices]),
        (Enrollment, "status", [value for value, _ in Enrollment.Status.choices]),
        (Exam, "status", [value for value, _ in Exam.Status.choices]),
        (Room, "room_type", [value for value, _ in Room.RoomType.choices]),
        (Room, "status", [value for value, _ in Room.Status.choices]),
        (Invigilator, "status", [value for value, _ in Invigilator.Status.choices]),
        (
            Accommodation,
            "accommodation_type",
            [value for value, _ in Accommodation.Type.choices],
        ),
        (
            Accommodation,
            "status",
            [value for value, _ in Accommodation.Status.choices],
        ),
        (RoomClosure, "reason", [value for value, _ in RoomClosure.Reason.choices]),
    )
    invalid_enums = sum(
        _invalid_choice_count(model, field_name, allowed_values)
        for model, field_name, allowed_values in enum_specs
    )
    _check(checks, "invalid_enum_values", invalid_enums)
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_exam_requirement_ratio",
        ExamRequirement.objects.exclude(
            invigilator_ratio__gte=20, invigilator_ratio__lte=100
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_course_credits",
        Course.objects.exclude(credits__gte=1, credits__lte=6).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_student_year_level",
        Student.objects.exclude(year_level__gte=1, year_level__lte=4).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_invigilator_daily_hours",
        Invigilator.objects.exclude(
            max_daily_hours__gte=1, max_daily_hours__lte=10
        ).count(),
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "invalid_term_format",
        sum(
            re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}-(FALL|SPRING)", term) is None
            for term in Enrollment.objects.values_list("term", flat=True)
        )
        + sum(
            re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}-(FALL|SPRING)", term) is None
            for term in Exam.objects.values_list("term", flat=True)
        ),
    )

    active_enrollment_rows = Enrollment.objects.filter(
        status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE, term=TERM
    ).count()
    reconciled_enrollment_rows = (
        Course.objects.filter(exams__term=TERM)
        .annotate(
            active_count=Count(
                "enrollments",
                filter=Q(
                    enrollments__status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE,
                    enrollments__term=TERM,
                ),
            )
        )
        .aggregate(total=Sum("active_count"))["total"]
        or 0
    )
    _check(
        checks,
        "active_enrollment_reconciliation",
        abs(active_enrollment_rows - reconciled_enrollment_rows),
    )

    failures = sum(check["failures"] for check in checks)
    return {
        "counts": counts,
        "checks": checks,
        "failures": failures,
        "ok": failures == 0,
    }

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\evaluator.py
======================================================================
"""Compare persisted violation detections with run-scoped ground truth."""

import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings

from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation

GROUND_TRUTH_FIELDS = {
    "anomaly_id",
    "issue_type",
    "assignment_id",
    "affected_entity_id",
    "expected_violation_code",
    "run_id",
}


def load_ground_truth(run_id=None):
    path = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "ground_truth.csv"
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Ground-truth file does not exist: {path}")
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as file:
        reader = csv.DictReader(file)
        if not reader.fieldnames or not GROUND_TRUTH_FIELDS.issubset(
            reader.fieldnames
        ):
            raise ValueError("Ground-truth CSV is missing required columns.")
        rows = list(reader)
    if run_id is not None:
        rows = [row for row in rows if row["run_id"] == run_id]
        if not rows:
            raise ValueError(
                f"No ground-truth rows are recorded for run {run_id}."
            )
    return rows


def evaluate(validation_run):
    ground_truth = load_ground_truth(
        validation_run.scheduler_run.run_id
    )
    detected = list(
        DetectedViolation.objects.filter(validation_run=validation_run)
        .exclude(assignment__isnull=True)
        .values_list("assignment_id", "violation_code")
    )
    expected_set = {
        (
            row["assignment_id"],
            row["expected_violation_code"],
        )
        for row in ground_truth
    }
    detected_set = set(detected)
    true_positives = expected_set & detected_set
    false_positives = detected_set - expected_set
    false_negatives = expected_set - detected_set

    precision = (
        len(true_positives) / (len(true_positives) + len(false_positives))
        if true_positives or false_positives
        else 0.0
    )
    recall = (
        len(true_positives) / (len(true_positives) + len(false_negatives))
        if true_positives or false_negatives
        else 0.0
    )
    f1 = (
        2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)
        if precision + recall
        else 0.0
    )

    return {
        "validation_id": validation_run.validation_id,
        "scheduler_run": validation_run.scheduler_run_id,
        "ground_truth_count": len(expected_set),
        "detected_count": len(detected_set),
        "tp": len(true_positives),
        "fp": len(false_positives),
        "fn": len(false_negatives),
        "precision": round(precision, 4),
        "recall": round(recall, 4),
        "f1": round(f1, 4),
        "true_positives": sorted(true_positives),
        "false_positives": sorted(false_positives),
        "false_negatives": sorted(false_negatives),
    }


def write_evaluation_report(result):
    output = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "anomaly_evaluation.json"
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return output

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\exporter.py
======================================================================
"""Canonical CSV, JSON, and SQLite-compatible SQL exports."""

import csv
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Equipment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import Availability, RoomClosure, TimeSlot


_MODELS = [
    ("equipment", Equipment),
    ("students", Student),
    ("courses", Course),
    ("rooms", Room),
    ("invigilators", Invigilator),
    ("timeslots", TimeSlot),
    ("room_capacities", RoomCapacity),
    ("room_equipment", RoomEquipment),
    ("availability", Availability),
    ("room_closures", RoomClosure),
    ("accommodations", Accommodation),
    ("enrollments", Enrollment),
    ("exams", Exam),
    ("exam_requirements", ExamRequirement),
    ("exam_requirement_equipment", ExamRequirementEquipment),
]


def _field_names(model):
    return [field.attname for field in model._meta.concrete_fields]


def _rows(model):
    return list(
        model.objects.order_by(model._meta.pk.name).values(*_field_names(model))
    )


def export_csv(outdir):
    outdir = Path(outdir)
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name, model in _MODELS:
        rows = _rows(model)
        with (outdir / f"{name}.csv").open(
            "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as output:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(output, fieldnames=_field_names(model))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(rows)


def export_json(outdir):
    outdir = Path(outdir)
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name, model in _MODELS:
        (outdir / f"{name}.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(_rows(model), indent=2, default=str) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )


def _quote_identifier(identifier):
    return '"' + identifier.replace('"', '""') + '"'


def _sql_value(value):
    if value is None:
        return "NULL"
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "1" if value else "0"
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return str(value)
    return "'" + str(value).replace("'", "''") + "'"


def export_sql(outdir):
    """Write dependency-ordered INSERT statements for SQLite."""
    outdir = Path(outdir)
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lines = ["-- Synthetic seed data; generated locally, no external data sources."]
    for _name, model in _MODELS:
        table_name = _quote_identifier(model._meta.db_table)
        fields = model._meta.concrete_fields
        columns = ", ".join(_quote_identifier(field.column) for field in fields)
        for row in _rows(model):
            values = ", ".join(_sql_value(row[field.attname]) for field in fields)
            lines.append(f"INSERT INTO {table_name} ({columns}) VALUES ({values});")
    (outdir / "seed.sql").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def write_export_manifest(outdir, seed, scale):
    outdir = Path(outdir)
    export_files = sorted(
        path for path in outdir.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file() and path.name not in {"manifest.json", "README.txt"}
    )
    files = {}
    for path in export_files:
        content = path.read_bytes()
        files[str(path.relative_to(outdir)).replace("\\", "/")] = {
            "bytes": len(content),
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(),
        }

    manifest = {
        "seed": seed,
        "scale": scale,
        "generator": "generator.config, generator.master_data, generator.enrollments",
        "source": "synthetic generator; no external datasets",
        "record_counts": {
            name: model.objects.count() for name, model in _MODELS
        },
        "files": files,
    }
    (outdir / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )
    (outdir / "README.txt").write_text(
        "Synthetic examination scheduling data exports\n"
        f"Seed: {seed}\n"
        f"Scale: {scale}\n"
        "Source: locally generated synthetic data; no personal or external data.\n"
        "Formats: CSV and JSON files are in their matching subdirectories; "
        "seed.sql contains dependency-ordered SQLite INSERT statements.\n"
        "See manifest.json for row counts and SHA-256 checksums.\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return manifest

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\metrics.py
======================================================================
"""Scheduling coverage, utilization, and persisted-row reconciliation metrics."""

from collections import defaultdict

from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models import Count

from apps.core.models import Enrollment, Exam
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.validation.services.schedule_validator import effective_capacity


def _merge_intervals(intervals):
    merged = []
    for start_at, end_at in sorted(intervals):
        if not merged or start_at > merged[-1][1]:
            merged.append([start_at, end_at])
        else:
            merged[-1][1] = max(merged[-1][1], end_at)
    return merged


def _available_minutes(queryset, resource_field, resource_ids, start_at, end_at):
    if not resource_ids or start_at is None or end_at is None:
        return 0
    by_resource = defaultdict(list)
    for resource_id, window_start, window_end in queryset.filter(
        **{f"{resource_field}__in": resource_ids}
    ).values_list(resource_field, "start_at", "end_at"):
        clipped_start = max(window_start, start_at)
        clipped_end = min(window_end, end_at)
        if clipped_start < clipped_end:
            by_resource[resource_id].append((clipped_start, clipped_end))
    return sum(
        int((right - left).total_seconds() // 60)
        for intervals in by_resource.values()
        for left, right in _merge_intervals(intervals)
    )


def compute_business_metrics(run):
    term = settings.SCHEDULER_POLICY["TERM"]
    assignments = list(
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            run=run,
            status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
        ).select_related("exam", "room")
    )
    assignment_exam_ids = {assignment.exam_id for assignment in assignments}
    unassigned_rows = list(
        UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run).select_related("exam")
    )
    unassigned_exam_ids = {row.exam_id for row in unassigned_rows}
    event_exam_ids = set(
        ScheduleEvent.objects.filter(run=run, exam__isnull=False)
        .exclude(event_type=ScheduleEvent.EventType.CANCELLED)
        .values_list("exam_id", flat=True)
    )
    represented_exam_ids = (
        assignment_exam_ids | unassigned_exam_ids | event_exam_ids
    )
    scheduled_exam_ids = represented_exam_ids - unassigned_exam_ids
    total_exams = len(represented_exam_ids)

    active_enrollments_by_exam = dict(
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            term=term,
            status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE,
            course__exams__exam_id__in=scheduled_exam_ids,
            course__exams__term=term,
        )
        .values("course__exams__exam_id")
        .annotate(count=Count("enrollment_id"))
        .values_list("course__exams__exam_id", "count")
    )
    allocation_counts = dict(
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
        .values("exam_id")
        .annotate(count=Count("allocation_id"))
        .values_list("exam_id", "count")
    )
    active_enrollment_count = sum(active_enrollments_by_exam.values())
    allocation_count = sum(allocation_counts.values())
    student_allocation_rate = (
        allocation_count / active_enrollment_count
        if active_enrollment_count
        else 0.0
    )

    allocated_seats = sum(assignment.allocated_count for assignment in assignments)
    used_capacity = sum(
        effective_capacity(assignment.room_id, assignment.start_at.date())
        for assignment in assignments
    )
    seat_utilization = (
        allocated_seats / used_capacity if used_capacity else 0.0
    )

    assignment_starts = [assignment.start_at for assignment in assignments]
    assignment_ends = [assignment.end_at for assignment in assignments]
    start_at = min(assignment_starts) if assignment_starts else None
    end_at = max(assignment_ends) if assignment_ends else None
    room_ids = {assignment.room_id for assignment in assignments}
    room_booked_minutes = sum(
        (assignment.end_at - assignment.start_at).total_seconds() / 60
        for assignment in assignments
    )
    room_available_minutes = _available_minutes(
        Availability.objects.filter(room__isnull=False),
        "room_id",
        room_ids,
        start_at,
        end_at,
    )
    room_time_utilization = (
        room_booked_minutes / room_available_minutes
        if room_available_minutes
        else 0.0
    )

    invigilator_assignments = list(
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment__run=run,
            assignment__status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
        ).select_related("assignment")
    )
    invigilator_ids = {
        row.invigilator_id for row in invigilator_assignments
    }
    invigilator_booked_minutes = sum(
        (row.assignment.end_at - row.assignment.start_at).total_seconds()
        / 60
        for row in invigilator_assignments
    )
    invigilator_available_minutes = _available_minutes(
        Availability.objects.filter(invigilator__isnull=False),
        "invigilator_id",
        invigilator_ids,
        start_at,
        end_at,
    )
    invigilator_utilization = (
        invigilator_booked_minutes / invigilator_available_minutes
        if invigilator_available_minutes
        else 0.0
    )

    documented_exceptions = sum(
        bool(row.primary_reason_code and row.evidence)
        for row in unassigned_rows
    )
    exception_documentation_rate = (
        documented_exceptions / len(unassigned_rows)
        if unassigned_rows
        else 1.0
    )

    reconciliation_errors = []
    if len(represented_exam_ids) != (
        len(scheduled_exam_ids) + len(unassigned_exam_ids)
    ):
        reconciliation_errors.append(
            "Run exam count does not equal scheduled plus unassigned."
        )
    if scheduled_exam_ids & unassigned_exam_ids:
        reconciliation_errors.append(
            "An exam is both scheduled and marked unassigned."
        )

    assignment_counts = dict(
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
        .values("assignment_id")
        .annotate(count=Count("allocation_id"))
        .values_list("assignment_id", "count")
    )
    for assignment in assignments:
        actual = assignment_counts.get(assignment.assignment_id, 0)
        if actual != assignment.allocated_count:
            reconciliation_errors.append(
                f"{assignment.assignment_id}: allocated_count="
                f"{assignment.allocated_count}, student allocations={actual}."
            )

    for exam_id in sorted(scheduled_exam_ids):
        expected = active_enrollments_by_exam.get(exam_id, 0)
        actual = allocation_counts.get(exam_id, 0)
        if actual != expected:
            reconciliation_errors.append(
                f"{exam_id}: allocated={actual}, active_enrollments={expected}."
            )
    if set(allocation_counts) - scheduled_exam_ids:
        reconciliation_errors.append(
            "Student allocations exist for exams not scheduled in this run."
        )
    if set(unassigned_exam_ids) & set(allocation_counts):
        reconciliation_errors.append(
            "Unassigned exams have persisted student allocations."
        )

    total_exam_count = Exam.objects.filter(term=term).count()
    return {
        "scheduler_run": run.run_id,
        "term": term,
        "total_exams": total_exams,
        "term_exam_count": total_exam_count,
        "scheduled_exams": len(scheduled_exam_ids),
        "unassigned_exams": len(unassigned_exam_ids),
        "scheduling_coverage": round(
            len(scheduled_exam_ids) / total_exams if total_exams else 0.0,
            4,
        ),
        "student_allocation_rate": round(student_allocation_rate, 4),
        "room_seat_utilization": round(seat_utilization, 4),
        "room_time_utilization": round(room_time_utilization, 4),
        "invigilator_utilization": round(invigilator_utilization, 4),
        "exception_documentation_rate": round(
            exception_documentation_rate, 4
        ),
        "reconciliation_ok": not reconciliation_errors,
        "reconciliation_errors": reconciliation_errors,
    }

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\reproducibility.py
======================================================================
"""Compare canonical persisted data across isolated generator runs."""

import hashlib
import json

from django.db import transaction

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Equipment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Student,
)
from apps.core.services.data_generator import (
    clear_generated_data,
    generate_and_persist,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import Availability, RoomClosure, TimeSlot


_CANONICAL_MODELS = [
    Equipment,
    Student,
    Course,
    Room,
    Invigilator,
    TimeSlot,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
    Availability,
    RoomClosure,
    Accommodation,
    Enrollment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
]


def canonical_snapshot():
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    row_counts = {}
    for model in _CANONICAL_MODELS:
        rows = model.objects.order_by(model._meta.pk.name).values()
        row_counts[model._meta.db_table] = rows.count()
        for row in rows.iterator():
            digest.update(
                json.dumps(
                    row,
                    sort_keys=True,
                    separators=(",", ":"),
                    default=str,
                ).encode("utf-8")
            )
            digest.update(b"\n")
    return {"row_counts": row_counts, "checksum": digest.hexdigest()}


def canonical_checksum():
    return canonical_snapshot()["checksum"]


def _run_once(seed, scale):
    savepoint_id = transaction.savepoint()
    try:
        clear_generated_data()
        generate_and_persist(seed=seed, scale=scale)
        return canonical_snapshot()
    finally:
        transaction.savepoint_rollback(savepoint_id)


def verify_reproducibility(seed, scale):
    with transaction.atomic():
        first = _run_once(seed, scale)
    with transaction.atomic():
        second = _run_once(seed, scale)
    return {
        "seed": seed,
        "scale": scale,
        "checksum_run_1": first["checksum"],
        "checksum_run_2": second["checksum"],
        "row_counts_run_1": first["row_counts"],
        "row_counts_run_2": second["row_counts"],
        "match": (
            first["checksum"] == second["checksum"]
            and first["row_counts"] == second["row_counts"]
        ),
    }

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\schedule_validator.py
======================================================================
"""Independent validation of persisted schedule assignments."""

from collections import defaultdict

from django.db.models import Count, Q

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Enrollment,
    ExamRequirement,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    RoomCapacity,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    RoomClosure,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
)
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation

CODE = DetectedViolation.Code


def _overlap(first_start, first_end, second_start, second_end):
    return first_start < second_end and second_start < first_end


def effective_capacity(room_id, on_date):
    capacity = (
        RoomCapacity.objects.filter(
            room_id=room_id,
            effective_from__lte=on_date,
        )
        .filter(Q(effective_to__isnull=True) | Q(effective_to__gte=on_date))
        .order_by("-effective_from")
        .first()
    )
    return capacity.exam_capacity if capacity else 0


def _assignments(run):
    return ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
        run=run,
        status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
    )


def check_student_overlap(run):
    allocations = list(
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
        .select_related("assignment", "exam")
        .order_by("student_id", "assignment_id")
    )
    by_student = defaultdict(list)
    for allocation in allocations:
        by_student[allocation.student_id].append(allocation)

    violations = []
    for student_id, items in by_student.items():
        for index, first in enumerate(items):
            for second in items[index + 1:]:
                if first.exam_id == second.exam_id:
                    continue
