                if not _overlap(
                    first.assignment.start_at,
                    first.assignment.end_at,
                    second.assignment.start_at,
                    second.assignment.end_at,
                ):
                    continue
                for assignment, other in (
                    (first.assignment, second.assignment),
                    (second.assignment, first.assignment),
                ):
                    violations.append(
                        {
                            "code": CODE.STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING,
                            "assignment": assignment,
                            "entity_id": student_id,
                            "detail": f"Student overlaps {other.assignment_id}.",
                        }
                    )
    return violations, len(allocations)


def check_room_capacity(run):
    assignments = list(_assignments(run).select_related("room"))
    violations = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        capacity = effective_capacity(
            assignment.room_id, assignment.start_at.date()
        )
        if assignment.allocated_count > capacity:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": assignment.room_id,
                    "detail": (
                        f"Allocated {assignment.allocated_count}; "
                        f"effective capacity {capacity}."
                    ),
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_missing_equipment(run):
    assignments = list(
        _assignments(run).select_related("room", "exam")
    )
    requirements = defaultdict(set)
    requirement_by_exam = dict(
        ExamRequirement.objects.filter(
            exam_id__in={assignment.exam_id for assignment in assignments}
        ).values_list("exam_id", "requirement_id")
    )
    for requirement_id, equipment_id in (
        ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
            requirement__exam_id__in={a.exam_id for a in assignments}
        ).values_list("requirement_id", "equipment_id")
    ):
        requirements[requirement_id].add(equipment_id)
    violations = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        requirement_id = requirement_by_exam.get(assignment.exam_id)
        needed = requirements.get(requirement_id, set())
        if not needed:
            continue
        available = set(
            assignment.room.equipment.values_list("code", flat=True)
        )
        missing = needed - available
        if missing:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.MISSING_EQUIPMENT,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": assignment.room_id,
                    "detail": f"Missing equipment: {sorted(missing)}.",
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_room_double_booking(run):
    assignments = list(_assignments(run).order_by("room_id", "start_at"))
    by_room = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment in assignments:
        by_room[assignment.room_id].append(assignment)

    violations = []
    for room_id, items in by_room.items():
        for index, first in enumerate(items):
            for second in items[index + 1:]:
                if second.start_at >= first.end_at:
                    break
                if not _overlap(
                    first.start_at,
                    first.end_at,
                    second.start_at,
                    second.end_at,
                ):
                    continue
                for assignment, other in (
                    (first, second),
                    (second, first),
                ):
                    violations.append(
                        {
                            "code": CODE.ROOM_DOUBLE_BOOKED,
                            "assignment": assignment,
                            "entity_id": room_id,
                            "detail": f"Overlaps {other.assignment_id}.",
                        }
                    )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_invigilator_double_booking(run):
    assignments = list(
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment__run=run,
            assignment__status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
        )
        .select_related("assignment")
        .order_by("invigilator_id", "assignment__start_at")
    )
    by_invigilator = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment in assignments:
        by_invigilator[assignment.invigilator_id].append(
            assignment.assignment
        )

    violations = []
    for invigilator_id, items in by_invigilator.items():
        for index, first in enumerate(items):
            for second in items[index + 1:]:
                if second.start_at >= first.end_at:
                    break
                if first.exam_id == second.exam_id:
                    continue
                if not _overlap(
                    first.start_at,
                    first.end_at,
                    second.start_at,
                    second.end_at,
                ):
                    continue
                for assignment in (first, second):
                    violations.append(
                        {
                            "code": CODE.INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED,
                            "assignment": assignment,
                            "entity_id": invigilator_id,
                            "detail": "Invigilator is assigned to overlapping exams.",
                        }
                    )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_invigilator_availability(run):
    assignments = list(
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment__run=run,
            assignment__status=ScheduleAssignment.Status.ASSIGNED,
        ).select_related("assignment")
    )
    violations = []
    for inv_assignment in assignments:
        assignment = inv_assignment.assignment
        available = Availability.objects.filter(
            invigilator_id=inv_assignment.invigilator_id,
            start_at__lte=assignment.start_at,
            end_at__gte=assignment.end_at,
        ).exists()
        if not available:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.INVIGILATOR_UNAVAILABLE,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": inv_assignment.invigilator_id,
                    "detail": "Invigilator is unavailable for the full interval.",
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_accommodation(run):
    allocations = list(
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
        .select_related("assignment__room", "exam")
        .order_by("assignment_id", "student_id")
    )
    by_assignment = defaultdict(list)
    for allocation in allocations:
        by_assignment[allocation.assignment_id].append(allocation)
    student_ids = {allocation.student_id for allocation in allocations}
    accommodation_by_student = defaultdict(list)
    for accommodation in Accommodation.objects.filter(
        student_id__in=student_ids,
        status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE,
    ).order_by("accommodation_id"):
        accommodation_by_student[accommodation.student_id].append(
            accommodation
        )

    violations = []
    for items in by_assignment.values():
        assignment = items[0].assignment
        on_date = assignment.start_at.date()
        minutes = (assignment.end_at - assignment.start_at).total_seconds() / 60
        accommodations = {
            item.student_id: [
                accommodation
                for accommodation in accommodation_by_student[item.student_id]
                if accommodation.effective_from <= on_date
                and (
                    accommodation.effective_to is None
                    or accommodation.effective_to >= on_date
                )
            ]
            for item in items
        }
        separate_students = {
            student_id
            for student_id, student_accommodations in accommodations.items()
            if any(
                item.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM
                for item in student_accommodations
            )
        }
        reported = False
        for item in items:
            student_accommodations = accommodations[item.student_id]
            extra_percent = max(
                (
                    acc.extra_time_percent
                    for acc in student_accommodations
                    if acc.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME
                ),
                default=0,
            )
            required_minutes = (
                item.exam.duration_minutes * (100 + extra_percent) + 99
            ) // 100
            separate_required = item.student_id in separate_students
            accessible_required = any(
                acc.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM
                for acc in student_accommodations
            )
            reasons = []
            if minutes < required_minutes:
                reasons.append(
                    f"requires {required_minutes} minutes, got {int(minutes)}"
                )
            if accessible_required and not assignment.room.is_accessible:
                reasons.append("requires an accessible room")
            if separate_required and len(separate_students) != len(items):
                reasons.append("separate-room student shares with other students")
            if (
                not separate_required
                and separate_students
            ):
                reasons.append("non-separate student shares a separate room")
            if reasons and not reported:
                violations.append(
                    {
                        "code": CODE.ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION,
                        "assignment": assignment,
                        "entity_id": item.student_id,
                        "detail": "; ".join(reasons) + ".",
                    }
                )
                reported = True
    return violations, len(allocations)


def check_room_unavailable(run):
    assignments = list(_assignments(run))
    violations = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        covered = Availability.objects.filter(
            room_id=assignment.room_id,
            start_at__lte=assignment.start_at,
            end_at__gte=assignment.end_at,
        ).exists()
        closed = RoomClosure.objects.filter(
            room_id=assignment.room_id,
        ).filter(
            Q(run__isnull=True) | Q(run=run)
        ).filter(
            start_at__lt=assignment.end_at,
            end_at__gt=assignment.start_at,
        ).exists()
        if not covered or closed:
            detail = (
                "Room availability does not cover the full interval."
                if not covered
                else "Room closure overlaps the assignment."
            )
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": assignment.room_id,
                    "detail": detail,
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_student_allocation_mismatch(run):
    assignments = list(
        _assignments(run).select_related("exam").order_by("exam_id")
    )
    if not assignments:
        return [], 0
    counts = dict(
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
        .values("assignment_id")
        .annotate(count=Count("allocation_id"))
        .values_list("assignment_id", "count")
    )
    violations = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        actual = counts.get(assignment.assignment_id, 0)
        if actual != assignment.allocated_count:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": assignment.assignment_id,
                    "detail": (
                        f"Allocated count is {assignment.allocated_count}; "
                        f"found {actual} student allocations."
                    ),
                }
            )

    by_exam = defaultdict(list)
    for assignment in assignments:
        by_exam[assignment.exam_id].append(assignment)
    for exam_id, exam_assignments in by_exam.items():
        expected = set(
            Enrollment.objects.filter(
                course_id=exam_assignments[0].exam.course_id,
                term=exam_assignments[0].exam.term,
                status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE,
            ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
        )
        actual = set(
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(
                run=run, exam_id=exam_id
            ).values_list("student_id", flat=True)
        )
        if actual != expected:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH,
                    "assignment": exam_assignments[0],
                    "entity_id": exam_id,
                    "detail": (
                        f"Student roster differs: missing={len(expected - actual)}, "
                        f"unexpected={len(actual - expected)}."
                    ),
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


def check_invalid_duration(run):
    assignments = list(
        _assignments(run).select_related("exam")
    )
    violations = []
    for assignment in assignments:
        duration = (
            assignment.end_at - assignment.start_at
        ).total_seconds() / 60
        if duration < assignment.exam.duration_minutes:
            violations.append(
                {
                    "code": CODE.INVALID_DURATION,
                    "assignment": assignment,
                    "entity_id": assignment.assignment_id,
                    "detail": (
                        f"Duration {int(duration)} is shorter than the "
                        f"{assignment.exam.duration_minutes}-minute exam."
                    ),
                }
            )
    return violations, len(assignments)


CHECKS = [
    ("student_double_booking", check_student_overlap),
    ("room_capacity", check_room_capacity),
    ("missing_equipment", check_missing_equipment),
    ("room_double_booked", check_room_double_booking),
    ("invigilator_double_booked", check_invigilator_double_booking),
    ("invigilator_unavailable", check_invigilator_availability),
    ("accommodation_violation", check_accommodation),
    ("room_unavailable", check_room_unavailable),
    ("student_allocation_mismatch", check_student_allocation_mismatch),
    ("invalid_duration", check_invalid_duration),
]

======================================================================
FILE: apps\validation\services\validation_runner.py
======================================================================
"""Persist schedule validation runs and scorecards."""

import json
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_id
from apps.core.services.lifecycle import transition
from apps.scheduling.models import SchedulerRun
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun
from apps.validation.services.metrics import compute_business_metrics
from apps.validation.services.schedule_validator import CHECKS


def _next_id(model, field_name, kind):
    last_id = model.objects.order_by(f"-{field_name}").values_list(
        field_name, flat=True
    ).first()
    number = int(last_id.split("-")[-1]) + 1 if last_id else 1
    return make_id(kind, number)


def run_all_checks(run):
    """Run all independent checks and persist their violations and scorecard."""
    if run.status != SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Scheduler run {run.run_id} must be COMPLETED before validation."
        )

    with transaction.atomic():
        validation_run = ValidationRun.objects.create(
            validation_id=_next_id(
                ValidationRun, "validation_id", "validation_run"
            ),
            scheduler_run=run,
            status=ValidationRun.Status.RUNNING,
        )
        violation_number = int(
            DetectedViolation.objects.order_by("-violation_id")
            .values_list("violation_id", flat=True)
            .first()
            .split("-")[-1]
        ) if DetectedViolation.objects.exists() else 0

        scorecard = []
        total_checks = 0
        total_failures = 0
        for check_name, check in CHECKS:
            violations, inspected = check(run)
            total_checks += inspected
            total_failures += len(violations)
            scorecard.append(
                {
                    "check": check_name,
                    "checks_performed": inspected,
                    "failures": len(violations),
                    "status": "PASS" if not violations else "FAIL",
                }
            )
            for violation in violations:
                violation_number += 1
                DetectedViolation.objects.create(
                    violation_id=make_id("violation", violation_number),
                    validation_run=validation_run,
                    violation_code=violation["code"],
                    assignment=violation.get("assignment"),
                    entity_id=violation.get("entity_id", ""),
                    detail=violation.get("detail", ""),
                )

        validation_run.checks_performed = total_checks
        validation_run.failures = total_failures
        validation_run.save(update_fields=["checks_performed", "failures"])
        transition(
            validation_run,
            ValidationRun.Status.PASSED
            if total_failures == 0
            else ValidationRun.Status.FAILED_CHECKS,
        )
        validation_run.finished_at = timezone.now()
        validation_run.save(update_fields=["finished_at"])

        result = {
            "validation_id": validation_run.validation_id,
            "scheduler_run": run.run_id,
            "status": validation_run.status,
            "checks_performed": total_checks,
            "failures": total_failures,
            "checks": scorecard,
            "business_metrics": compute_business_metrics(run),
        }

    output = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "data" / "validation_scorecard.json"
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return validation_run, result

FILE: config\urls.py

from django.contrib import admin
from django.contrib.auth import views as auth_views
from django.urls import include, path

urlpatterns = [
    path("admin/", admin.site.urls),
    path(
        "accounts/login/",
        auth_views.LoginView.as_view(
            template_name="dashboard/login.html",
            redirect_authenticated_user=True,
        ),
        name="login",
    ),
    path(
        "accounts/logout/",
        auth_views.LogoutView.as_view(next_page="dashboard:home"),
        name="logout",
    ),
    path("", include("apps.dashboard.urls")),
]

FILE: docs\architecture.md
======================================================================
# Architecture: Exam & Room Scheduling System

**Stack:** Python 3.11+ · Django · Django Templates · Bootstrap 5 · JavaScript · SQLite

## 1. Three tiers

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 1 - PRESENTATION (Frontend)                             │
│ Django Templates + Bootstrap 5 + JavaScript                  │
│ Dashboard · Timetable · Exam detail · Student lookup         │
│ Exceptions centre · Validation scorecard · Conflict graph    │
└──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
                               │ HTTP request / response (CSRF-protected POST for actions)
┌──────────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 2 - APPLICATION (Backend)                               │
│ Django Views + Forms  ──calls──►  Python service modules     │
│ Services: data generator · conflict graph · slot allocator · │
│ room allocator · invigilator allocator · accommodation ·     │
│ explanations · schedule validator · anomaly detector ·       │
│ evaluator · metrics                                          │
│ Management commands call the SAME services as the views      │
└──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┘
                               │ Django ORM + transaction.atomic()
┌──────────────────────────────▼───────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 3 - DATA                                                │
│ SQLite: 11 core entities + supporting tables                 │
│ Append-only ScheduleEvent history · SchedulerRun records     │
│ ValidationRun / DetectedViolation                            │
│ Files: CSV/JSON/SQL exports · ground_truth.* (kept separate) │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 2. Architectural principles

1. Templates only present data.
2. Views only handle HTTP: parse input, call a service, render the result.
3. Business rules live in reusable service modules, never in views or templates.
4. Management commands and web actions call the same service functions.
5. Every scheduling execution is a `SchedulerRun` with its own ID.
6. History is never overwritten: assignments are `SUPERSEDED` or `CANCELLED`, events are append-only.
7. The scheduler never reads ground-truth labels.
8. The validator is an independent implementation that reads persisted rows.
9. Every decision and exception is explainable (reason code plus evidence).
10. Web actions never run shell commands.

## 3. Module responsibilities

| App / package | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `apps/core` | Master data models, ID generator, data generator service, export command |
| `apps/scheduling` | Scheduling models, conflict graph, slot/room/invigilator allocators, accommodation logic, explanations, `run_scheduler` |
| `apps/validation` | `ValidationRun`, `DetectedViolation`, independent schedule checks, anomaly fixtures, evaluator, and business metrics |
| `apps/dashboard` | Read-only dashboard views, filters, workflow actions, role helpers, templates, and static files |
| Django auth groups | Learner, Instructor, Academic Administrator, Support Staff, and Platform Administrator role labels |
| `generator/` | Pure-Python seeded generation logic (config, master data, enrollments, anomalies) |

## 4. Why the validator is independent

If the scheduler and validator share the same feasibility function, one bug fools both and the report shows "0 violations" when the schedule is wrong. So the scheduler checks feasibility in memory while building, and the validator re-checks the saved database rows with its own queries.

## 5. Baseline vs anomaly isolation

Every `SchedulerRun` has a `run_type`:

- `BASELINE`: the clean schedule. Must show **0 hard violations**.
- `ANOMALY_FIXTURE`: a separate run, copied from the baseline, into which the five labelled anomalies are injected.

The baseline is never modified by the anomaly commands.

## 6. Actors and access

| Actor | Responsibility | Access |
|---|---|---|
| Learner | View exam information | Read-only dashboard pages |
| Instructor | View course and exam information | Read-only dashboard pages |
| Academic Administrator | Run scheduling and validation workflows | Staff actions and exceptions |
| Support Staff | Review accommodation and accessibility information | Read-only dashboard pages |
| Platform Administrator | Users, permissions, settings | Django admin and staff actions |

Dashboard pages are intentionally readable without login for demonstrations.
Mutating workflow actions require a POST request and either Django staff status
or membership in the Academic Administrator group. Internal record management
remains in Django admin.

## 7. Technology notes

- SQLite is enough for 5,000 students, 300 exams and 100 rooms. Limitation: one writer at a time. Production path: PostgreSQL.
- No DRF, React, Celery or PostgreSQL needed. Long actions run synchronously and must finish in a reasonable web-request time.
- All datetimes are timezone-aware and stored in UTC (`USE_TZ = True`).
======================================================================
FILE: docs\business_rules.md
======================================================================
# Business Rules

## 1. Hard constraints (never violated)

| ID | Rule | Violation code (validator) | Unassigned reason code |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | A student cannot sit two exams whose time intervals overlap | `STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING` | `NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT` |
| H2 | Students allocated to a room never exceed its effective exam capacity | `ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` | `NO_ROOM_CAPACITY` |
| H3 | Room has all equipment the exam requires | `MISSING_EQUIPMENT` | `NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM` |
| H4 | Room is available for the whole interval and no closure overlaps it | `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION` | `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE` |
| H5 | Invigilator is available for the whole interval and not double-booked | `INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED`, `INVIGILATOR_UNAVAILABLE` | `NO_INVIGILATOR` |
| H6 | Accommodations are respected (extra time, separate room, accessible room) | `ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION` | `ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE` |
| H7 | A room hosts only one exam at a time | `ROOM_DOUBLE_BOOKED` | `NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT` |

Additional integrity checks (also hard):

| Check | Violation code |
|---|---|
| Every active enrollment of a scheduled exam has exactly one `StudentRoomAllocation` | `STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH` |
| A student is never allocated to two rooms for the same exam | `STUDENT_ALLOCATION_MISMATCH` |
| Assignment interval length >= required duration for its students | `INVALID_DURATION` |

## 2. Soft constraints (quality only, never override hard rules)

| ID | Goal |
|---|---|
| S1 | Avoid back-to-back exams for a student (gap below 30 minutes) |
| S2 | Spread exams across available days |
| S3 | Minimise wasted seats (best-fit room) |
| S4 | Balance invigilator workload |
| S5 | Avoid more than 2 exams per student per day |

## 3. Reason codes for unassigned exams

| Code | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `NO_CONFLICT_FREE_SLOT` | Every candidate slot clashes with student exams |
| `NO_ROOM_CAPACITY` | No room or room combination fits the students |
| `NO_EQUIPMENT_ROOM` | No eligible room has the required equipment |
| `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE` | Suitable rooms are closed or unavailable |
| `NO_INVIGILATOR` | Not enough available invigilators |
| `ACCOMMODATION_UNSATISFIABLE` | Extended time or room needs cannot be met |
| `NO_FEASIBLE_ASSIGNMENT` | No complete assignment satisfies all constraints |

The scheduler records the **primary** reason (the most common rejection) and keeps every rejection as evidence.

## 4. Formulas

```
required_duration = base_duration * (1 + extra_time_percent / 100)

required_invigilators(room) = max(1, ceil(allocated_students / invigilators_ratio))   # default ratio = 50

room_seat_utilization    = sum(allocated_students) / sum(capacity of rooms used)
room_time_utilization    = booked_room_minutes / available_room_minutes
invigilator_utilization  = assigned_hours / available_hours
scheduling_coverage      = fully_scheduled_exams / total_exams

precision = TP / (TP + FP)
recall    = TP / (TP + FN)
F1        = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall)
```

Reconciliation checks:

```
total_exams = fully_scheduled_exams + unassigned_exams
for every fully scheduled exam: active_enrolled_students = allocated_students
```

## 5. Time rules

- Intervals are **half-open**: `[start, end)`. An exam ending at 11:00 and another starting at 11:00 do NOT overlap.
- Conflict checks use real intervals, not slot labels.
- Hard buffer between exams (same student / room / invigilator): **0 minutes** by default; configurable.
- Soft buffer for students (S1): 30 minutes.

## 6. Configurable policy assumptions

These are university policies chosen by us, not given by the problem statement.

| Assumption | Default |
|---|---|
| Term | `2026-FALL` |
| Exam period | 10 consecutive weekdays, 7 Dec 2026 to 18 Dec 2026 |
| Operating hours | 08:00 to 20:00 |
| Slot start times | 08:00, 10:30, 13:00, 15:30 (4 per day, 40 total) |
| Base exam durations | 60, 90, 120, 180 minutes |
| Extra-time options | 0, 25, 50, 100 percent |
| Longest possible exam | 180 x 2 = 360 minutes, so it must start by 14:00 |
| Exams per course | 1 per term |
| Invigilator ratio | 1 per 50 students, minimum 1 per room |
| Invigilator daily limit | 8 hours (`max_daily_hours`) |
| Invigilator consecutive limit | 4 continuous hours; any positive gap resets the consecutive-work period |
| Room turnover buffer | 0 minutes |
| Anomaly-fixture room closures | Scoped to their `SchedulerRun`; null run means a global closure |
| Accommodated student rule | Allocated to rooms whose interval covers their extended time. First implementation reserves the maximum required duration for everyone sharing that room. |
| Separate-room accommodation | Student is placed in a room with only accommodated students |
| Accommodation share | About 8-10 percent of students |
| Synthetic data | No real personal data. Emails use `@university.example` |

## 7. Accommodation rules

| Type | Effect |
|---|---|
| `EXTRA_TIME` | Student's duration = base x (1 + percent/100) |
| `SEPARATE_ROOM` | Student must be in a room with no non-accommodated students |
| `ACCESSIBLE_ROOM` | Student must be in a room where `is_accessible = true` |

Only accommodations where `effective_from <= exam date` and (`effective_to` is null or `>= exam date`) and `status = ACTIVE` apply. A student may have several accommodations; the strictest combination applies.

## 8. Anomaly types (for ground truth)

| `issue_type` | Injected by | Expected `violation_code` |
|---|---|---|
| `STUDENT_OVERLAP` | Moving an exam into a slot overlapping a connected exam | `STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING` |
| `CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` | Assigning an exam to a room smaller than its allocation | `ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED` |
| `INVIGILATOR_CLASH` | Giving one invigilator two overlapping assignments | `INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED` |
| `ACCOMMODATION_BREACH` | Cutting an extended-time student's interval short or using an unsuitable room | `ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION` |
| `ROOM_CLOSED` | Assigning an exam during a room closure | `ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION` |

`issue_type` is the ground-truth label (what we injected). `violation_code` is what the detector must report. Ground-truth rows point to the specific `assignment_id` affected so evaluation is exact.

## 9. Boundary cases the tests must cover

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Students = capacity | Accepted |
| Students = capacity + 1 | Rejected |
| Exam B starts exactly when exam A ends | Accepted |
| Room closes mid-exam | Rejected |
| Invigilator availability exactly covers exam | Accepted |
| Invigilator leaves before exam ends | Rejected |
| Accommodation expired | Not applied |
| Accommodation active | Applied |
| No suitable room | Unassigned with reason |
| Rerun scheduler | Earlier run retained |
| Failed assignment transaction | No partial rows |
| Unknown student ID in lookup | Friendly error |

# Data Dictionary

Conventions: PK = primary key, FK = foreign key. "Req" = required. All datetimes are timezone-aware (UTC). All PKs are `CharField` holding the ID formats in `er_diagram.md`. IDs are immutable.

Departments (enum `DEPT`): `CS, EE, ME, CE, BIO, CHEM, MATH, PHY, BUS, ARTS`.

---

## 1. Student

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| student_id | Char(10) PK | Y | `STU-\d{6}` | | Y | regex |
| full_name | Char(150) | Y | synthetic name | | N | non-empty |
| email | Email | Y | `stu000001@university.example` | | Y | valid email |
| department | Char(10) | Y | `DEPT` | | N | in enum |
| year_level | SmallInt | Y | 1-4 | year | N | 1 <= v <= 4 |
| status | Char(12) | Y | `ACTIVE, INACTIVE, GRADUATED` | | N | in enum, default ACTIVE |

## 2. Course

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| course_id | Char(10) PK | Y | `CRS-\d{6}` | | Y | regex |
| course_code | Char(12) | Y | e.g. `CS101` | | Y | `^[A-Z]{2,4}\d{3}$` |
| title | Char(200) | Y | | | N | non-empty |
| department | Char(10) | Y | `DEPT` | | N | in enum |
| credits | SmallInt | Y | 1-6 | credit | N | 1 <= v <= 6 |

## 3. Enrollment (bridge Student-Course)

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| enrollment_id | Char(10) PK | Y | `ENR-\d{6}` | | Y | regex |
| student | FK Student | Y | existing student_id | | | FK exists |
| course | FK Course | Y | existing course_id | | | FK exists |
| term | Char(12) | Y | `2026-FALL` | | | in configured terms |
| status | Char(12) | Y | `ACTIVE, DROPPED, WITHDRAWN, COMPLETED` | | | in enum |
| effective_from | Date | Y | | date | | |
| effective_to | Date | N | | date | | null or >= effective_from |

Unique together: (student, course, term). Inactive/graduated students may not have ACTIVE enrollments.

## 4. Exam

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| exam_id | Char(20) PK | Y | `EXAM-\d{4}-\d{6}` | | Y | regex, year = term year |
| course | FK Course | Y | existing course_id | | | FK exists |
| title | Char(200) | Y | | | N | non-empty |
| term | Char(12) | Y | `2026-FALL` | | | |
| duration_minutes | SmallInt | Y | 60, 90, 120, 180 (valid range 30-240) | minute | N | multiple of 15 |
| status | Char(12) | Y | `DRAFT, PLANNED, SCHEDULED, UNASSIGNED` | | | valid transition |

Unique together: (course, term). Active enrollment count is **derived** from Enrollment, never stored.

## 5. ExamRequirement

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| exam | OneToOne Exam | Y | existing exam_id | | Y | FK exists |
| equipment | M2M Equipment via `ExamRequirementEquipment` | N | equipment codes | | | codes exist |

| invigilator_ratio | SmallInt | Y | 20-100 | students per invigilator | | default 50 |

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |

## 9. Availability

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| availability_id | Char(10) PK | Y | `AVL-\d{6}` | | Y | regex |
| room | FK Room | N | existing room_id | | | exactly one of room/invigilator |
| invigilator | FK Invigilator | N | existing invigilator_id | | | exactly one of room/invigilator |
| start_at | DateTime | Y | UTC | datetime | | |
| end_at | DateTime | Y | UTC | datetime | | end_at > start_at |

DB CHECK: `(room IS NULL) <> (invigilator IS NULL)`. An allocation must fit completely inside one window.

## 10. Accommodation

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| accommodation_id | Char(10) PK | Y | `ACC-\d{6}` | | Y | regex |
| student | FK Student | Y | existing student_id | | | FK exists |
| accommodation_type | Char(20) | Y | `EXTRA_TIME, SEPARATE_ROOM, ACCESSIBLE_ROOM` | | | in enum |
| extra_time_percent | SmallInt | Y | 0, 25, 50, 100 | percent | | >0 only if EXTRA_TIME, else 0 |
| effective_from | Date | Y | | date | | |
| effective_to | Date | N | | date | | null or >= effective_from |
| status | Char(10) | Y | `ACTIVE, REVOKED` | | | in enum |

No free-text medical notes are stored.

## 11. ScheduleAssignment (bridge Exam-Room, per run)

| Field | Type | Req | Allowed values / format | Unit | Unique | Validation |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| assignment_id | Char(64) PK | Y | `ASN-<exam-id>-<room-id>-<run-id>` | | Y | regex, matches its FKs |
| run | FK SchedulerRun | Y | existing run_id | | | FK exists |
| exam | FK Exam | Y | existing exam_id | | | FK exists |
| room | FK Room | Y | existing room_id | | | FK exists |
| start_at | DateTime | Y | UTC | datetime | | within operating hours |
| end_at | DateTime | Y | UTC | datetime | | end_at > start_at; >= required duration |
| allocated_count | SmallInt | Y | >= 1 | student | | equals count of its StudentRoomAllocation rows |
| status | Char(12) | Y | `ASSIGNED, SUPERSEDED, CANCELLED` | | | valid transition |

Unique together: (run, exam, room). All rooms of one exam in a run share the same `start_at`.

## Supporting entities (summary)

| Entity | PK | Key fields | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Equipment | code | name | e.g. `PROJECTOR, COMPUTER, LAB_BENCH, ACCESSIBLE_DESK` |
| RoomEquipment | auto | room FK, equipment FK | unique (room, equipment) |
| ExamRequirementEquipment | auto | requirement FK, equipment FK | unique pair |
| RoomClosure | `CLS-` | room FK, optional scheduler-run FK, start_at, end_at, reason (`MAINTENANCE, EVENT, REPAIR`) | end_at > start_at; null run means a global closure |
| TimeSlot | `SLOT-` | slot_date, start_time, label | unique (slot_date, start_time) |
| SchedulerRun | `RUN-` | run_type (`BASELINE, ANOMALY_FIXTURE`), status, algorithm_version, seed, started_at, finished_at, notes | never deleted |
| ScheduleEvent | `EVT-` | run FK, assignment FK (null), exam FK, event_type (`ASSIGNED, MOVED, CANCELLED, UNASSIGNED, SUPERSEDED`), detail JSON, event_at | append-only; no update/delete |
| InvigilatorAssignment | `IAS-` | assignment FK, invigilator FK, role (`CHIEF, ASSISTANT`) | unique (assignment, invigilator) |
| StudentRoomAllocation | `SRA-` | assignment FK, student FK, required_minutes | unique (assignment, student) |
| UnassignedExam | `UNA-` | run FK, exam FK, primary_reason_code, evidence JSON | unique (run, exam) |
| ValidationRun | `VAL-` | run FK, status, checks_performed, failures, started_at | |
| DetectedViolation | `VIO-` | validation_run FK, violation_code, assignment FK (null), entity_id, detail | |

## Ground-truth file (NOT a database input)

`data/ground_truth.csv` columns: `anomaly_id, issue_type, assignment_id, affected_entity_id, expected_violation_code, run_id`.
It is read only by the evaluator after detection finishes.

## Phase 3 generation assumptions

- The default seed is `20261022`; `starter` creates 200 students, 30 courses,
  20 exams, 15 rooms, and 25 invigilators. `full` creates 5,000 students,
  300 courses/exams, 100 rooms, and 200 invigilators.
- Each course has at most one exam in a term. Starter scale intentionally leaves
  ten courses without an exam; only courses with an exam receive enrollments.
- Each student is enrolled in 4-6 exam courses. Enrollment counts are derived
  from the bridge rows rather than stored separately.
- Lecture halls are accessible and provisioned for computer/projector/practical
  examinations so both starter and full scales can schedule specialist exams
  without relying on unusually rare room-type draws. Invigilators have broad
  operating-day windows on a deterministic subset of exam days.
- Generated identities are deterministic counters. Timestamps use the fixed
  UTC exam calendar; no external or personal data is used.
- Assignment-linked anomaly labels are produced with the isolated anomaly
  fixture in the later validation phase, not mixed into this clean baseline.

# ER Diagram, Relationships, IDs and Lifecycles

## 1. ER diagram (Mermaid)

```mermaid
erDiagram
    STUDENT ||--o{ ENROLLMENT : has
    COURSE ||--o{ ENROLLMENT : has
    COURSE ||--o{ EXAM : "assessed by"
    EXAM ||--o| EXAM_REQUIREMENT : has
    EXAM_REQUIREMENT }o--o{ EQUIPMENT : "needs (ExamRequirementEquipment)"
    ROOM }o--o{ EQUIPMENT : "has (RoomEquipment)"
    ROOM ||--o{ ROOM_CAPACITY : has
    ROOM ||--o{ ROOM_CLOSURE : has
    ROOM ||--o{ AVAILABILITY : "available (room)"
    INVIGILATOR ||--o{ AVAILABILITY : "available (invigilator)"
    STUDENT ||--o{ ACCOMMODATION : has
    SCHEDULER_RUN ||--o{ SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT : contains
    EXAM ||--o{ SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT : "placed by"
    ROOM ||--o{ SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT : "hosts"
    SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT ||--o{ INVIGILATOR_ASSIGNMENT : staffed
    INVIGILATOR ||--o{ INVIGILATOR_ASSIGNMENT : works
    SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT ||--o{ STUDENT_ROOM_ALLOCATION : seats
    STUDENT ||--o{ STUDENT_ROOM_ALLOCATION : "seated as"
    SCHEDULER_RUN ||--o{ UNASSIGNED_EXAM : records
    EXAM ||--o{ UNASSIGNED_EXAM : "failed"
    SCHEDULER_RUN ||--o{ SCHEDULE_EVENT : logs
    SCHEDULE_EVENT }o--o| SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT : about
    SCHEDULER_RUN ||--o{ VALIDATION_RUN : "checked by"
    VALIDATION_RUN ||--o{ DETECTED_VIOLATION : finds
    TIME_SLOT ||--o{ SCHEDULE_ASSIGNMENT : "suggests start"
```

## 2. Cardinality table

| Relationship | Cardinality | Optional? | Implemented by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Student - Course | N:M | yes | `Enrollment` (bridge) |
| Course - Exam | 1:N (one per term) | Exam must have a Course | FK `Exam.course` + unique(course, term) |
| Exam - ExamRequirement | 1:1 | Requirement optional | OneToOne `ExamRequirement.exam` |
| ExamRequirement - Equipment | N:M | yes | `ExamRequirementEquipment` |
| Room - Equipment | N:M | yes | `RoomEquipment` |
| Room - RoomCapacity | 1:N | at least one | FK `RoomCapacity.room` |
| Room - RoomClosure | 1:N | yes | FK `RoomClosure.room` |
| Room - Availability | 1:N | yes | nullable FK `Availability.room` |
| Invigilator - Availability | 1:N | yes | nullable FK `Availability.invigilator` |
| Student - Accommodation | 1:N | yes | FK `Accommodation.student` |
| Exam - Room | N:M | yes | `ScheduleAssignment` (bridge, one row per exam-room-run) |
| SchedulerRun - ScheduleAssignment | 1:N | no | FK `ScheduleAssignment.run` |
| ScheduleAssignment - Invigilator | N:M | at least 1 for a valid assignment | `InvigilatorAssignment` |
| ScheduleAssignment - Student | N:M | one row per enrolled student | `StudentRoomAllocation` |
| SchedulerRun - UnassignedExam | 1:N | yes | FK |
| ValidationRun - DetectedViolation | 1:N | yes | FK |

`Availability` has two nullable FKs (`room`, `invigilator`) and a database CHECK that **exactly one** is filled.

## 3. ID conventions

IDs are deterministic, zero-padded, immutable, generated by `apps/core/services/id_generator.py` (not in `save()`).

| Entity | Format | Regex |
|---|---|---|
| Student | `STU-000001` | `^STU-\d{6}$` |
| Course | `CRS-000001` | `^CRS-\d{6}$` |
| Enrollment | `ENR-000001` | `^ENR-\d{6}$` |
| Exam | `EXAM-2026-000001` | `^EXAM-\d{4}-\d{6}$` |
| ExamRequirement | `REQ-000001` | `^REQ-\d{6}$` |
| Room | `ROOM-000001` | `^ROOM-\d{6}$` |
| RoomCapacity | `CAP-000001` | `^CAP-\d{6}$` |
| RoomClosure | `CLS-000001` | `^CLS-\d{6}$` |
| Invigilator | `INV-000001` | `^INV-\d{6}$` |
| Availability | `AVL-000001` | `^AVL-\d{6}$` |
| Accommodation | `ACC-000001` | `^ACC-\d{6}$` |
| TimeSlot | `SLOT-000001` | `^SLOT-\d{6}$` |
| SchedulerRun | `RUN-000001` | `^RUN-\d{6}$` |
| ScheduleAssignment | `ASN-<exam-id>-<room-id>-<run-id>` e.g. `ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000001-RUN-000001` | `^ASN-EXAM-\d{4}-\d{6}-ROOM-\d{6}-RUN-\d{6}$` |
| InvigilatorAssignment | `IAS-000001` | `^IAS-\d{6}$` |
| StudentRoomAllocation | `SRA-000001` | `^SRA-\d{6}$` |
| UnassignedExam | `UNA-000001` | `^UNA-\d{6}$` |
| ScheduleEvent | `EVT-000001` | `^EVT-\d{6}$` |
| ValidationRun | `VAL-000001` | `^VAL-\d{6}$` |
| DetectedViolation | `VIO-000001` | `^VIO-\d{6}$` |
| Equipment | natural key, e.g. `PROJECTOR`, `COMPUTER`, `LAB_BENCH` | `^[A-Z_]+$` |

The run suffix is added to the required `ASN-<exam-id>-<room-id>` pattern so two runs can hold the same exam-room pair without an ID clash.

## 4. Lifecycle states

```
Exam:           DRAFT -> PLANNED -> SCHEDULED  (SCHEDULED -> PLANNED on re-run)
                              \-> UNASSIGNED          (UNASSIGNED -> PLANNED on re-run)
Enrollment:     ACTIVE -> DROPPED | WITHDRAWN | COMPLETED
ScheduleAssignment: ASSIGNED -> SUPERSEDED | CANCELLED
SchedulerRun:   RUNNING -> COMPLETED | FAILED
ValidationRun:  RUNNING -> PASSED | FAILED_CHECKS | ERROR
Student:        ACTIVE <-> INACTIVE ; ACTIVE -> GRADUATED
Accommodation:  ACTIVE -> REVOKED   (expiry is by effective_to date, not status)
```

Invalid transitions (e.g. `CANCELLED -> ASSIGNED`) are rejected by a service-layer function. Old rows are never deleted.

## 5. Effective dates

| Entity | Fields | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| RoomCapacity | `effective_from`, `effective_to` (null = open-ended) | Ranges for one room must not overlap |
| Accommodation | `effective_from`, `effective_to` | Applies only if exam date falls inside |
| Enrollment | `effective_from`, `effective_to` | Active only inside the range and `status = ACTIVE` |
| RoomClosure | `start_at`, `end_at` | Blocks any overlapping interval |
| Availability | `start_at`, `end_at` | Allocation must be fully inside one window |

FILE: generator\enrollments.py

"""Generate deterministic, weighted Student-Course bridge rows."""

from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_id

from .config import TERM, TERM_START


def _weighted_sample(rng, items, weights, count):
    """Sample without replacement using the provided positive weights."""
    remaining_items = list(items)
    remaining_weights = list(weights)
    chosen = []
    for _ in range(min(count, len(remaining_items))):
        total = sum(remaining_weights)
        if total <= 0:
            break
        value = rng.uniform(0, total)
        cumulative = 0.0
        for index, weight in enumerate(remaining_weights):
            cumulative += weight
            if value <= cumulative:
                chosen.append(remaining_items.pop(index))
                remaining_weights.pop(index)
                break
    return chosen


def generate_enrollments(rng, students, courses, courses_with_exams_ids):
    exam_courses = [
        course for course in courses
        if course["course_id"] in courses_with_exams_ids
    ]
    popularity = {
        course["course_id"]: rng.paretovariate(1.5)
        for course in exam_courses
    }

    enrollments = []
    index = 1
    for student in students:
        own_department = [
            course for course in exam_courses
            if course["department"] == student["department"]
        ]
        other_departments = [
            course for course in exam_courses
            if course["department"] != student["department"]
        ]
        target_count = rng.randint(4, 6)
        target_own = max(0, min(target_count - 1, round(target_count * 0.6)))

        chosen = []
        if own_department:
            chosen.extend(
                _weighted_sample(
                    rng,
                    own_department,
                    [popularity[course["course_id"]] for course in own_department],
                    target_own,
                )
            )
        remaining_count = target_count - len(chosen)
        if remaining_count and other_departments:
            chosen.extend(
                _weighted_sample(
                    rng,
                    other_departments,
                    [popularity[course["course_id"]] for course in other_departments],
                    remaining_count,
                )
            )
        if len(chosen) < min(target_count, len(exam_courses)):
            already_chosen = {course["course_id"] for course in chosen}
            fallback = [
                course for course in exam_courses
                if course["course_id"] not in already_chosen
            ]
            chosen.extend(
                _weighted_sample(
                    rng,
                    fallback,
                    [popularity[course["course_id"]] for course in fallback],
                    min(target_count - len(chosen), len(fallback)),
                )
            )

        for course in chosen:
            enrollments.append(
                dict(
                    enrollment_id=make_id("enrollment", index),
                    student_id=student["student_id"],
                    course_id=course["course_id"],
                    term=TERM,
                    status="ACTIVE",
                    effective_from=TERM_START,
                    effective_to=None,
                )
            )
            index += 1
    return enrollments

FILE: generator\master_data.py

"""Django-independent synthetic master-data generators."""

import datetime as dt

from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_exam_id, make_id

from .config import (
    ACC_EFFECTIVE_FROM,
    ACC_EFFECTIVE_TO,
    ACCOMMODATION_PCT,
    DEPARTMENTS,
    EQUIPMENT,
    EXAM_DATES,
    EXAM_DURATIONS,
    EXAM_YEAR,
    SLOT_STARTS,
    TERM,
    TERM_START,
)


def _date(value):
    return dt.date.fromisoformat(value)


def _time(value):
    hour, minute = value.split(":")
    return dt.time(int(hour), int(minute))


def generate_equipment():
    return [dict(code=code, name=name) for code, name in EQUIPMENT.items()]


def generate_timeslots():
    slots = []
    for index, (day, start) in enumerate(
        ((day, start) for day in EXAM_DATES for start in SLOT_STARTS),
        start=1,
    ):
        slots.append(
            dict(
                slot_id=make_id("slot", index),
                slot_date=_date(day),
                start_time=_time(start),
                label=f"{day} {start}",
            )
        )
    return slots


def generate_students(rng, count):
    return [
        dict(
            student_id=make_id("student", index),
            full_name=f"Student {index:05d}",
            email=f"stu{index:06d}@university.example",
            department=rng.choice(DEPARTMENTS),
            year_level=rng.randint(1, 4),
            status="ACTIVE",
        )
        for index in range(1, count + 1)
    ]


def generate_courses(rng, count):
    courses = []
    for index in range(1, count + 1):
        department = DEPARTMENTS[(index - 1) % len(DEPARTMENTS)]
        courses.append(
            dict(
                course_id=make_id("course", index),
                course_code=f"{department}{100 + index}",
                title=f"{department} Course {index}",
                department=department,
                credits=rng.choice([3, 3, 3, 4, 4, 6]),
            )
        )
    return courses


def generate_exams(rng, courses, count):
    return [
        dict(
            exam_id=make_exam_id(index, EXAM_YEAR),
            course_id=courses[index - 1]["course_id"],
            title=f"Exam for {courses[index - 1]['course_code']}",
            term=TERM,
            duration_minutes=rng.choice(EXAM_DURATIONS),
            status="PLANNED",
        )
        for index in range(1, count + 1)
    ]


def generate_rooms_and_capacities(rng, count):
    rooms = []
    capacities = []
    per_building = max(1, (count + 4) // 5)
    for index in range(1, count + 1):
        building_index = (index - 1) // per_building
        building_letter = chr(65 + building_index)
        room_number = (index - 1) % per_building + 1
        room_type = rng.choices(
            ["LECTURE_HALL", "CLASSROOM", "COMPUTER_LAB", "SCIENCE_LAB"],
            weights=[0.20, 0.50, 0.15, 0.15],
            k=1,
        )[0]
        if room_type == "LECTURE_HALL":
            capacity = rng.randint(200, 500)
        elif room_type == "CLASSROOM":
            capacity = rng.randint(30, 80)
        else:
            capacity = rng.randint(20, 40)

        room_id = make_id("room", index)
        rooms.append(
            dict(
                room_id=room_id,
                building=f"Block {building_letter}",
                room_number=f"{building_letter}-{room_number:03d}",
                room_type=room_type,
                is_accessible=rng.random() < 0.30,
                status="ACTIVE",
            )
        )
        capacities.append(
            dict(
                capacity_id=make_id("capacity", index),
                room_id=room_id,
                exam_capacity=capacity,
                effective_from=_date(TERM_START),
                effective_to=None,
            )
        )
    for room in rooms:
        if room["room_type"] == "LECTURE_HALL":
            room["is_accessible"] = True
    return rooms, capacities


def generate_room_equipment_pairs(rooms):
    pairs = []
    for room in rooms:
        equipment = {"WHITEBOARD"}
        if room["room_type"] == "LECTURE_HALL":
            equipment.update({"COMPUTER", "LAB_BENCH", "PROJECTOR"})
        elif room["room_type"] == "COMPUTER_LAB":
            equipment.update({"COMPUTER", "PROJECTOR"})
        elif room["room_type"] == "SCIENCE_LAB":
            equipment.add("LAB_BENCH")
        if room["is_accessible"]:
            equipment.add("ACCESSIBLE_DESK")
        pairs.extend((room["room_id"], code) for code in sorted(equipment))
    return pairs


def generate_invigilators(rng, count):
    from decimal import Decimal

    return [
        dict(
            invigilator_id=make_id("invigilator", index),
            full_name=f"Invigilator {index:05d}",
            email=f"inv{index:06d}@university.example",
            department=rng.choice(DEPARTMENTS),
            max_daily_hours=Decimal(str(rng.choice([6.0, 7.0, 8.0]))),
            status="ACTIVE",
        )
        for index in range(1, count + 1)
    ]


def generate_room_availability(rooms):
    start = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 8, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    end = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 18, 20, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    return [
        dict(
            availability_id=make_id("availability", index),
            room_id=room["room_id"],
            invigilator_id=None,
            start_at=start,
            end_at=end,
        )
        for index, room in enumerate(rooms, start=1)
    ]


def generate_room_closures(rng, rooms):
    closures = []
    count = max(1, len(rooms) // 20)
    for index, room in enumerate(rng.sample(rooms, min(count, len(rooms))), start=1):
        day = rng.choice(EXAM_DATES)
        start_hour = rng.choice([8, 9, 10, 13, 15])
        end_hour = start_hour + rng.choice([1, 2, 3])
        closures.append(
            dict(
                closure_id=make_id("closure", index),
                room_id=room["room_id"],
                start_at=dt.datetime.fromisoformat(day).replace(
                    hour=start_hour, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc
                ),
                end_at=dt.datetime.fromisoformat(day).replace(
                    hour=end_hour, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc
                ),
                reason=rng.choice(["MAINTENANCE", "EVENT", "REPAIR"]),
            )
        )
    return closures


def generate_invigilator_availability(rng, invigilators, start_index=1):
    availability = []
    index = start_index
    for invigilator in invigilators:
        window_count = rng.randint(4, 7)
        for day in rng.sample(EXAM_DATES, min(window_count, len(EXAM_DATES))):
            availability.append(
                dict(
                    availability_id=make_id("availability", index),
                    room_id=None,
                    invigilator_id=invigilator["invigilator_id"],
                    start_at=dt.datetime.fromisoformat(day).replace(
                        hour=8, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc
                    ),
                    end_at=dt.datetime.fromisoformat(day).replace(
                        hour=20, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc
                    ),
                )
            )
            index += 1
    return availability


def generate_accommodations(rng, students):
    count = min(len(students), max(1, int(len(students) * ACCOMMODATION_PCT)))
    accommodations = []
    for index, student in enumerate(rng.sample(students, count), start=1):
        accommodation_type = rng.choices(
            ["EXTRA_TIME", "SEPARATE_ROOM", "ACCESSIBLE_ROOM"],
            weights=[0.60, 0.20, 0.20],
            k=1,
        )[0]
        extra_time_percent = (
            rng.choice([25, 50, 100]) if accommodation_type == "EXTRA_TIME" else 0
        )
        accommodations.append(
            dict(
                accommodation_id=make_id("accommodation", index),
                student_id=student["student_id"],
                accommodation_type=accommodation_type,
                extra_time_percent=extra_time_percent,
                effective_from=_date(ACC_EFFECTIVE_FROM),
                effective_to=_date(ACC_EFFECTIVE_TO),
                status="ACTIVE",
            )
        )
    return accommodations


def generate_exam_requirements(rng, exams, courses_by_id):
    requirements = []
    equipment_pairs = []
    for index, exam in enumerate(exams, start=1):
        course = courses_by_id[exam["course_id"]]
        required_equipment = set()
        if course["department"] in ("CS", "BUS"):
            required_equipment.add("COMPUTER")
        if course["department"] in ("BIO", "CHEM", "PHY"):
            required_equipment.add("LAB_BENCH")
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            required_equipment.add("PROJECTOR")

        requirement_id = make_id("requirement", index)
        requirements.append(
            dict(
                requirement_id=requirement_id,
                exam_id=exam["exam_id"],
                allow_multi_room=True,
                invigilator_ratio=50,
            )
        )
        equipment_pairs.extend(
            (requirement_id, code) for code in sorted(required_equipment)
        )
    return requirements, equipment_pairs

"""Tiny helpers that build valid records so each test only changes what it is testing."""
import datetime as dt

from django.utils import timezone

from apps.core.models import Accommodation, Course, Enrollment, Exam, Invigilator, Room, RoomCapacity, Student
from apps.scheduling.models import ScheduleAssignment, SchedulerRun
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_assignment_id, make_exam_id, make_id

D = dt.date(2026, 12, 7)


def dtm(day=7, hour=9, minute=0):
    return timezone.make_aware(dt.datetime(2026, 12, day, hour, minute))


def make_student(n=1, **kw):
    d = dict(student_id=make_id("student", n), full_name=f"Student {n}", email=f"stu{n:06d}@university.example",
             department="CS", year_level=1)
    d.update(kw)
    return Student.objects.create(**d)


def make_course(n=1, **kw):
    d = dict(course_id=make_id("course", n), course_code=f"CS{100 + n}", title=f"Course {n}",
             department="CS", credits=3)
    d.update(kw)
    return Course.objects.create(**d)


def make_exam(n=1, course=None, **kw):
    course = course or make_course(n)
    d = dict(exam_id=make_exam_id(n), course=course, title=f"Exam {n}", term="2026-FALL", duration_minutes=120)
    d.update(kw)
    return Exam.objects.create(**d)


def make_room(n=1, **kw):
    d = dict(room_id=make_id("room", n), building="Block A", room_number=f"A-{100 + n}", room_type="CLASSROOM")
    d.update(kw)
    return Room.objects.create(**d)


def make_invigilator(n=1, **kw):
    d = dict(invigilator_id=make_id("invigilator", n), full_name=f"Inv {n}", email=f"inv{n:06d}@university.example")
    d.update(kw)
    return Invigilator.objects.create(**d)


def make_run(n=1, **kw):
    return SchedulerRun.objects.create(run_id=make_id("run", n), **kw)


def make_assignment(exam, room, run, **kw):
    d = dict(assignment_id=make_assignment_id(exam.exam_id, room.room_id, run.run_id), run=run, exam=exam,
             room=room, start_at=dtm(hour=9), end_at=dtm(hour=11), allocated_count=30)
    d.update(kw)
    return ScheduleAssignment.objects.create(**d)


def make_enrollment(n, student, course, **kw):
    d = dict(enrollment_id=make_id("enrollment", n), student=student, course=course, term="2026-FALL",
             effective_from=D)
    d.update(kw)
    return Enrollment.objects.create(**d)


def make_capacity(n, room, cap=50, **kw):
    d = dict(capacity_id=make_id("capacity", n), room=room, exam_capacity=cap, effective_from=D)
    d.update(kw)
    return RoomCapacity.objects.create(**d)


def make_accommodation(n, student, **kw):
    d = dict(accommodation_id=make_id("accommodation", n), student=student,
             accommodation_type="EXTRA_TIME", extra_time_percent=50, effective_from=D)
    d.update(kw)
    return Accommodation.objects.create(**d)

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_accommodations.py

import datetime as dt

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import Accommodation, Student
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_id
from apps.scheduling.services.accommodation import (
    accommodations_active_on,
    needs_accessible_room,
    needs_separate_room,
    required_minutes,
)


class AccommodationTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.student = Student.objects.create(
            student_id=make_id("student", 1),
            full_name="Test student",
            email="student@example.test",
            department="CS",
            year_level=1,
        )

    def create_accommodation(self, number, **overrides):
        values = {
            "accommodation_id": make_id("accommodation", number),
            "student": self.student,
            "accommodation_type": Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME,
            "extra_time_percent": 25,
            "effective_from": dt.date(2026, 9, 1),
            "effective_to": dt.date(2026, 12, 31),
        }
        values.update(overrides)
        return Accommodation.objects.create(**values)

    def test_required_time_uses_strictest_active_extension(self):
        twenty_five = self.create_accommodation(1)
        fifty = self.create_accommodation(
            2, extra_time_percent=50
        )
        self.assertEqual(required_minutes(120, [twenty_five, fifty]), 180)
        self.assertEqual(required_minutes(120, []), 120)
        self.assertEqual(required_minutes(180, [fifty]), 270)

    def test_effective_dates_and_status_are_honored(self):
        expired = self.create_accommodation(
            1, effective_to=dt.date(2026, 10, 1)
        )
        future = self.create_accommodation(
            2, effective_from=dt.date(2026, 12, 11)
        )
        revoked = self.create_accommodation(
            3, status=Accommodation.Status.REVOKED
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            accommodations_active_on(
                self.student.student_id, dt.date(2026, 12, 10)
            ),
            [],
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            accommodations_active_on(
                self.student.student_id, dt.date(2026, 12, 11)
            ),
            [future],
        )
        self.assertNotIn(expired, [future, revoked])

    def test_room_accommodation_flags(self):
        separate = self.create_accommodation(
            1,
            accommodation_type=Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM,
            extra_time_percent=0,
        )
        accessible = self.create_accommodation(
            2,
            accommodation_type=Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM,
            extra_time_percent=0,
        )
        self.assertTrue(needs_separate_room([separate]))
        self.assertFalse(needs_accessible_room([separate]))
        self.assertTrue(needs_accessible_room([accessible]))

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_anomalies.py
======================================================================
import json
import tempfile
from io import StringIO
from pathlib import Path

from django.core.management import call_command
from django.test import TestCase, override_settings

from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.scheduling.models import ScheduleAssignment, SchedulerRun
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun
from apps.validation.services.anomaly_injector import inject
from apps.validation.services.evaluator import evaluate
from apps.validation.services.validation_runner import run_all_checks
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class AnomalyPipelineTests(TestCase):
    def test_phase_five_management_commands(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        baseline = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="phase5_commands_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                baseline_output = StringIO()
                call_command(
                    "validate_schedule",
                    run=baseline.run_id,
                    stdout=baseline_output,
                )
                self.assertIn("PASS", baseline_output.getvalue())

                injection_output = StringIO()
                call_command(
                    "inject_anomalies",
                    baseline=baseline.run_id,
                    stdout=injection_output,
                )
                self.assertIn("ground-truth rows: 7", injection_output.getvalue())

                detection_output = StringIO()
                call_command("detect_violations", stdout=detection_output)
                self.assertIn("total: 7 violations", detection_output.getvalue())

                evaluation_output = StringIO()
                call_command("evaluate_anomalies", stdout=evaluation_output)
                self.assertIn("TP=7 FP=0 FN=0", evaluation_output.getvalue())
                self.assertTrue(
                    (Path(folder) / "data" / "anomaly_evaluation.json").exists()
                )

    def test_inject_detect_evaluate_and_revalidate_baseline(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        baseline = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        baseline_assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=baseline)
            .order_by("assignment_id")
            .values_list("assignment_id", "start_at", "end_at", "room_id")
        )

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="phase5_outputs_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                anomaly_run, labels, selected = inject(baseline.run_id)
                self.assertEqual(
                    anomaly_run.run_type,
                    SchedulerRun.RunType.ANOMALY_FIXTURE,
                )
                self.assertEqual(len(selected), 7)
                self.assertEqual(len(labels), 7)
                self.assertEqual(
                    {row["issue_type"] for row in labels},
                    {
                        "STUDENT_OVERLAP",
                        "CAPACITY_EXCEEDED",
                        "INVIGILATOR_CLASH",
                        "ACCOMMODATION_BREACH",
                        "ROOM_CLOSED",
                    },
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    {row["expected_violation_code"] for row in labels},
                    {
                        DetectedViolation.Code.STUDENT_DOUBLE_BOOKING,
                        DetectedViolation.Code.ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED,
                        DetectedViolation.Code.INVIGILATOR_DOUBLE_BOOKED,
                        DetectedViolation.Code.ACCOMMODATION_VIOLATION,
                        DetectedViolation.Code.ROOM_UNAVAILABLE_VIOLATION,
                    },
                )
                with (Path(folder) / "data" / "ground_truth.json").open(
                    encoding="utf-8"
                ) as file:
                    saved_labels = json.load(file)
                self.assertEqual(len(saved_labels), 7)
                self.assertEqual(
                    {row["run_id"] for row in saved_labels},
                    {anomaly_run.run_id},
                )

                validation_run, scorecard = run_all_checks(anomaly_run)
                self.assertEqual(
                    validation_run.status,
                    ValidationRun.Status.FAILED_CHECKS,
                )
                self.assertEqual(scorecard["failures"], 7)
                result = evaluate(validation_run)

                self.assertEqual(result["tp"], 7, msg=result)
                self.assertEqual(result["fp"], 0, msg=result)
                self.assertEqual(result["fn"], 0, msg=result)
                self.assertEqual(result["precision"], 1.0)
                self.assertEqual(result["recall"], 1.0)
                self.assertEqual(result["f1"], 1.0)
                self.assertTrue(
                    scorecard["business_metrics"]["reconciliation_ok"],
                    msg=scorecard["business_metrics"]["reconciliation_errors"],
                )

                baseline_validation, baseline_scorecard = run_all_checks(
                    baseline
                )

        self.assertEqual(baseline_scorecard["failures"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            baseline_validation.status, ValidationRun.Status.PASSED
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            list(
                ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=baseline)
                .order_by("assignment_id")
                .values_list(
                    "assignment_id", "start_at", "end_at", "room_id"
                )
            ),
            baseline_assignments,
        )

    def test_inject_rejects_non_baseline_run(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        baseline = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        anomaly, _labels, _selected = inject(baseline.run_id)

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "not a BASELINE"):
            inject(anomaly.run_id)

    def test_evaluator_requires_run_scoped_ground_truth(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        baseline = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="phase5_outputs_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                validation_run, _scorecard = run_all_checks(baseline)
                with self.assertRaises(FileNotFoundError):
                    evaluate(validation_run)

FILE: tests\test_conflict_graph.py

import datetime as dt

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import Course, Enrollment, Exam, Student
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_exam_id, make_id
from apps.scheduling.services.conflict_graph import build_conflict_graph
from apps.scheduling.services.slot_allocator import color_exams
from generator.config import TERM


class ConflictGraphTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.students = [
            Student.objects.create(
                student_id=make_id("student", index),
                full_name=f"Student {index}",
                email=f"student{index}@example.test",
                department="CS",
                year_level=1,
            )
            for index in range(1, 4)
        ]
        self.exams = []
        self.courses = []
        for index in range(1, 4):
            course = Course.objects.create(
                course_id=make_id("course", index),
                course_code=f"CS{100 + index}",
                title=f"Course {index}",
                department="CS",
                credits=3,
            )
            self.courses.append(course)
            self.exams.append(
                Exam.objects.create(
                    exam_id=make_exam_id(index),
                    course=course,
                    title=f"Exam {index}",
                    term=TERM,
                    duration_minutes=120,
                    status=Exam.Status.PLANNED,
                )
            )

    def _enroll(self, index, student, course, *, status="ACTIVE", term=TERM):
        return Enrollment.objects.create(
            enrollment_id=make_id("enrollment", index),
            student=student,
            course=course,
            term=term,
            status=status,
            effective_from=dt.date(2026, 9, 1),
        )

    def test_edge_weights_count_shared_active_students(self):
        self._enroll(1, self.students[0], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(2, self.students[0], self.courses[1])
        self._enroll(3, self.students[1], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(4, self.students[1], self.courses[1])
        self._enroll(5, self.students[2], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(6, self.students[2], self.courses[2])
        graph, students_by_exam = build_conflict_graph(self.exams, TERM)

        self.assertEqual(graph.edge_weight(self.exams[0].exam_id, self.exams[1].exam_id), 2)
        self.assertEqual(graph.edge_weight(self.exams[0].exam_id, self.exams[2].exam_id), 1)
        self.assertEqual(graph.degree[self.exams[0].exam_id], 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(students_by_exam[self.exams[0].exam_id]), 3)

    def test_dropped_and_other_term_enrollments_do_not_create_edges(self):
        self._enroll(
            1, self.students[0], self.courses[0], status=Enrollment.Status.DROPPED
        )
        self._enroll(
            2, self.students[0], self.courses[1], term="2025-FALL"
        )
        graph, students_by_exam = build_conflict_graph(self.exams, TERM)
        self.assertEqual(graph.edge_weight(self.exams[0].exam_id, self.exams[1].exam_id), 0)
        self.assertEqual(students_by_exam[self.exams[0].exam_id], set())

    def test_dsatur_colors_conflicting_exams_into_different_time_groups(self):
        self._enroll(1, self.students[0], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(2, self.students[0], self.courses[1])
        self._enroll(3, self.students[1], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(4, self.students[1], self.courses[1])
        self._enroll(5, self.students[2], self.courses[0])
        self._enroll(6, self.students[2], self.courses[2])
        graph, students_by_exam = build_conflict_graph(self.exams, TERM)

        ordered, colors = color_exams(graph, students_by_exam)

        self.assertEqual(len(ordered), len(self.exams))
        for exam_id, neighbors in graph.adj.items():
            for neighbor_id in neighbors:
                self.assertNotEqual(colors[exam_id], colors[neighbor_id])

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import ExamRequirement, Student
from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.validation.services.data_validator import validate_master_data
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class DataValidatorTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")

    def test_clean_data_passes(self):
        summary = validate_master_data()
        self.assertTrue(summary["ok"], msg=summary["checks"])
        self.assertEqual(summary["failures"], 0)
        self.assertGreater(summary["counts"]["active_enrollments"], 0)

    def test_missing_requirement_is_detected(self):
        ExamRequirement.objects.first().delete()
        summary = validate_master_data()
        self.assertFalse(summary["ok"])
        self.assertGreater(
            next(
                check["failures"]
                for check in summary["checks"]
                if check["check"] == "exams_have_requirement"
            ),
            0,
        )

    def test_invalid_enum_value_is_detected(self):
        Student.objects.filter(student_id="STU-000001").update(status="INVALID")
        summary = validate_master_data()
        self.assertFalse(summary["ok"])
        self.assertGreater(
            next(
                check["failures"]
                for check in summary["checks"]
                if check["check"] == "invalid_enum_values"
            ),
            0,
        )

FILE: tests\test_exporter.py

import csv
import json
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.validation.services.exporter import (
    export_csv,
    export_json,
    export_sql,
    write_export_manifest,
)
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class ExporterTests(TestCase):
    def test_csv_json_sql_and_manifest_exports(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary_directory:
            base = Path(temporary_directory)
            export_csv(base / "csv")
            export_json(base / "json")
            export_sql(base / "sql")
            manifest = write_export_manifest(
                base, seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter"
            )

            with (base / "csv" / "students.csv").open(
                newline="", encoding="utf-8"
            ) as source:
                student_rows = list(csv.DictReader(source))
            self.assertEqual(len(student_rows), 200)

            exams = json.loads(
                (base / "json" / "exams.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )
            self.assertEqual(len(exams), 20)
            self.assertIn(
                "exam_requirement_equipment.json",
                [path.name for path in (base / "json").iterdir()],
            )
            self.assertIn("sql/seed.sql", manifest["files"])
            self.assertTrue((base / "README.txt").exists())
            self.assertTrue((base / "manifest.json").exists())

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_generator.py
