import re

from django.db.models import Count
from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Course,
    Enrollment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirement,
    Invigilator,
    Room,
    RoomCapacity,
    Student,
)
from apps.core.services.data_generator import (
    clear_generated_data,
    generate_and_persist,
)
from apps.scheduling.models import Availability, ScheduleEvent, SchedulerRun, TimeSlot
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class GeneratorTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.stats = generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")

    def test_starter_volumes_and_relationships(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.stats["students"], 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.stats["courses"], 30)
        self.assertEqual(self.stats["exams"], 20)
        self.assertEqual(self.stats["rooms"], 15)
        self.assertEqual(self.stats["invigilators"], 25)
        self.assertEqual(Student.objects.count(), 200)
        self.assertEqual(Course.objects.count(), 30)
        self.assertEqual(Exam.objects.count(), 20)
        self.assertEqual(Room.objects.count(), 15)
        self.assertEqual(Invigilator.objects.count(), 25)
        self.assertEqual(ExamRequirement.objects.count(), 20)
        self.assertEqual(TimeSlot.objects.count(), 40)
        self.assertEqual(RoomCapacity.objects.count(), 15)

    def test_ids_and_unique_course_codes(self):
        self.assertTrue(
            all(re.fullmatch(r"STU-\d{6}", value) for value in
                Student.objects.values_list("student_id", flat=True))
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            all(re.fullmatch(r"CRS-\d{6}", value) for value in
                Course.objects.values_list("course_id", flat=True))
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            all(re.fullmatch(r"EXAM-2026-\d{6}", value) for value in
                Exam.objects.values_list("exam_id", flat=True))
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            Course.objects.values("course_code").distinct().count(),
            Course.objects.count(),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            Exam.objects.values("course_id").distinct().count(),
            Exam.objects.count(),
        )

    def test_enrollment_loads_are_realistic_and_unique(self):
        loads = (
            Enrollment.objects.values("student_id")
            .annotate(total=Count("enrollment_id"))
        )
        self.assertEqual(loads.count(), Student.objects.count())
        self.assertTrue(all(4 <= row["total"] <= 6 for row in loads))
        self.assertEqual(
            Enrollment.objects.values(
                "student_id", "course_id", "term"
            ).distinct().count(),
            Enrollment.objects.count(),
        )

    def test_generated_domain_values_and_effective_dates(self):
        self.assertTrue(
            all(
                value in [60, 90, 120, 180]
                for value in Exam.objects.values_list(
                    "duration_minutes", flat=True
                )
            )
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            all(
                10 <= value <= 500
                for value in RoomCapacity.objects.values_list(
                    "exam_capacity", flat=True
                )
            )
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            all(
                (row["room_id"] is None) != (row["invigilator_id"] is None)
                for row in Availability.objects.values(
                    "room_id", "invigilator_id"
                )
            )
        )
        self.assertGreater(Availability.objects.count(), 0)
        self.assertGreater(TimeSlot.objects.count(), 0)
        self.assertGreater(Accommodation.objects.count(), 0)
        for accommodation in Accommodation.objects.all():
            if accommodation.accommodation_type == Accommodation.Type.EXTRA_TIME:
                self.assertIn(accommodation.extra_time_percent, [25, 50, 100])
            else:
                self.assertEqual(accommodation.extra_time_percent, 0)

    def test_invalid_scale_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="unknown")

    def test_clear_refuses_to_delete_append_only_schedule_history(self):
        run = SchedulerRun.objects.create(run_id="RUN-000001")
        ScheduleEvent.objects.create(
            event_id="EVT-000001",
            run=run,
            exam=Exam.objects.first(),
            event_type=ScheduleEvent.EventType.UNASSIGNED,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            clear_generated_data()
        self.assertEqual(Student.objects.count(), 200)
        self.assertEqual(ScheduleEvent.objects.count(), 1)

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_ids.py
======================================================================
from django.test import SimpleTestCase

from apps.core.services.id_generator import is_valid, make_assignment_id, make_exam_id, make_id


class IdGeneratorTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def test_zero_padded_formats(self):
        self.assertEqual(make_id("student", 1), "STU-000001")
        self.assertEqual(make_id("room", 100), "ROOM-000100")
        self.assertEqual(make_id("invigilator", 200), "INV-000200")
        self.assertEqual(make_exam_id(1), "EXAM-2026-000001")

    def test_assignment_id_includes_exam_room_run(self):
        self.assertEqual(
            make_assignment_id("EXAM-2026-000001", "ROOM-000001", "RUN-000001"),
            "ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000001-RUN-000001",
        )

    def test_boundaries(self):
        self.assertEqual(make_id("student", 999999), "STU-999999")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            make_id("student", 0)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            make_id("student", 1000000)

    def test_validation_accepts_and_rejects(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_valid("student", "STU-000001"))
        self.assertFalse(is_valid("student", "STU-1"))
        self.assertFalse(is_valid("student", "stu-000001"))
        self.assertFalse(is_valid("student", "STU-0000011"))
        self.assertTrue(is_valid("assignment", "ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000001-RUN-000001"))
        self.assertFalse(is_valid("assignment", "ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000001"))
        self.assertFalse(is_valid("exam", None))

    def test_ids_are_deterministic(self):
        self.assertEqual([make_id("course", i) for i in range(1, 4)], [make_id("course", i) for i in range(1, 4)])

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_invigilator_allocator.py
======================================================================
import datetime as dt

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import Course, Exam, Invigilator, Room, Student
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_exam_id, make_id
from apps.scheduling.models import UnassignedExam
from apps.scheduling.services.context import SchedulingContext
from apps.scheduling.services.invigilator_allocator import allocate_invigilators
from apps.scheduling.services.slot_allocator import buffered_overlap


UTC = dt.timezone.utc


class InvigilatorAllocatorTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        course = Course.objects.create(
            course_id=make_id("course", 1),
            course_code="CS101",
            title="Course",
            department="CS",
            credits=3,
        )
        self.exam = Exam.objects.create(
            exam_id=make_exam_id(1),
            course=course,
            title="Exam",
            term="2026-FALL",
            duration_minutes=120,
            status=Exam.Status.PLANNED,
        )
        self.room = Room.objects.create(
            room_id=make_id("room", 1),
            building="Block A",
            room_number="A-001",
            room_type=Room.RoomType.CLASSROOM,
        )
        self.student = Student.objects.create(
            student_id=make_id("student", 1),
            full_name="Student",
            email="student@example.test",
            department="CS",
            year_level=1,
        )
        self.invigilator = Invigilator.objects.create(
            invigilator_id=make_id("invigilator", 1),
            full_name="Invigilator",
            email="invigilator@example.test",
            max_daily_hours=2,
        )
        self.start = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 8, tzinfo=UTC)
        self.end = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 10, tzinfo=UTC)

    def context(self, availability, policy=None):
        return SchedulingContext(
            run=type("Run", (), {"run_id": "RUN-000001"})(),
            term="2026-FALL",
            exams=[],
            graph=None,
            students_by_exam={},
            invigilators=[self.invigilator],
            invigilator_avails={
                self.invigilator.invigilator_id: availability
            },
            exam_requirements={},
            policy=policy,
        )

    def test_exact_availability_window_is_sufficient(self):
        context = self.context([(self.start, self.end)])
        assignments, reason = allocate_invigilators(
            context,
            self.exam,
            [(self.room, [self.student])],
            self.start,
            self.end,
        )
        self.assertIsNone(reason)
        self.assertEqual(assignments[0][1], [self.invigilator])

    def test_unavailable_invigilator_has_reason_code(self):
        context = self.context([])
        assignments, reason = allocate_invigilators(
            context,
            self.exam,
            [(self.room, [self.student])],
            self.start,
            self.end,
        )
        self.assertIsNone(assignments)
        self.assertEqual(reason, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_INVIGILATOR)

    def test_daily_hours_limit_is_respected(self):
        context = self.context([(self.start, self.end)])
        context.invigilator_bookings[self.invigilator.invigilator_id].append(
            (
                dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 10, tzinfo=UTC),
                dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 11, tzinfo=UTC),
            )
        )
        assignments, reason = allocate_invigilators(
            context,
            self.exam,
            [(self.room, [self.student])],
            self.start,
            self.end,
        )
        self.assertIsNone(assignments)
        self.assertEqual(reason, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_INVIGILATOR)

    def test_hard_buffer_is_required_between_invigilator_assignments(self):
        candidate_start = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 10, 15, tzinfo=UTC)
        self.assertTrue(
            buffered_overlap(
                self.start,
                self.end,
                candidate_start,
                dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 11, tzinfo=UTC),
                30,
            )
        )
        self.assertFalse(
            buffered_overlap(
                self.start,
                self.end,
                candidate_start,
                dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 11, tzinfo=UTC),
                15,
            )
        )

    def test_consecutive_hours_limit_is_respected(self):
        candidate_start = self.end
        exact_limit_end = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 12, tzinfo=UTC)
        candidate_end = dt.datetime(2026, 12, 7, 12, 30, tzinfo=UTC)
        self.invigilator.max_daily_hours = 8
        self.invigilator.save(update_fields=["max_daily_hours"])
        exact_limit_context = self.context(
            [(self.start, exact_limit_end)],
            policy={"MAX_CONSECUTIVE_INVIGILATOR_HOURS": 4},
        )
        exact_limit_context.invigilator_bookings[
            self.invigilator.invigilator_id
        ].append((self.start, self.end))
        assignments, reason = allocate_invigilators(
            exact_limit_context,
            self.exam,
            [(self.room, [self.student])],
            candidate_start,
            exact_limit_end,
        )
        self.assertIsNone(reason)
        self.assertEqual(assignments[0][1], [self.invigilator])

        context = self.context(
            [(self.start, candidate_end)],
            policy={"MAX_CONSECUTIVE_INVIGILATOR_HOURS": 4},
        )
        context.invigilator_bookings[self.invigilator.invigilator_id].append(
            (self.start, self.end)
        )

        assignments, reason = allocate_invigilators(
            context,
            self.exam,
            [(self.room, [self.student])],
            candidate_start,
            candidate_end,
        )

        self.assertIsNone(assignments)
        self.assertEqual(reason, UnassignedExam.Reason.NO_INVIGILATOR)

FILE: tests\test_metrics.py

import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.test import TestCase, override_settings

from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from apps.validation.services.metrics import compute_business_metrics
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class BusinessMetricsTests(TestCase):
    def test_metrics_reconcile_baseline_assignments_and_unassigned_exams(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="phase5_metrics_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                metrics = compute_business_metrics(run)

        self.assertEqual(
            metrics["total_exams"],
            metrics["scheduled_exams"] + metrics["unassigned_exams"],
        )
        self.assertEqual(metrics["term_exam_count"], 20)
        self.assertEqual(metrics["scheduling_coverage"], 0.75)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(metrics["student_allocation_rate"], 1.0)
        self.assertTrue(
            metrics["reconciliation_ok"],
            msg=metrics["reconciliation_errors"],
        )
        for metric in (
            "room_seat_utilization",
            "room_time_utilization",
            "invigilator_utilization",
        ):
            self.assertGreaterEqual(metrics[metric], 0.0)

import datetime as dt

from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import IntegrityError, transaction
from django.db.models.deletion import ProtectedError
from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import Accommodation, Enrollment, Exam, Room, RoomCapacity, Student
from apps.core.services.id_generator import make_id
from apps.core.services.lifecycle import InvalidTransition, can_transition, transition
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability, InvigilatorAssignment, RoomClosure, ScheduleAssignment, ScheduleEvent,
    StudentRoomAllocation, UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun

from .factories import (
    D, dtm, make_accommodation, make_assignment, make_capacity, make_course, make_enrollment,
    make_exam, make_invigilator, make_room, make_run, make_student,
)


class PrimaryKeyTests(TestCase):
    def test_duplicate_primary_key_rejected(self):
        make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_student(1, email="other@university.example")

    def test_bad_id_format_rejected_by_full_clean(self):
        s = Student(student_id="STU-1", full_name="x", email="a@b.example", department="CS", year_level=1)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError) as ctx:
            s.full_clean()
        self.assertIn("student_id", ctx.exception.message_dict)

    def test_duplicate_email_rejected(self):
        make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_student(2, email="stu000001@university.example")


class ForeignKeyTests(TestCase):
    def test_enrollment_with_missing_student_fails_validation(self):
        course = make_course(1)
        e = Enrollment(enrollment_id="ENR-000001", student_id="STU-999999", course=course,
                       term="2026-FALL", effective_from=D)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            e.full_clean()

    def test_cannot_delete_student_with_enrollment(self):
        s, c = make_student(1), make_course(1)
        make_enrollment(1, s, c)
        with self.assertRaises(ProtectedError):
            s.delete()

    def test_cannot_delete_room_with_assignment(self):
        exam, room, run = make_exam(1), make_room(1), make_run(1)
        make_assignment(exam, room, run)
        with self.assertRaises(ProtectedError):
            room.delete()


class EnrollmentTests(TestCase):
    def test_duplicate_enrollment_rejected(self):
        s, c = make_student(1), make_course(1)
        make_enrollment(1, s, c)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_enrollment(2, s, c)

    def test_same_course_different_term_allowed(self):
        s, c = make_student(1), make_course(1)
        make_enrollment(1, s, c)
        make_enrollment(2, s, c, term="2027-SPRING")
        self.assertEqual(Enrollment.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_end_date_before_start_rejected_by_database(self):
        s, c = make_student(1), make_course(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_enrollment(1, s, c, effective_to=D - dt.timedelta(days=1))

    def test_end_date_equal_start_allowed_boundary(self):
        s, c = make_student(1), make_course(1)
        make_enrollment(1, s, c, effective_to=D)

    def test_inactive_student_cannot_have_active_enrollment(self):
        s, c = make_student(1, status="INACTIVE"), make_course(1)
        e = Enrollment(enrollment_id="ENR-000001", student=s, course=c, term="2026-FALL", effective_from=D)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            e.full_clean()


class ExamTests(TestCase):
    def test_duration_boundaries(self):
        for n, minutes in enumerate([30, 240], start=1):
            make_exam(n, duration_minutes=minutes).full_clean()

    def test_invalid_durations_rejected(self):
        for n, minutes in enumerate([0, 29, 241, 100], start=1):
            course = make_course(n)
            exam = Exam(exam_id=f"EXAM-2026-{n:06d}", course=course, title="t", term="2026-FALL",
                        duration_minutes=minutes)
            with self.assertRaises(ValidationError, msg=f"{minutes} should fail"):
                exam.full_clean()

    def test_database_blocks_out_of_range_duration(self):
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_exam(1, duration_minutes=500)

    def test_one_exam_per_course_per_term(self):
        c = make_course(1)
        make_exam(1, course=c)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_exam(2, course=c)

    def test_exam_id_year_must_match_term(self):
        c = make_course(1)
        exam = Exam(exam_id="EXAM-2025-000001", course=c, title="t", term="2026-FALL", duration_minutes=60)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            exam.full_clean()


class RoomCapacityTests(TestCase):
    def test_capacity_boundaries(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        for n, cap in enumerate([10, 500], start=1):
            RoomCapacity(capacity_id=f"CAP-{n:06d}", room=make_room(n + 1), exam_capacity=cap,
                         effective_from=D).full_clean()

    def test_invalid_capacity_rejected(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        for cap in (0, 9, 501):
            rc = RoomCapacity(capacity_id="CAP-000001", room=room, exam_capacity=cap, effective_from=D)
            with self.assertRaises(ValidationError, msg=f"{cap} should fail"):
                rc.full_clean()

    def test_overlapping_capacity_ranges_rejected(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        make_capacity(1, room, 50, effective_from=D, effective_to=D + dt.timedelta(days=10))
        overlap = RoomCapacity(capacity_id="CAP-000002", room=room, exam_capacity=60,
                               effective_from=D + dt.timedelta(days=10))
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            overlap.full_clean()

    def test_adjacent_capacity_ranges_allowed(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        make_capacity(1, room, 50, effective_from=D, effective_to=D + dt.timedelta(days=10))
        ok = RoomCapacity(capacity_id="CAP-000002", room=room, exam_capacity=60,
                          effective_from=D + dt.timedelta(days=11))
        ok.full_clean()


class AvailabilityAndClosureTests(TestCase):
    def test_exactly_one_target_required(self):
        room, inv = make_room(1), make_invigilator(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000001", start_at=dtm(hour=8), end_at=dtm(hour=12))
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000002", room=room, invigilator=inv,
                                        start_at=dtm(hour=8), end_at=dtm(hour=12))

    def test_valid_room_and_invigilator_availability(self):
        room, inv = make_room(1), make_invigilator(1)
        Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000001", room=room, start_at=dtm(hour=8), end_at=dtm(hour=12))
        Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000002", invigilator=inv, start_at=dtm(hour=8), end_at=dtm(hour=12))
        self.assertEqual(Availability.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_invalid_date_range_rejected(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000001", room=room, start_at=dtm(hour=12), end_at=dtm(hour=8))
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Availability.objects.create(availability_id="AVL-000002", room=room, start_at=dtm(hour=8), end_at=dtm(hour=8))

    def test_closure_end_before_start_rejected(self):
        room = make_room(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            RoomClosure.objects.create(closure_id="CLS-000001", room=room, start_at=dtm(hour=12),
                                       end_at=dtm(hour=8), reason="REPAIR")


class AccommodationTests(TestCase):
    def test_valid_extra_time_percentages(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        for n, pct in enumerate([25, 50, 100], start=1):
            make_accommodation(n, s, extra_time_percent=pct)
        self.assertEqual(Accommodation.objects.count(), 3)

    def test_invalid_extra_time_percentage_rejected(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_accommodation(1, s, extra_time_percent=30)

    def test_extra_time_needs_percent(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_accommodation(1, s, extra_time_percent=0)

    def test_percent_not_allowed_on_room_accommodation(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_accommodation(1, s, accommodation_type="SEPARATE_ROOM", extra_time_percent=25)
        make_accommodation(2, s, accommodation_type="ACCESSIBLE_ROOM", extra_time_percent=0)

    def test_expiry_before_start_rejected(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_accommodation(1, s, effective_to=D - dt.timedelta(days=1))


class AssignmentTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.exam, self.room, self.run = make_exam(1), make_room(1), make_run(1)

    def test_assignment_id_must_match_its_parts(self):
        a = ScheduleAssignment(assignment_id="ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000002-RUN-000001", run=self.run,
                               exam=self.exam, room=self.room, start_at=dtm(hour=9), end_at=dtm(hour=11),
                               allocated_count=10)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            a.full_clean()

    def test_valid_assignment_passes_full_clean(self):
        a = make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run)
        a.full_clean()
        self.assertEqual(a.assignment_id, "ASN-EXAM-2026-000001-ROOM-000001-RUN-000001")

    def test_end_must_be_after_start(self):
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run, start_at=dtm(hour=11), end_at=dtm(hour=9))

    def test_allocated_count_must_be_positive(self):
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run, allocated_count=0)

    def test_same_exam_room_allowed_in_different_runs_history_kept(self):
        run2 = make_run(2)
        make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run)
        make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, run2)
        self.assertEqual(ScheduleAssignment.objects.count(), 2)

    def test_student_cannot_sit_exam_in_two_rooms_in_same_run(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        room2 = make_room(2)
        a1 = make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run)
        a2 = make_assignment(self.exam, room2, self.run)
        StudentRoomAllocation.objects.create(allocation_id="SRA-000001", assignment=a1, run=self.run,
                                             exam=self.exam, student=s, required_minutes=120)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.create(allocation_id="SRA-000002", assignment=a2, run=self.run,
                                                 exam=self.exam, student=s, required_minutes=120)

    def test_allocation_must_match_assignment_exam_and_run(self):
        s = make_student(1)
        other_exam = make_exam(2)
        a = make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run)
        alloc = StudentRoomAllocation(allocation_id="SRA-000001", assignment=a, run=self.run,
                                      exam=other_exam, student=s, required_minutes=120)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            alloc.full_clean()

    def test_invigilator_cannot_be_added_twice_to_same_assignment(self):
        inv = make_invigilator(1)
        a = make_assignment(self.exam, self.room, self.run)
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.create(ias_id="IAS-000001", assignment=a, invigilator=inv)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            InvigilatorAssignment.objects.create(ias_id="IAS-000002", assignment=a, invigilator=inv)

    def test_one_unassigned_record_per_exam_per_run(self):
        UnassignedExam.objects.create(unassigned_id="UNA-000001", run=self.run, exam=self.exam,
                                      primary_reason_code="NO_ROOM_CAPACITY")
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            UnassignedExam.objects.create(unassigned_id="UNA-000002", run=self.run, exam=self.exam,
                                          primary_reason_code="NO_INVIGILATOR")

    def test_invalid_reason_code_rejected_by_full_clean(self):
        u = UnassignedExam(unassigned_id="UNA-000001", run=self.run, exam=self.exam, primary_reason_code="BOGUS")
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            u.full_clean()


class AppendOnlyEventTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.run, self.exam = make_run(1), make_exam(1)
        self.event = ScheduleEvent.objects.create(event_id="EVT-000001", run=self.run, exam=self.exam,
                                                  event_type="UNASSIGNED", detail={"why": "test"})

    def test_event_cannot_be_modified(self):
        self.event.detail = {"why": "changed"}
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
            self.event.save()

    def test_event_cannot_be_deleted(self):
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
            self.event.delete()

    def test_queryset_update_and_delete_blocked(self):
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
            ScheduleEvent.objects.all().update(event_type="MOVED")
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
            ScheduleEvent.objects.all().delete()
        self.assertEqual(ScheduleEvent.objects.get(pk="EVT-000001").detail, {"why": "test"})

    def test_new_events_can_still_be_appended(self):
        ScheduleEvent.objects.create(event_id="EVT-000002", run=self.run, event_type="ASSIGNED")
        self.assertEqual(ScheduleEvent.objects.count(), 2)


class LifecycleTests(TestCase):
    def test_exam_valid_path(self):
        exam = make_exam(1)
        transition(exam, "PLANNED")
        transition(exam, "SCHEDULED")
        exam.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(exam.status, "SCHEDULED")

    def test_exam_cannot_skip_planning(self):
        exam = make_exam(1)
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidTransition):
            transition(exam, "SCHEDULED")
        exam.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(exam.status, "DRAFT")

    def test_assignment_terminal_states(self):
        a = make_assignment(make_exam(1), make_room(1), make_run(1))
        transition(a, "CANCELLED")
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidTransition):
            transition(a, "ASSIGNED")

    def test_exam_can_be_reset_for_rerun(self):
        self.assertTrue(can_transition("Exam", "UNASSIGNED", "PLANNED"))
        self.assertTrue(can_transition("Exam", "SCHEDULED", "PLANNED"))

    def test_dropped_enrollment_cannot_be_reactivated(self):
        e = make_enrollment(1, make_student(1), make_course(1))
        transition(e, "DROPPED")
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidTransition):
            transition(e, "ACTIVE")

    def test_run_lifecycle(self):
        run = make_run(1)
        transition(run, "COMPLETED")
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidTransition):
            transition(run, "RUNNING")


class ValidationModelTests(TestCase):
    def test_violation_links_to_validation_run(self):
        run = make_run(1)
        v = ValidationRun.objects.create(validation_id="VAL-000001", scheduler_run=run)
        DetectedViolation.objects.create(violation_id="VIO-000001", validation_run=v,
                                         violation_code="ROOM_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED", entity_id="ROOM-000001")
        self.assertEqual(v.violations.count(), 1)

    def test_invalid_violation_code_rejected(self):
        run = make_run(1)
        v = ValidationRun.objects.create(validation_id="VAL-000001", scheduler_run=run)
        bad = DetectedViolation(violation_id="VIO-000001", validation_run=v, violation_code="NOPE")
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            bad.full_clean()


class EnumTests(TestCase):
    def test_invalid_enums_rejected_by_full_clean(self):
        s = Student(student_id="STU-000001", full_name="x", email="a@b.example", department="XX",
                    year_level=1, status="ACTIVE")
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            s.full_clean()
        s2 = Student(student_id="STU-000002", full_name="x", email="b@b.example", department="CS",
                     year_level=5)
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            s2.full_clean()

        make_room(1)
        with self.assertRaises(IntegrityError), transaction.atomic():
            Room.objects.create(room_id="ROOM-000099", building="Block A", room_number="A-101", room_type="CLASSROOM")

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_reproducibility.py

from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import Student
from apps.validation.services.reproducibility import (
    canonical_checksum,
    verify_reproducibility,
)
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class ReproducibilityTests(TestCase):
    def test_same_seed_has_same_checksum_and_row_counts(self):
        result = verify_reproducibility(
            seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter"
        )
        self.assertTrue(result["match"])
        self.assertEqual(result["checksum_run_1"], result["checksum_run_2"])
        self.assertEqual(
            result["row_counts_run_1"], result["row_counts_run_2"]
        )

    def test_different_seed_has_different_checksum(self):
        first = verify_reproducibility(
            seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter"
        )
        second = verify_reproducibility(seed=99999999, scale="starter")
        self.assertNotEqual(
            first["checksum_run_1"], second["checksum_run_1"]
        )

    def test_verification_leaves_existing_database_unchanged(self):
        initial_count = Student.objects.count()
        initial_checksum = canonical_checksum()
        verify_reproducibility(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        self.assertEqual(Student.objects.count(), initial_count)
        self.assertEqual(canonical_checksum(), initial_checksum)

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_scheduler.py
======================================================================
from collections import defaultdict

from django.db.models import Count, Q
from django.test import TestCase

from apps.core.models import (
    Accommodation,
    Enrollment,
    Exam,
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    RoomCapacity,
    RoomEquipment,
)
from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    RoomClosure,
    ScheduleAssignment,
    ScheduleEvent,
    SchedulerRun,
    StudentRoomAllocation,
    UnassignedExam,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT, TERM


class SchedulerTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")

    def test_run_schedules_exams_and_persists_allocation_rows(self):
        run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        self.assertEqual(run.status, SchedulerRun.Status.COMPLETED)
        statuses = set(
            Exam.objects.filter(term=TERM).values_list("status", flat=True)
        )
        self.assertLessEqual(
            statuses, {Exam.Status.SCHEDULED, Exam.Status.UNASSIGNED}
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            Exam.objects.filter(
                term=TERM,
                status__in=[Exam.Status.SCHEDULED, Exam.Status.UNASSIGNED],
            ).count(),
            Exam.objects.filter(term=TERM).count(),
        )
        self.assertGreater(ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=run).count(), 0)
        self.assertGreater(
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run).count(), 0
        )
        self.assertGreater(
            InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
                assignment__run=run
            ).count(),
            0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            ScheduleEvent.objects.filter(run=run).count(),
            Exam.objects.filter(term=TERM).count(),
        )

    def test_allocations_reconcile_with_active_enrollments(self):
        run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        allocated = dict(
            StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(run=run)
            .values("exam_id")
            .annotate(count=Count("allocation_id"))
            .values_list("exam_id", "count")
        )
        enrolled = dict(
            Enrollment.objects.filter(
                term=TERM, status=Enrollment.Status.ACTIVE
            )
            .values("course__exams__exam_id")
            .annotate(count=Count("enrollment_id"))
            .values_list("course__exams__exam_id", "count")
        )
        scheduled_exam_ids = set(
            Exam.objects.filter(
                term=TERM, status=Exam.Status.SCHEDULED
            ).values_list("exam_id", flat=True)
        )
        unassigned_exam_ids = set(
            UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run).values_list(
                "exam_id", flat=True
            )
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            len(scheduled_exam_ids) + len(unassigned_exam_ids),
            Exam.objects.filter(term=TERM).count(),
        )
        self.assertFalse(scheduled_exam_ids & unassigned_exam_ids)
        for exam_id in scheduled_exam_ids:
            self.assertEqual(
                allocated.get(exam_id, 0), enrolled.get(exam_id, 0), msg=exam_id
            )
        self.assertTrue(
            set(allocated).issubset(scheduled_exam_ids),
            "Unassigned exams must not retain partial student allocations.",
        )

    def test_unassigned_rows_always_include_reason_and_evidence(self):
        run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        for row in UnassignedExam.objects.filter(run=run):
            self.assertIn(
                row.primary_reason_code,
                {choice.value for choice in UnassignedExam.Reason},
            )
            self.assertTrue(row.evidence)

    def test_rerun_preserves_previous_history_and_is_deterministic(self):
        first_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        first_assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=first_run)
            .order_by("exam_id", "room_id")
            .values_list("exam_id", "room_id", "start_at", "end_at")
        )
        first_events = ScheduleEvent.objects.filter(run=first_run).count()

        second_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        second_assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=second_run)
            .order_by("exam_id", "room_id")
            .values_list("exam_id", "room_id", "start_at", "end_at")
        )
        self.assertEqual(first_assignments, second_assignments)
        self.assertEqual(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=first_run).count(),
            len(first_assignments),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            ScheduleEvent.objects.filter(run=first_run).count(), first_events
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first_run.run_id, second_run.run_id)

    def test_schedule_obeys_room_staff_student_and_accommodation_constraints(self):
        run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        room_intervals = defaultdict(list)
        staff_intervals = defaultdict(list)
        student_intervals = defaultdict(list)

        for assignment in ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=run):
            room = assignment.room
            start_at, end_at = assignment.start_at, assignment.end_at
            room_intervals[room.room_id].append((start_at, end_at))

            capacity = RoomCapacity.objects.filter(
                room=room,
                effective_from__lte=start_at.date(),
            ).filter(
                Q(effective_to__isnull=True)
                | Q(effective_to__gte=start_at.date())
            ).first()
            self.assertIsNotNone(capacity)
            self.assertLessEqual(
                assignment.allocated_count, capacity.exam_capacity
            )
            self.assertTrue(
                set(
                    ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
                        requirement__exam=assignment.exam
                    ).values_list("equipment_id", flat=True)
                ).issubset(
                    set(
                        RoomEquipment.objects.filter(room=room).values_list(
                            "equipment_id", flat=True
                        )
                    )
                )
            )
            self.assertTrue(
                Availability.objects.filter(
                    room=room,
                    start_at__lte=start_at,
                    end_at__gte=end_at,
                ).exists()
            )
            self.assertFalse(
                RoomClosure.objects.filter(
                    room=room,
                    start_at__lt=end_at,
                    end_at__gt=start_at,
                ).exists()
            )

            allocations = list(
                StudentRoomAllocation.objects.filter(
                    assignment=assignment
                ).select_related("student")
            )
            self.assertEqual(len(allocations), assignment.allocated_count)
            for allocation in allocations:
                self.assertGreaterEqual(
                    (end_at - start_at).total_seconds() / 60,
                    allocation.required_minutes,
                )
                student_intervals[allocation.student_id].append(
                    (start_at, end_at)
                )

            exam_date = start_at.date()
            accommodations_by_student = {
                allocation.student_id: list(
                    Accommodation.objects.filter(
                        student_id=allocation.student_id,
                        status=Accommodation.Status.ACTIVE,
                        effective_from__lte=exam_date,
                    ).filter(
                        Q(effective_to__isnull=True)
                        | Q(effective_to__gte=exam_date)
                    )
                )
                for allocation in allocations
            }
            if any(
                any(
                    accommodation.accommodation_type
                    == Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM
                    for accommodation in student_accommodations
                )
                for student_accommodations in accommodations_by_student.values()
            ):
                self.assertTrue(
                    all(
                        any(
                            accommodation.accommodation_type
                            == Accommodation.Type.SEPARATE_ROOM
                            for accommodation in student_accommodations
                        )
                        for student_accommodations
                        in accommodations_by_student.values()
                    )
                )
            if any(
                any(
                    accommodation.accommodation_type
                    == Accommodation.Type.ACCESSIBLE_ROOM
                    for accommodation in student_accommodations
                )
                for student_accommodations in accommodations_by_student.values()
            ):
                self.assertTrue(room.is_accessible)

            for inv_assignment in InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
                assignment=assignment
            ).select_related("invigilator"):
                self.assertTrue(
                    Availability.objects.filter(
                        invigilator=inv_assignment.invigilator,
                        start_at__lte=start_at,
                        end_at__gte=end_at,
                    ).exists()
                )
                staff_intervals[
                    inv_assignment.invigilator_id
                ].append((start_at, end_at))

        for intervals_by_resource in (
            room_intervals,
            staff_intervals,
            student_intervals,
        ):
            for intervals in intervals_by_resource.values():
                intervals.sort()
                for first, second in zip(intervals, intervals[1:]):
                    self.assertLessEqual(first[1], second[0])

    def test_failed_run_type_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            run_scheduler(run_type="INVALID")

======================================================================
FILE: tests\test_schedule_validator.py
======================================================================
import datetime as dt
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.test import TestCase, override_settings

from apps.core.models import (
    ExamRequirementEquipment,
    Invigilator,
    RoomEquipment,
)
from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.scheduling.models import (
    Availability,
    InvigilatorAssignment,
    ScheduleAssignment,
)
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from apps.validation.models import DetectedViolation, ValidationRun
from apps.validation.services import schedule_validator
from apps.validation.services.validation_runner import run_all_checks
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class ScheduleValidatorTests(TestCase):
    def test_baseline_schedule_passes_all_independent_checks(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        scheduler_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="schedule_scorecard_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                validation_run, scorecard = run_all_checks(scheduler_run)

        self.assertEqual(validation_run.status, ValidationRun.Status.PASSED)
        self.assertEqual(scorecard["failures"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(scorecard["checks"]), 10)
        self.assertTrue(
            all(check["status"] == "PASS" for check in scorecard["checks"])
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            DetectedViolation.objects.filter(
                validation_run=validation_run
            ).count(),
            0,
        )

    def test_validation_run_is_persisted_with_finish_time(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        scheduler_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        before = ValidationRun.objects.count()

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="schedule_scorecard_") as folder:
            with override_settings(BASE_DIR=Path(folder)):
                validation_run, _scorecard = run_all_checks(scheduler_run)

        self.assertEqual(ValidationRun.objects.count(), before + 1)
        self.assertIsNotNone(validation_run.finished_at)

    def test_independent_checks_detect_persisted_row_corruption(self):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        scheduler_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        assignments = list(
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=scheduler_run)
            .select_related("exam", "room")
            .order_by("assignment_id")
        )

        equipment_assignment = next(
            assignment
            for assignment in assignments
            if ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
                requirement__exam_id=assignment.exam_id
            ).exists()
        )
        equipment_id = ExamRequirementEquipment.objects.filter(
            requirement__exam_id=equipment_assignment.exam_id
        ).values_list("equipment_id", flat=True).first()
        RoomEquipment.objects.filter(
            room_id=equipment_assignment.room_id,
            equipment_id=equipment_id,
        ).delete()
        violations, _count = schedule_validator.check_missing_equipment(
            scheduler_run
        )
        self.assertIn(
            equipment_assignment.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

        room_assignment = assignments[0]
        Availability.objects.filter(room_id=room_assignment.room_id).delete()
        violations, _count = schedule_validator.check_room_unavailable(
            scheduler_run
        )
        self.assertIn(
            room_assignment.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

        allocation_assignment = ScheduleAssignment.objects.get(
            pk=room_assignment.assignment_id
        )
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            pk=allocation_assignment.pk
        ).update(allocated_count=allocation_assignment.allocated_count + 1)
        violations, _count = (
            schedule_validator.check_student_allocation_mismatch(
                scheduler_run
            )
        )
        self.assertIn(
            allocation_assignment.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

        invalid_end = allocation_assignment.start_at + dt.timedelta(
            minutes=allocation_assignment.exam.duration_minutes - 15
        )
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            pk=allocation_assignment.pk
        ).update(end_at=invalid_end)
        violations, _count = schedule_validator.check_invalid_duration(
            scheduler_run
        )
        self.assertIn(
            allocation_assignment.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

        room_pair = next(
            (
                (first, second)
                for index, first in enumerate(assignments)
                for second in assignments[index + 1:]
                if first.exam_id != second.exam_id
                and first.room_id != second.room_id
                and not ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
                    run=scheduler_run,
                    exam_id=second.exam_id,
                    room_id=first.room_id,
                ).exists()
            ),
            None,
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(room_pair)
        first, second = room_pair
        ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(
            pk=second.assignment_id
        ).update(
            room_id=first.room_id,
            start_at=first.start_at,
            end_at=first.end_at,
        )
        violations, _count = schedule_validator.check_room_double_booking(
            scheduler_run
        )
        self.assertIn(
            second.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

        inv_assignment = InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            assignment__run=scheduler_run
        ).select_related("assignment").first()
        unavailable_invigilator = next(
            (
                invigilator
                for invigilator in Invigilator.objects.filter(
                    status=Invigilator.Status.ACTIVE
                )
                if not InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
                    assignment=inv_assignment.assignment,
                    invigilator=invigilator,
                ).exists()
                and not Availability.objects.filter(
                    invigilator=invigilator,
                    start_at__lte=inv_assignment.assignment.start_at,
                    end_at__gte=inv_assignment.assignment.end_at,
                ).exists()
            ),
            None,
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(unavailable_invigilator)
        InvigilatorAssignment.objects.filter(
            pk=inv_assignment.pk
        ).update(invigilator=unavailable_invigilator)
        violations, _count = (
            schedule_validator.check_invigilator_availability(
                scheduler_run
            )
        )
        self.assertIn(
            inv_assignment.assignment_id,
            {item["assignment"].assignment_id for item in violations},
        )

import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.contrib.auth.models import Group, User
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.test import Client, TestCase, override_settings
from django.urls import reverse

from apps.core.services.data_generator import generate_and_persist
from apps.scheduling.models import ScheduleAssignment
from apps.scheduling.services.scheduler import run_scheduler
from generator.config import SEED_DEFAULT


class PublicPagesTests(TestCase):
    def test_dashboard_loads_anonymously(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:home"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Overview")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Workflow actions")

    def test_login_page_loads(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("login"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Sign in")

    def test_timetable_shows_empty_state_without_run(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:timetable"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "needs a scheduler run")

    def test_admin_login_page_loads(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/admin/login/").status_code, 200)


class PopulatedPagesTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        cls.scheduler_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)
        cls.assignment = (
            ScheduleAssignment.objects.filter(run=cls.scheduler_run)
            .order_by("start_at")
            .first()
        )

    def test_dashboard_shows_latest_run_and_business_metrics(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:home"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, self.scheduler_run.run_id)
        self.assertContains(response, "Business metrics")

    def test_timetable_lists_assigned_exam(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:timetable"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, self.assignment.exam_id)

    def test_timetable_filter_by_exam_id(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("dashboard:timetable"), {"exam": self.assignment.exam_id}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, self.assignment.exam_id)

    def test_timetable_filter_with_invalid_date_is_safe(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("dashboard:timetable"), {"date": "not-a-date"}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Enter a valid date")

    def test_exam_detail_renders_assignment_explanation(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("dashboard:exam_detail", args=[self.assignment.exam_id])
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Why this assignment?")

    def test_exam_detail_unknown_id_returns_not_found_page(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse(
                "dashboard:exam_detail",
                args=["EXAM-9999-000001"],
            )
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "No record found")

    def test_student_lookup_returns_enrollments(self):
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("dashboard:student_lookup"), {"q": "STU-000001"}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Enrollments")

    def test_student_lookup_accepts_email(self):
        from apps.core.models import Student

        email = Student.objects.get(pk="STU-000001").email
        response = self.client.get(
            reverse("dashboard:student_lookup"), {"q": email}
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Personal timetable")

    def test_exceptions_page_loads(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:exceptions"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)

    def test_validation_page_loads(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:validation"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Validation scorecard")

    def test_conflict_graph_page_loads(self):
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:conflict_graph"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Conflict graph")


class ActionPermissionTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        generate_and_persist(seed=SEED_DEFAULT, scale="starter")
        cls.scheduler_run = run_scheduler(seed=SEED_DEFAULT)

    def setUp(self):
        self.output_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="dashboard_test_")
        self.settings_override = override_settings(
            BASE_DIR=Path(self.output_dir.name)
        )
        self.settings_override.enable()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.settings_override.disable()
        self.output_dir.cleanup()

    def test_anonymous_cannot_run_scheduler(self):
        response = self.client.post(reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn("/accounts/login/", response.url)

    def test_non_staff_user_cannot_run_scheduler(self):
        user = User.objects.create_user(username="alice", password="test-pass-123")
        self.client.force_login(user)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "requires the Academic Administrator role")
        self.assertNotContains(response, "Workflow actions")

    def test_action_is_post_only(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-get", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 405)

    def test_action_requires_csrf_token(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-csrf", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        csrf_client = Client(enforce_csrf_checks=True)
        csrf_client.force_login(staff)
        response = csrf_client.post(reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)

    def test_external_next_url_is_not_followed(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-next", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        dashboard = self.client.get(reverse("dashboard:home"))
        self.assertContains(dashboard, "Workflow actions")
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"),
            {"seed": "invalid", "next": "https://example.invalid/"},
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertEqual(response.url, reverse("dashboard:home"))

    def test_invalid_seed_is_reported(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-invalid-seed", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"),
            {"seed": "not-an-integer"},
            follow=True,
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Seed must be an integer.")

    def test_staff_can_run_scheduler(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-run", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"),
            {"seed": str(SEED_DEFAULT)},
            follow=True,
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Ran scheduler")

    def test_academic_administrator_group_can_run_actions(self):
        call_command("setup_roles", verbosity=0)
        user = User.objects.create_user(username="academic-admin", password="test-pass-123")
        user.groups.add(Group.objects.get(name="Academic Administrator"))
        self.client.force_login(user)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_run_scheduler"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Ran scheduler")

    def test_staff_can_validate_schedule(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-validate", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_validate_schedule"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "VAL-")

    def test_staff_can_validate_master_data(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-data-check", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_validate_data"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Data validation passed")

    def test_staff_can_export_data(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-export", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)
        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_export_data"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Exported")
        self.assertTrue(
            (Path(self.output_dir.name) / "data" / "exports" / "manifest.json").exists()
        )

    def test_staff_can_run_complete_anomaly_workflow(self):
        staff = User.objects.create_user(
            username="staff-anomalies", password="test-pass-123", is_staff=True
        )
        self.client.force_login(staff)

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_inject_anomalies"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "labelled anomalies")

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_detect_violations"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "violations on RUN-")

        response = self.client.post(
            reverse("dashboard:action_evaluate_anomalies"), follow=True
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "Evaluation: TP=")
